1: # GDCefGlue
2: 
3: A CEF (Chromium Embedded Framework) browser control for Godot 4.x using CefGlue.
4: 
5: [中文文档](README_CN.md)
6:

14:

20: 
21: ![WebGL Aquarium](img/WebGL水族馆.png)
22: 
23: ## Quick Start
24:

32: 
33: ## Requirements
34:

40:

50: </ItemGroup>

52: 
53: **CEF 120 (Official):**

55: <ItemGroup>
56:   <PackageReference Include="CefGlue.Common" Version="120.6099.0" />

58: </ItemGroup>

61: ### Option 2: Build from Source

67: git clone https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue.git

70: **CEF 120 (Official):**
71: ```bash

74:

82: │   ├── NormalProject/     # Normal C# project example
83: │   ├── img/

86: ```
87: 
88: ## Project Types
89: 
90: ### GDExtension Project (Experimental)
91: 
92: Located in `GDExtension/` directory. For Godot 4.6+.
93: 
94: > **Warning:** This project is currently unstable and not recommended for production use.
95: 
96: **Features:**
97: - Uses Godot's GDExtension system
98: - Compiled as native AOT library
99: - Better performance and smaller file size
100: - Requires manual CEF file copying during export
101: - Entry point: `gdcefglue_library_init`
102: 
103: **Structure:**
104: - `GDExtension/Extension/` - C# source code for GDExtension
105: - `GDExtension/Extension/Dll/` - Godot .NET bindings (from [godot-dotnet](https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet))
106: - `GDExtension/Project/` - Godot project for testing
107: 
108: **Build Instructions:**
109: 
110: 1. **Get Godot .NET Bindings:**
111:    ```bash
112:    git clone https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet.git
113:    cd godot-dotnet

116:    Copy the generated `Godot.Bindings.dll` and related files to `GDExtension/Extension/Dll/`.

118: 2. **Build GDExtension:**
119:    ```bash
120:    cd GDExtension/Extension

122:    ```

124: 3. **Deploy:**
125:    Copy all files from `Debug/net10.0/win-x64/publish/` to `GDExtension/Project/lib/`.
126: 
127: 4. **Run:**
128:    Open the project with Godot 4.6 and run.

131:

133: 
134: **Features:**
135: - Standard Godot .NET SDK project
136: - Uses `Godot.NET.Sdk`
137: - CEF files are automatically copied when using NuGet packages
138: - May need manual resource copying after export (except NuGet packages)
139: - Source code in `addons/GCefGlue/`

148:   <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue\CefGlue.csproj" />
149:   <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue.Common\CefGlue.Common.csproj" />
150:   <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue.Common.Shared\CefGlue.Common.Shared.csproj" />
151: </ItemGroup>
152: ```
153: 
154: ### CefGlue Sources
155: 
156: | Source | CEF Version | Status | NuGet | GitHub |
157: |--------|-------------|--------|-------|--------|
158: | youfch/CefGlue | 134 | Unofficial (Recommended) | `CefGlue.Common 134.6998.178` | [GitHub](https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue) |
159: | OutSystems/CefGlue | 120 | Official | `CefGlue.Common 120.6099.0` | [GitHub](https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue) |
160:

164: 
165: ```powershell

167: dotnet restore
168: 
169: # Build
170: dotnet build
171: ```
172:

174: 
175: ```powershell

187: 
188: After a successful build, the following files will be generated:
189: 
190: **Core Files:**

192: - `Xilium.CefGlue.dll` - CefGlue wrapper
193: - `Xilium.CefGlue.Common.dll` - Common functionality
194: 
195: **CEF Native Files:**
196: - `libcef.dll` - Chromium core library

199: - `locales\*.pak` - Language packs
200: 
201: **BrowserProcess Files:**
202: - `CefGlueBrowserProcess\` - Browser subprocess files

204: ## Export for Distribution
205: 
206: ### Using NuGet Package
207: 
208: When using NuGet packages, all necessary files are automatically copied during build. Just export your Godot project normally.
209: 
210: ### Using Source Code
211: 
212: When using source code dependencies, you need to manually copy files after export. See [CEF_EXPORT_GUIDE.md](CEF_EXPORT_GUIDE.md) for details.
213: 
214: ## CefGlueControl Properties
215: 
216: | Property | Type | Default | Description |
217: |----------|------|---------|-------------|
218: | `InitialUrl` | string | "about:blank" | The URL to load when the browser is created |
219: | `OpenPopupInCurrentBrowser` | bool | true | If true, popup windows navigate in the current browser |
220: | `GpuAcceleration` | bool | true | If true, enables GPU hardware acceleration |
221: | `FrameRate` | int | 60 | Browser frame rate, range 1-360 |
222: | `Transparent` | bool | false | If true, enables transparent background support |
223: 
224: ### Static Properties
225: 
226: | Property | Type | Description |
227: |----------|------|-------------|
228: | `UseGpuAcceleration` | bool | Global GPU acceleration setting, must be set before CEF initialization |
229: | `UseTransparent` | bool | Global transparent background setting, must be set before CEF initialization |
230: 
231: ### Read-only Properties
232: 
233: | Property | Type | Description |
234: |----------|------|-------------|
235: | `Address` | string | Current page URL |
236: | `IsBrowserInitialized` | bool | Whether the browser is initialized |
237: | `IsLoading` | bool | Whether the page is loading |
238: | `Title` | string | Current page title |
239: 
240: ## CefGlueControl Methods
241: 
242: | Method | Description |
243: |--------|-------------|
244: | `GoBack()` | Navigate back in history |
245: | `GoForward()` | Navigate forward in history |
246: | `NavigateToUrl(string url)` | Navigate to the specified URL |
247: | `Reload(bool ignoreCache = false)` | Reload the current page, optionally ignoring cache |
248: | `ExecuteJavaScript(string code, ...)` | Execute JavaScript code |
249: | `EvaluateJavaScript<T>(string code, ...)` | Execute JavaScript and return result |
250: | `ShowDeveloperTools()` | Open developer tools |
251: | `CloseDeveloperTools()` | Close developer tools |
252: 
253: ## GPU Configuration
254: 
255: ```csharp
256: // Enable GPU acceleration (default) - set in inspector
257: // GpuAcceleration = true;
258: 
259: // Disable GPU acceleration (software rendering) - set in inspector
260: // GpuAcceleration = false;
261: ```
262: 
263: **Note:** This property is exposed to the Godot inspector. Set it before the control is initialized.
264: 
265: ## Troubleshooting
266: 
267: ### GPU Process Crashed
268: 
269: 1. Ensure all CEF files are copied correctly
270: 2. Try disabling GPU acceleration in the inspector
271: 
272: ### Missing DLLs
273: 
274: 1. Run `dotnet restore` to restore NuGet packages
275: 2. Clean and rebuild the solution
276: 
277: ### Blank Page
278: 
279: 1. Check if `locales` directory exists
280: 2. Ensure `resources.pak` is present
281: 
282: ## Known Issues
283: 
284: 1. **Right-click Context Menu**: Not supported
285: 2. **Network Notification**: `WSALookupServiceBegin failed with: 10108` is a normal warning

[Project README: E:\Work\Hub\GDCefGlue\README.md]
# GDCefGlue

A CEF (Chromium Embedded Framework) browser control for Godot 4.x using CefGlue.

[中文文档](README_CN.md)

## Features

- GPU hardware acceleration support
- IME support for Chinese/Japanese/Korean input
- Popup handling
- Complete keyboard and mouse support
- Easy integration with Godot 4.x

## Performance Demo

### WebGL Aquarium

Supports WebGL rendering with 20,000 fish at stable 120fps:

![WebGL Aquarium](img/WebGL水族馆.png)

## Quick Start

Add a `CefGlueControl` node to your scene as a browser control:

```csharp
var browser = new CefGlueControl();
browser.InitialUrl = "https://godotengine.org";
AddChild(browser);
```

## Requirements

- **Godot Engine**: 4.6.0 or later (with .NET/Mono support)
- **.NET SDK**: 8.0 or later
- **Windows**: x64 architecture

## Dependency Options

### Option 1: NuGet Package (Recommended)

The easiest way to use GDCefGlue. All necessary files are automatically copied during build.

**CEF 134 (Recommended):**
```xml
<ItemGroup>
  <PackageReference Include="CefGlue.Common" Version="134.6998.178" />
  <PackageReference Include="chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64" Version="134.3.9" />
</ItemGroup>
```

**CEF 120 (Official):**
```xml
<ItemGroup>
  <PackageReference Include="CefGlue.Common" Version="120.6099.0" />
  <PackageReference Include="chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64" Version="120.2.3" />
</ItemGroup>
```

### Option 2: Build from Source

If you need the latest version or want to customize CefGlue:

**CEF 134 (Recommended):**
```bash
git clone https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue.git
```

**CEF 120 (Official):**
```bash
git clone https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue.git
```

Place the cloned repository in your project directory:
```
YourProject/
├── GDCefGlue/
│   ├── GDExtension/
│   │   ├── Extension/     # C# GDExtension source code
│   │   └── Project/       # Godot test project
│   ├── NormalProject/     # Normal C# project example
│   ├── img/
│   └── README*.md
└── CefGlue/               # Cloned repository
```

## Project Types

### GDExtension Project (Experimental)

Located in `GDExtension/` directory. For Godot 4.6+.

> **Warning:** This project is currently unstable and not recommended for production use.

**Features:**
- Uses Godot's GDExtension system
- Compiled as native AOT library
- Better performance and smaller file size
- Requires manual CEF file copying during export
- Entry point: `gdcefglue_library_init`

**Structure:**
- `GDExtension/Extension/` - C# source code for GDExtension
- `GDExtension/Extension/Dll/` - Godot .NET bindings (from [godot-dotnet](https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet))
- `GDExtension/Project/` - Godot project for testing

**Build Instructions:**

1. **Get Godot .NET Bindings:**
   ```bash
   git clone https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet.git
   cd godot-dotnet
   dotnet build -p:GenerateGodotBindings=true
   ```
   Copy the generated `Godot.Bindings.dll` and related files to `GDExtension/Extension/Dll/`.

2. **Build GDExtension:**
   ```bash
   cd GDExtension/Extension
   dotnet publish -c Debug -r win-x64 --self-contained true
   ```

3. **Deploy:**
   Copy all files from `Debug/net10.0/win-x64/publish/` to `GDExtension/Project/lib/`.

4. **Run:**
   Open the project with Godot 4.6 and run.

### Normal C# Project

Located in `NormalProject/` directory. Traditional Godot C# project approach.

**Features:**
- Standard Godot .NET SDK project
- Uses `Godot.NET.Sdk`
- CEF files are automatically copied when using NuGet packages
- May need manual resource copying after export (except NuGet packages)
- Source code in `addons/GCefGlue/`

**When to use:**
- If you prefer traditional Godot C# development
- If GDExtension doesn't meet your needs

Update your `.csproj` to reference the projects:
```xml
<ItemGroup>
  <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue\CefGlue.csproj" />
  <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue.Common\CefGlue.Common.csproj" />
  <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue.Common.Shared\CefGlue.Common.Shared.csproj" />
</ItemGroup>
```

### CefGlue Sources

| Source | CEF Version | Status | NuGet | GitHub |
|--------|-------------|--------|-------|--------|
| youfch/CefGlue | 134 | Unofficial (Recommended) | `CefGlue.Common 134.6998.178` | [GitHub](https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue) |
| OutSystems/CefGlue | 120 | Official | `CefGlue.Common 120.6099.0` | [GitHub](https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue) |

## Build Instructions

### Using NuGet Package

```powershell
# Restore packages
dotnet restore

# Build
dotnet build
```

### Using Source Code

```powershell
# Clone CefGlue (choose one)
git clone https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue.git   # CEF 134
# or
git clone https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue.git  # CEF 120

# Build
dotnet restore
dotnet build
```

## Build Output

After a successful build, the following files will be generated:

**Core Files:**
- `GDCefGlue.dll` - Main plugin assembly
- `Xilium.CefGlue.dll` - CefGlue wrapper
- `Xilium.CefGlue.Common.dll` - Common functionality

**CEF Native Files:**
- `libcef.dll` - Chromium core library
- `chrome_*.pak` - UI resources
- `resources.pak` - Application resources
- `locales\*.pak` - Language packs

**BrowserProcess Files:**
- `CefGlueBrowserProcess\` - Browser subprocess files

## Export for Distribution

### Using NuGet Package

When using NuGet packages, all necessary files are automatically copied during build. Just export your Godot project normally.

### Using Source Code

When using source code dependencies, you need to manually copy files after export. See [CEF_EXPORT_GUIDE.md](CEF_EXPORT_GUIDE.md) for details.

## CefGlueControl Properties

| Property | Type | Default | Description |
|----------|------|---------|-------------|
| `InitialUrl` | string | "about:blank" | The URL to load when the browser is created |
| `OpenPopupInCurrentBrowser` | bool | true | If true, popup windows navigate in the current browser |
| `GpuAcceleration` | bool | true | If true, enables GPU hardware acceleration |
| `FrameRate` | int | 60 | Browser frame rate, range 1-360 |
| `Transparent` | bool | false | If true, enables transparent background support |

### Static Properties

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `UseGpuAcceleration` | bool | Global GPU acceleration setting, must be set before CEF initialization |
| `UseTransparent` | bool | Global transparent background setting, must be set before CEF initialization |

### Read-only Properties

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `Address` | string | Current page URL |
| `IsBrowserInitialized` | bool | Whether the browser is initialized |
| `IsLoading` | bool | Whether the page is loading |
| `Title` | string | Current page title |

## CefGlueControl Methods

| Method | Description |
|--------|-------------|
| `GoBack()` | Navigate back in history |
| `GoForward()` | Navigate forward in history |
| `NavigateToUrl(string url)` | Navigate to the specified URL |
| `Reload(bool ignoreCache = false)` | Reload the current page, optionally ignoring cache |
| `ExecuteJavaScript(string code, ...)` | Execute JavaScript code |
| `EvaluateJavaScript<T>(string code, ...)` | Execute JavaScript and return result |
| `ShowDeveloperTools()` | Open developer tools |
| `CloseDeveloperTools()` | Close developer tools |

## GPU Configuration

```csharp
// Enable GPU acceleration (default) - set in inspector
// GpuAcceleration = true;

// Disable GPU acceleration (software rendering) - set in inspector
// GpuAcceleration = false;
```

**Note:** This property is exposed to the Godot inspector. Set it before the control is initialized.

## Troubleshooting

### GPU Process Crashed

1. Ensure all CEF files are copied correctly
2. Try disabling GPU acceleration in the inspector

### Missing DLLs

1. Run `dotnet restore` to restore NuGet packages
2. Clean and rebuild the solution

### Blank Page

1. Check if `locales` directory exists
2. Ensure `resources.pak` is present

## Known Issues

1. **Right-click Context Menu**: Not supported
2. **Network Notification**: `WSALookupServiceBegin failed with: 10108` is a normal warning

1: # GDCefGlue
2: 
3: 基于 CefGlue 的 Godot 4.x CEF 浏览器控件。

7: ## 功能特性

16: 
17: ### WebGL 水族馆
18: 
19: 支持 WebGL 渲染，20000 条鱼稳定 120fps：
20: 
21: !\[WebGL水族馆]\(img/WebGL水族馆.png null)
22: 
23: ## 环境要求
24: 
25: - **Godot Engine**: 4.6.0 或更高版本（需要 .NET/Mono 支持）
26: - **.NET SDK**: 8.0 或更高版本
27: - **Windows**: x64 架构
28: 
29: ## 依赖方式
30: 
31: ### 方式一：NuGet 包（推荐）
32: 
33: 最简单的使用方式。构建时自动复制所有必要文件。
34: 
35: **CEF 134（推荐）：**
36: 
37: ```xml
38: <ItemGroup>
39:   <PackageReference Include="CefGlue.Common" Version="134.6998.178" />
40:   <PackageReference Include="chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64" Version="134.3.9" />
41: </ItemGroup>
42: ```
43: 
44: **CEF 120（官方）：**
45: 
46: ```xml
47: <ItemGroup>
48:   <PackageReference Include="CefGlue.Common" Version="120.6099.0" />
49:   <PackageReference Include="chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64" Version="120.2.3" />
50: </ItemGroup>
51: ```
52: 
53: ### 方式二：从源码构建
54: 
55: 如果需要最新版本或自定义 CefGlue：
56: 
57: **CEF 134（推荐）：**
58: 
59: ```bash
60: git clone https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue.git
61: ```
62: 
63: **CEF 120（官方）：**
64: 
65: ```bash
66: git clone https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue.git
67: ```
68: 
69: 将克隆的仓库放置到项目目录：
70: 
71: ```
72: 你的项目/
73: ├── GDCefGlue/
74: │   ├── GDExtension/
75: │   │   ├── Extension/     # C# GDExtension 源代码
76: │   │   └── Project/       # Godot 测试项目
77: │   ├── NormalProject/     # 普通 C# 项目示例
78: │   ├── img/
79: │   └── README*.md
80: └── CefGlue/               # 克隆的仓库
81: ```
82: 
83: ## 项目类型
84: 
85: ### GDExtension 项目
86: 
87: 位于 `GDExtension/` 目录。适用于 Godot 4.6+。
88: 
89: **特点：**
90: 
91: - 使用 Godot 的 GDExtension 系统
92: - 编译为原生 AOT 库
93: - 性能更好，文件体积更小
94: - 导出时需要手动复制 CEF 文件
95: - 入口点：`gdcefglue_library_init`
96: 
97: **结构：**
98: 
99: - `GDExtension/Extension/` - GDExtension 的 C# 源代码
100: - `GDExtension/Extension/Dll/` - Godot .NET 绑定（来自 [godot-dotnet](https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet)）
101: - `GDExtension/Project/` - 用于测试的 Godot 项目
102: 
103: **构建说明：**
104: 
105: 1. **获取 Godot .NET 绑定：**
106: 
107:    不同 Godot 版本需要对应版本的 godot-dotnet：
108:    | Godot 版本 | godot-dotnet 分支 |
109:    | -------- | --------------- |
110:    | 4.6.x    | master 或对应标签    |
111:    | 4.5.x    | 检查对应 release 标签 |
112:    | 4.4.x    | 检查对应 release 标签 |
113:    > **注意：** godot-dotnet 没有发布 release 包，需要查看历史提交，下载对应 Godot 版本的源码后手动编译。
114:    ```bash
115:    git clone https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet.git
116:    cd godot-dotnet
117:    # 切换到对应 Godot 版本的分支/标签（如需要）
118:    # git checkout <godot-version-tag>
119:    dotnet build -p:GenerateGodotBindings=true
120:    ```
121:    将生成的 `Godot.Bindings.dll` 及相关文件复制到 `GDExtension/Extension/Dll/`。
122: 2. **CEF 依赖（跨平台）：**
123:    - **Windows：** 通过 NuGet 包自动获取 `chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64`
124:    - **Linux：** 需要添加 [cef.redist.linux](https://github.com/OutSystems/cef.redist.linux) 依赖
125:    - **macOS：** 需要添加 [cef.redist.osx](https://github.com/OutSystems/cef.redist.osx) 依赖
126:    查看 [CefGlue 仓库](https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue) 了解如何添加跨平台依赖。
127: 3. **构建 GDExtension：**
128: 
129:    进入 `GDExtension/Extension` 目录执行：
130: 
131:    **Windows x64：**
132:    ```bash
133:    # Debug 版本
134:    dotnet publish -c Debug -r win-x64 --self-contained true
135: 
136:    # Release 版本
137:    dotnet publish -c Release -r win-x64 --self-contained true
138:    ```
139:    **Linux x64：**
140:    ```bash
141:    dotnet publish -c Release -r linux-x64 --self-contained true
142:    ```
143:    **macOS x64/ARM64：**
144:    ```bash
145:    # Intel Mac
146:    dotnet publish -c Release -r osx-x64 --self-contained true
147: 
148:    # Apple Silicon Mac
149:    dotnet publish -c Release -r osx-arm64 --self-contained true
150:    ```
151: 4. **部署：**
152: 
153:    编译输出位于 `bin\Release(Debug)\net9.0\win-x64\publish\`（Windows）或对应平台目录。
154: 
155:    将 publish 目录中的所有文件复制到 `GDExtension/Game/lib/` 目录。
156: 5. **运行：**
157:    使用 Godot 4.6 打开 `GDExtension/Game/` 项目并运行。
158: 
159: **不同 CEF 版本支持：**
160: 
161: 如需使用不同版本的 CEF，有两种方式：
162: 
163: 1. **NuGet 包方式（推荐）：** 修改 `.csproj` 中的 NuGet 包版本
164:    ```xml
165:    <PackageReference Include="CefGlue.Common" Version="xxx.xxxx.x" />
166:    <PackageReference Include="chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64" Version="xxx.x.x" />
167:    ```
168: 2. **手动编译：** 从 [CefGlue 仓库](https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue) 下载对应 CEF 版本的源码，手动编译 NuGet 包后引用。
169: 
170: ### 普通 C# 项目
171: 
172: 位于 `NormalProject/` 目录。传统的 Godot C# 项目方式。
173: 
174: **特点：**
175: 
176: - 标准 Godot .NET SDK 项目
177: - 使用 `Godot.NET.Sdk`
178: - 使用 NuGet 包时 CEF 文件自动复制
179: - 导出后可能需要手动复制资源（NuGet 包除外）
180: - 源代码位于 `addons/GCefGlue/`
181: 
182: **适用场景：**
183: 
184: - 如果你偏好传统的 Godot C# 开发方式
185: - 如果 GDExtension 不能满足你的需求
186: 
187: 更新 `.csproj` 引用项目：
188: 
189: ```xml
190: <ItemGroup>
191:   <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue\CefGlue.csproj" />
192:   <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue.Common\CefGlue.Common.csproj" />
193:   <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue.Common.Shared\CefGlue.Common.Shared.csproj" />
194: </ItemGroup>
195: ```
196: 
197: ### CefGlue 来源
198: 
199: | 来源                 | CEF 版本 | 状态      | NuGet                         | GitHub                                          |
200: | ------------------ | ------ | ------- | ----------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------- |
201: | youfch/CefGlue     | 134    | 非官方（推荐） | `CefGlue.Common 134.6998.178` | [GitHub](https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue)     |
202: | OutSystems/CefGlue | 120    | 官方      | `CefGlue.Common 120.6099.0`   | [GitHub](https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue) |
203: 
204: ## 构建说明
205: 
206: ### 使用 NuGet 包
207: 
208: ```powershell
209: # 还原包
210: dotnet restore
211: 
212: # 构建
213: dotnet build
214: ```
215: 
216: ### 使用源码
217: 
218: ```powershell
219: # 克隆 CefGlue（选择一个）
220: git clone https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue.git   # CEF 134
221: # 或
222: git clone https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue.git  # CEF 120
223: 
224: # 构建
225: dotnet restore
226: dotnet build
227: ```
228: 
229: ## 构建输出
230: 
231: 成功构建后，将生成以下文件：
232: 
233: **核心文件：**
234: 
235: - `GDCefGlue.dll` - 主插件程序集
236: - `Xilium.CefGlue.dll` - CefGlue 包装器
237: - `Xilium.CefGlue.Common.dll` - 通用功能
238: 
239: **CEF 原生文件：**
240: 
241: - `libcef.dll` - Chromium 核心库
242: - `chrome_*.pak` - UI 资源
243: - `resources.pak` - 应用程序资源
244: - `locales\*.pak` - 语言包
245: 
246: **BrowserProcess 文件：**
247: 
248: - `CefGlueBrowserProcess\` - 浏览器子进程文件
249: 
250: ## 导出分发
251: 
252: ### 使用 NuGet 包
253: 
254: 使用 NuGet 包时，所有必要文件在构建时自动复制。正常导出 Godot 项目即可。
255: 
256: ### 使用源码
257: 
258: 使用源码依赖时，导出后需要手动复制文件。详见 [CEF\_EXPORT\_GUIDE.md](CEF_EXPORT_GUIDE.md)。
259: 
260: ## CefGlueControl 属性
261: 
262: | 属性                          | 类型     | 默认值           | 描述                   |
263: | --------------------------- | ------ | ------------- | -------------------- |
264: | `InitialUrl`                | string | "about:blank" | 浏览器创建时加载的 URL        |
265: | `OpenPopupInCurrentBrowser` | bool   | true          | 如果为 true，弹窗在当前浏览器中导航 |
266: | `GpuAcceleration`           | bool   | true          | 如果为 true，启用 GPU 硬件加速 |
267: | `FrameRate`                 | int    | 60            | 浏览器帧率，范围 1-360       |
268: | `Transparent`               | bool   | false         | 如果为 true，启用透明背景支持    |
269: 
270: ### 静态属性
271: 
272: | 属性                | 类型   | 描述                          |
273: | ----------------- | ---- | --------------------------- |
274: | `UseGpuAcceleration` | bool | 全局 GPU 加速设置，需在 CEF 初始化前设置    |
275: | `UseTransparent`     | bool | 全局透明背景设置，需在 CEF 初始化前设置       |
276: 
277: ### 只读属性
278: 
279: | 属性                    | 类型     | 描述             |
280: | --------------------- | ------ | -------------- |
281: | `Address`             | string | 当前页面 URL       |
282: | `IsBrowserInitialized` | bool   | 浏览器是否已初始化      |
283: | `IsLoading`           | bool   | 页面是否正在加载       |
284: | `Title`               | string | 当前页面标题         |
285: 
286: ## CefGlueControl 方法
287: 
288: | 方法                                      | 描述                  |
289: | --------------------------------------- | ------------------- |
290: | `GoBack()`                              | 后退                  |
291: | `GoForward()`                           | 前进                  |
292: | `NavigateToUrl(string url)`             | 导航到指定 URL           |
293: | `Reload(bool ignoreCache = false)`      | 刷新当前页面，可选忽略缓存       |
294: | `ExecuteJavaScript(string code, ...)`   | 执行 JavaScript 代码    |
295: | `EvaluateJavaScript<T>(string code, ...)` | 执行 JavaScript 并返回结果 |
296: | `ShowDeveloperTools()`                  | 打开开发者工具             |
297: | `CloseDeveloperTools()`                 | 关闭开发者工具             |
298: 
299: ## GPU 配置
300: 
301: ```csharp
302: // 启用 GPU 加速（默认）- 在检查器中设置
303: // GpuAcceleration = true;
304: 
305: // 禁用 GPU 加速（软件渲染）- 在检查器中设置
306: // GpuAcceleration = false;
307: ```
308: 
309: **注意：** 此属性已暴露给 Godot 检查器，在控件初始化前设置。
310: 
311: ## 故障排除
312: 
313: ### GPU 进程崩溃
314: 
315: 1. 确保所有 CEF 文件正确复制
316: 2. 尝试在检查器中禁用 GPU 加速
317: 
318: ### 缺少 DLL
319: 
320: 1. 运行 `dotnet restore` 还原 NuGet 包
321: 2. 清理并重新构建解决方案
322: 
323: ### 空白页面
324: 
325: 1. 检查 `locales` 目录是否存在
326: 2. 确保 `resources.pak` 存在
327: 
328: ## 已知问题
329: 
330: 1. **右键菜单**：暂不支持
331: 2. **网络通知**：`WSALookupServiceBegin failed with: 10108` 是正常警告
332:

337: - [Godot Engine](https://godotengine.org) - MIT
338: - [godot-dotnet](https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet) - MIT

## GDCefGlue 项目概览

### 一句话定位
**GDCefGlue** 是一个将 Chromium 浏览器嵌入 **Godot 4.x 游戏引擎**的 C# 插件，基于 CefGlue（CEF 的 .NET 绑定）。

### 核心能力
- 在 Godot 场景中嵌入完整的 Chromium 浏览器 (`CefGlueControl` 节点)
- GPU 硬件加速，WebGL 渲染（2万条鱼 120fps）
- 中文/日文/韩文输入法（IME）支持
- 弹窗处理、完整键鼠交互
- 支持透明背景、JS 执行、开发者工具

### 技术栈
| 层级 | 技术 |
|------|------|
| 引擎 | Godot 4.6+ (.NET/Mono) |
| 运行时 | .NET 8.0/9.0 |
| 浏览器内核 | Chromium (CEF 120 或 134) |
| C# 绑定 | CefGlue (Xilium) |
| 平台 | Windows x64（Linux/macOS 实验性支持） |

### 项目结构

```
GDCefGlue/
├── NormalProject/          ← 传统 Godot C# 项目（推荐/稳定）
│   ├── addons/GCefGlue/    # 10 个 C# 插件源文件
│   │   ├── CefGlueControl.cs        # 浏览器控件主体
│   │   ├── GodotCefClient.cs        # CEF 客户端
│   │   ├── GodotRenderHandler.cs    # 渲染处理器
│   │   ├── GodotLoadHandler.cs      # 加载处理器
│   │   ├── GodotDisplayHandler.cs   # 显示处理器
│   │   ├── GodotLifeSpanHandler.cs  # 生命周期处理器
│   │   ├── GodotBrowserProcessHandler.cs
│   │   ├── CefInitializer.cs        # CEF 初始化
│   │   └── ...
│   └── MainUi.cs
├── GDExtension/            ← 实验性 GDExtension 项目
│   ├── Extension/          # AOT 编译的 GDExtension 源码
│   └── Game/               # Godot 测试项目（GDScript）
├── src/
│   └── GDCefGlue.Extension/  # 共享扩展代码（bin/obj 有文件，暂无 .cs）
├── demos/                  # 示例项目
├── conversations/          # 对话记录
├── img/                    # 截图
└── Nuget/                  # NuGet 相关
```

### 两种集成方式

| 特性 | NormalProject (推荐) | GDExtension (实验性) |
|------|---------------------|---------------------|
| 项目类型 | 标准 Godot .NET SDK | 原生 AOT 库 |
| 稳定性 | 生产可用 | 不稳定 |
| 性能 | 正常 | 更好，体积更小 |
| 部署 | NuGet 自动复制 | 需手动复制 CEF 文件 |
| 入口 | 标准 C# 类 | `gdcefglue_library_init` |

### CefGlueControl 主要 API

```csharp
// 属性
InitialUrl, GpuAcceleration, FrameRate, Transparent
// 导航
GoBack(), GoForward(), NavigateToUrl(url), Reload()
// JS 交互
ExecuteJavaScript(code), EvaluateJavaScript<T>(code)
// 调试
ShowDeveloperTools(), CloseDeveloperTools()
```

### 已知局限
- 不支持右键菜单
- GDExtension 模式不稳定
- 目前主要支持 Windows x64

## Current Structure Summary

```
GDCefGlue/
├── NormalProject/                  ← Build mode 1: Standard Godot .NET SDK
│   ├── addons/GCefGlue/            #  9 handler + 1 control .cs (+ .uid files)
│   ├── MainUi.cs                   #  Godot Control entry point

│   ├── Extension/                  #  C# source & build
│   │   ├── *.cs                    #  IDENTICAL 9 files as NormalProject GLUE...
│   │   ├── Main.cs                 #  + GDExtension-specific entry point
│   │   ├── GDCefGlueExtension.csproj # SDK: Microsoft.NET.Sdk, TF: net10.0, PublishAot
│   │   ├── Dll/                    #  Manual godot-dotnet bindings
│   │   └── .gitignore
│   └── Game/                       #  Godot test project
│       ├── project.godot           #  template=gdextension
│       ├── Main.gd / Main.tscn     #  GDScript scene
│       ├── icon.svg
│       └── lib/                    #  Deploy target for built .dll
│           └── gdcefglue.gdextension  # GDExtension manifest
│
├── src/                            ← STALE — only bin/obj/, no .cs files
│   └── GDCefGlue.Extension/

│       └── obj/
│

```

## Critical Structural Problems

1. **9 C# files are 100% duplicated** between `NormalProject/addons/GCefGlue/` and `GDExtension/Extension/`. Any fix must be applied twice.

2. **`src/` is dead weight** — it appears to be where shared code was *intended* to go but it only contains build artifacts (bin/obj/), no source. The name `GDCefGlue.Extension` suggests it was meant for the GDExtension variant.

3. **No solution-level organization** — the single `.sln` only covers NormalProject. GDExtension builds independently via `dotnet publish`.

4. **`demos/` is empty** — a placeholder that should either be populated or removed.

5. **Build outputs leak into source tree** — top-level `bin/` and `obj/` are from building the Extension project.

6. **GDExtension/Game/ is a Godot project** but GDExtension/Extension/ is a C# project — the directory mixes engine project and C# source concerns.

7. **CI manually copies .uid files** from NormalProject for the release artifact, indicating these UID bindings are release-critical.

8. **Two different .NET TFMs** — NormalProject: net8.0, GDExtension: net10.0. They also have different CEF version configurations (Win 145 vs Unix 134).

</answer>

The `Main.cs` (GDExtension entry) and `MainUi.cs` (NormalProject entry) remain unique to each mode. ## 现状分析总结

当前目录的核心问题：

```
当前                                      问题
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
src/GDCefGlue.Extension/       ← 死目录，无源码，不在 git 里
demos/GDCefGlueDemo/           ← 空壳，只有构建缓存
bin/ obj/ (顶层)               ← 构建产物污染源码树
GDExtension/Extension/*.cs     ← 与 NormalProject 9 个文件同名但已分化
NormalProject/addons/GCefGlue/ ← 同上，命名空间和实现都不同
```

关键发现：**两份代码已经独立演化**，不是简单复制 — 命名空间不同 (`GDCefGlue` vs `GDCefGlueExtension`)、Godot API 访问方式不同 (动态 vs AOT)、内部字段不同。强行合并反而会增加 `#if` 复杂度。

基于此，设计原则应是：**承认差异，清晰分层，而非强行统一**。

---

以下给出两个方案，差异在于对已分化代码的态度：

---

### 方案 A：保守整理（推荐，改动最小）

承认两份代码独立演化的现实，只做目录清理和命名规范化。

```
GDCefGlue/
├── src/                          ← 复活！承载共享代码
│   └── GDCefGlue.Shared/         ← 抽取真正可共享的核心
│       ├── GDCefGlue.Shared.csproj
│       ├── GodotCefClient.cs     ← 两份几乎一样（28行 vs 24行）
│       └── ...（逐文件评估合并）
│
├── plugin/                       ← 原 NormalProject → 语义化
│   ├── addons/GCefGlue/          ← 不变
│   ├── GDCefGlue.csproj
│   ├── GDCefGlue.sln
│   └── project.godot
│
├── extension/                    ← 原 GDExtension/Extension → 扁平化
│   ├── GDCefGlueExtension.csproj
│   ├── Dll/                      ← godot-dotnet bindings
│   ├── *.cs                      ← 保持独立源码
│   └── Main.cs
│
├── test/                         ← 测试项目
│   └── GDExtensionGame/          ← 原 GDExtension/Game
│       ├── project.godot
│       ├── Main.gd
│       └── lib/                  ← 部署目标
│
├── demos/                        ← 填入实际 demo 或删除
├── .github/                      ← CI 不变
├── img/ / Nuget/ / README*.md    ← 不变
└── LICENSE
```

**改动清单：**
- `NormalProject/` → `plugin/`（改名 + 更新 CI 路径）
- `GDExtension/Extension/` → `extension/`（扁平一层）
- `GDExtension/Game/` → `test/GDExtensionGame/`
- 复活 `src/GDCefGlue.Shared/`，只放入两版本完全一致或差异极小的文件
- 删除 `demos/GDCefGlueDemo/` 空壳
- 删除顶层 `bin/` `obj/`（确保 `.gitignore` 生效）
- 更新 `.github/workflows/release.yml` 中的路径引用

**优点：** 改动小、风险低、不破坏已分化代码  
**缺点：** 代码重复问题只解决了一小部分



### 方案 B：激进统一（长期收益高，但工程量大）

将两份代码合并为单一源码，通过编译符号 (`GDEXTENSION`) 控制分支逻辑。

```
GDCefGlue/
├── src/
│   └── GDCefGlue.Core/           ← 唯一的源码
│       ├── GDCefGlue.Core.csproj   ← netstandard2.1，通过编译符号控制
│       ├── CefGlueControl.cs
│       ├── GodotCefClient.cs
│       ├── GodotCefApp.cs
│       ├── CefInitializer.cs
│       ├── GodotRenderHandler.cs
│       ├── ...
│       └── Entry/
│           ├── MainUi.cs         ← NormalProject 入口
│           └── Main.cs           ← GDExtension 入口
│
├── build/                        ← 构建项目（薄壳）
│   ├── GodotPlugin/              ← 引用 Core，用 Godot.NET.Sdk 打包
│   │   ├── GDCefGlue.GodotPlugin.csproj
│   │   └── addons/GCefGlue/     ← 仅 .uid 文件和 Godot 资源
│   └── GDExtension/              ← 引用 Core，AOT 发布
│       ├── GDCefGlue.GDExtension.csproj
│       ├── Dll/
│       └── gdcefglue.gdextension
│
├── test/
│   └── GDExtensionGame/          ← Godot 测试项目
│
├── demos/ / img/ / Nuget/ / .github/ / README*.md
└── LICENSE
```

Core 中使用条件编译：
```csharp
#if GDEXTENSION
namespace GDCefGlueExtension;
using Godot.Bridge;
#else
namespace GDCefGlue;
#endif
```

**优点：** 彻底消除重复，一次修改两处生效  
**缺点：** `#if` 多了会降低可读性；合并过程需要人工比对每个文件；GDExtension 比 NormalProject 多了 `_packedBuffer` 等内部字段，合并后 NormalProject 会带有无用字段；AOT 和动态加载的 Godot API 差异较大，不是所有代码都能用 `#if` 干净分开

---

The user is giving me important constraints:

1. CEF is special - projects must directly reference CEF (not through an intermediate shared library). This is because CEF native resources (libcef.dll, .pak files, etc.) need to be in the build output directory. If CEF is referenced indirectly through a shared library, the native files won't be copied correctly.

2. Normal Godot project uses GodotSharp (.NET SDK), GDExtension project uses raw DLLs (godot-dotnet bindings). These are completely incompatible - they can't share code because they use different Godot APIs.

3. Cannot have an intermediate library reference CEF - the final build output (plugin project for NormalProject, extension project for GDExtension) must be the one that directly references CEF NuGet packages, otherwise the native CEF resources won't end up in the build directory.

This fundamentally rules out Scheme B (shared code library). If you create a shared `GDCefGlue.Core` that references CEF, then the plugin project references Core, CEF native files won't be correctly copied to the plugin's build output. The NuGet package's MSBuild targets for copying native files only trigger for the project that directly references the package.

So the approach must be:
- The two build-mode projects (plugin and extension) must each directly reference CEF NuGet packages
- They can share C# source files via `<Compile Include>` with paths pointing to a shared directory, but CANNOT create a shared library project

This is actually good news - it simplifies the decision to Scheme A (keep separation, organize better).

Let me propose a revised Scheme A that:
1. Keeps both projects as independent projects that directly reference CEF
2. Shares source files via MSBuild `<Compile Include>` (or linked files) rather than via project reference
3. Organizes directories cleanly

- Move shared source files to `src/shared/` or similar
- Each csproj includes those shared files via `<Compile Include="../../src/shared/**/*.cs" />`

你的约束非常关键，让我逐个分析对方案的影响：

---

## 约束分析

| 约束 | 影响 |
|------|------|
| CEF 必须被构建项目**直接引用** | ❌ 不能有中间共享库 — NuGet 的 CEF 原生文件复制 targets 只对直接引用的项目生效 |
| GodotSharp vs 原生 DLL，完全不兼容 | ❌ 无法用 `#if` 合并 — 两套 Godot API 毫无交集 |
| 两份代码已分化 | ❌ 强行合并只会产生大量条件编译垃圾 |

**结论：方案 B（共享库统一）被完全否决。** 不仅工程量大，而且技术上根本不可行 — CEF 资源不会通过间接引用复制到输出目录。

---

## 修正方案：纯目录整理

既然代码必须独立，那就只做目录层面的规范化。核心思路：

```
GDCefGlue/                          ← 仓库根
│
├── plugin/                         ← 【改名】原 NormalProject/
│   ├── GDCefGlue.sln               ← 解决方案
│   ├── GDCefGlue.csproj            ← Godot.NET.Sdk，直接引用 CEF
│   ├── project.godot               ← Godot 项目配置
│   ├── MainUi.cs / MainUI.tscn     ← 入口
│   ├── addons/GCefGlue/            ← 插件源码（.cs + .uid）
│   ├── .editorconfig
│   ├── icon.svg
│   └── CEF_EXPORT_GUIDE.md         ← 考虑移到 docs/
│
├── extension/                      ← 【扁平化】原 GDExtension/Extension/
│   ├── GDCefGlueExtension.csproj   ← Microsoft.NET.Sdk，直接引用 CEF
│   ├── Dll/                        ← Godot.Bindings.dll（手动依赖）
│   ├── *.cs                        ← 9个glue文件 + Main.cs
│   └── .gitignore
│
├── test/                           ← 【归拢】测试项目
│   └── GDExtensionGame/            ← 原 GDExtension/Game/
│       ├── project.godot
│       ├── Main.gd / Main.tscn
│       └── lib/gdcefglue.gdextension
│
├── .github/workflows/release.yml   ← CI（需更新路径引用）
├── img/                            ← 截图
├── Nuget/                          ← 本地 NuGet 包（LFS）
├── README.md / README_CN.md       ← 不动（GitHub 需要根目录）
├── LICENSE
└── .gitignore
```

### 待清理
- `src/GDCefGlue.Extension/` → **删除**（不在 git 里，纯垃圾）
- `demos/GDCefGlueDemo/` → **删除**（空壳）
- 顶层 `bin/` `obj/` → **删除**（构建泄漏）
- `conversations/` → **删除**（对话记录不应提交）

### 改动矩阵

| 当前路径 | 新路径 | 操作 |
|----------|--------|------|
| `NormalProject/*` | `plugin/*` | `git mv` |
| `GDExtension/Extension/*` | `extension/*` | `git mv` |
| `GDExtension/Game/*` | `test/GDExtensionGame/*` | `git mv` |
| `GDExtension/` 空目录 | — | 删除 |
| `.github/workflows/release.yml` | 不变，更新路径 | 编辑 |
| `NormalProject/CEF_EXPORT_GUIDE.md` | `docs/CEF_EXPORT_GUIDE.md` 或放 `plugin/` 下 | 可选 |
| `src/` `demos/` `bin/` `obj/` | — | 删除 |

### 命名对比

| 原名称 | 新名称 | 理由 |
|--------|--------|------|
| `NormalProject` | `plugin` | 更语义化；"Normal"相对于什么才是正常？ |
| `GDExtension/Extension` | `extension` | 扁平一层，消除 `GDExtension/Extension/` 的尴尬嵌套 |
| `GDExtension/Game` | `test/GDExtensionGame` | 和 plugin 的测试职责并列 |

---

## 关于代码复用的务实态度

两套代码虽然文件名相同，但**本质是两套实现**，不应强行合并。唯一例外是 `GodotCefClient.cs`（28 行 vs 24 行，近乎一致）。如果一定要消除这唯一的重复，可以这样做但**不建议**：

```xml
<!-- extension/GDCefGlueExtension.csproj -->
<Compile Include="../plugin/addons/GCefGlue/GodotCefClient.cs" Link="GodotCefClient.cs">
  <!-- 如果 namespace 不同需要额外处理 -->
</Compile>
```

但这引入跨目录依赖，对 24 行代码来说得不偿失。

结合你的项目约束（CEF 直接引用、两套不兼容的 Godot API），我的建议是：

### 不需要 `src/`，当前结构已经合理

在这个项目中，`plugin/` 和 `extension/` **本身就是** `src/` 的等价物 — 它们是两个独立的构建产物入口，各自直接引用 CEF。加一层 `src/` 只会多此一举：

```
不推荐                          推荐（当前结构）
─────────────────────────       ────────────────────────
src/                            plugin/          ← 源码即项目
├── Plugin/                     ├── addons/GCefGlue/
└── Extension/                  ├── GDCefGlue.csproj
                                └── project.godot
                                extension/       ← 源码即项目
                                ├── *.cs
                                └── GDCefGlueExtension.csproj
```

### 关于 demos：不要放 `src/`

`src/` 放的是**被编译分发的东西**（库、插件本体），`demos/` 放的是**消费这些库的示例项目**。两者的生命周期完全不同 — 源码要进 NuGet/Release，demo 只是教学用途。标准社区实践：

| 目录 | 职责 | 是否发布 |
|------|------|---------|
| `plugin/` / `extension/` | 插件源码 | ✅ 进 Release zip |
| `demos/` 或 `examples/` | 使用示例 | ❌ 纯参考 |
| `test/` | 自动化测试 | ❌ |

参考 .NET 生态里类似项目的布局（如 Godot 官方插件、Avalonia 控件库等），`samples/` 或 `demos/` 一律是顶级目录，从不塞进 `src/`。

### 最终建议

当前结构不动，只需决定 `demos/` 要不要补内容或直接删掉（现在是空壳）：

```
GDCefGlue/
├── plugin/                    ← 构建产物入口1，源码在此
├── extension/                 ← 构建产物入口2，源码在此
├── test/GDExtensionGame/      ← GDExtension 的 Godot 测试
├── demos/                     ← 可选：示例项目（如果后续有）
├── .github/ / img/ / Nuget/
├── README.md
└── LICENSE
```

如果你确实想加 `src/`，唯一的合理用途是放**构建辅助**（如共享的 MSBuild `.targets` 文件、代码生成脚本），不放源码也不放 demo。

<content>
1: # GDCefGlue
2: 
3: A CEF (Chromium Embedded Framework) browser control for Godot 4.x using CefGlue.
4: 
5: [中文文档](README_CN.md)
6: 
7: ## Features
8: 
9: - GPU hardware acceleration support
10: - IME support for Chinese/Japanese/Korean input
11: - Popup handling
12: - Complete keyboard and mouse support
13: - Easy integration with Godot 4.x
14: 
15: ## Performance Demo
16: 
17: ### WebGL Aquarium
18: 
19: Supports WebGL rendering with 20,000 fish at stable 120fps:
20: 
21: ![WebGL Aquarium](img/WebGL水族馆.png)
22: 
23: ## Quick Start
24: 
25: Add a `CefGlueControl` node to your scene as a browser control:
26: 
27: ```csharp
28: var browser = new CefGlueControl();
29: browser.InitialUrl = "https://godotengine.org";
30: AddChild(browser);
31: ```
32: 
33: ## Requirements
34: 
35: - **Godot Engine**: 4.6.0 or later (with .NET/Mono support)
36: - **.NET SDK**: 8.0 or later
37: - **Windows**: x64 architecture
38: 
39: ## Dependency Options
40: 
41: ### Option 1: NuGet Package (Recommended)
42: 
43: The easiest way to use GDCefGlue. All necessary files are automatically copied during build.
44: 
45: **CEF 134 (Recommended):**
46: ```xml
47: <ItemGroup>
48:   <PackageReference Include="CefGlue.Common" Version="134.6998.178" />
49:   <PackageReference Include="chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64" Version="134.3.9" />
50: </ItemGroup>
51: ```
52: 
53: **CEF 120 (Official):**
54: ```xml
55: <ItemGroup>
56:   <PackageReference Include="CefGlue.Common" Version="120.6099.0" />
57:   <PackageReference Include="chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64" Version="120.2.3" />
58: </ItemGroup>
59: ```
60: 
61: ### Option 2: Build from Source
62: 
63: If you need the latest version or want to customize CefGlue:
64: 
65: **CEF 134 (Recommended):**
66: ```bash
67: git clone https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue.git
68: ```
69: 
70: **CEF 120 (Official):**
71: ```bash
72: git clone https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue.git
73: ```
74: 
75: Place the cloned repository in your project directory:
76: ```
77: YourProject/
78: ├── GDCefGlue/
79: │   ├── extension/              # C# GDExtension source code
80: │   ├── plugin/                 # Normal C# plugin example
81: │   ├── test/
82: │   │   └── GDExtensionGame/    # Godot test project for GDExtension
83: │   ├── img/
84: │   └── README*.md
85: └── CefGlue/                    # Cloned repository
86: ```
87: 
88: ## Project Types
89: 
90: ### GDExtension Project (Experimental)
91: 
92: Located in `extension/` directory. For Godot 4.6+.
93: 
94: > **Warning:** This project is currently unstable and not recommended for production use.
95: 
96: **Features:**
97: - Uses Godot's GDExtension system
98: - Compiled as native AOT library
99: - Better performance and smaller file size
100: - Requires manual CEF file copying during export
101: - Entry point: `gdcefglue_library_init`
102: 
103: **Structure:**
104: - `extension/` - C# source code for GDExtension
105: - `extension/Dll/` - Godot .NET bindings (from [godot-dotnet](https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet))
106: - `test/GDExtensionGame/` - Godot project for testing
107: 
108: **Build Instructions:**
109: 
110: 1. **Get Godot .NET Bindings:**
111:    ```bash
112:    git clone https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet.git
113:    cd godot-dotnet
114:    dotnet build -p:GenerateGodotBindings=true
115:    ```
116:    Copy the generated `Godot.Bindings.dll` and related files to `extension/Dll/`.
117: 
118: 2. **Build GDExtension:**
119:    ```bash
120:    cd extension
121:    dotnet publish -c Debug -r win-x64 --self-contained true
122:    ```
123: 
124: 3. **Deploy:**
125:    Copy all files from `Debug/net10.0/win-x64/publish/` to `test/GDExtensionGame/lib/`.
126: 
127: 4. **Run:**
128:    Open the project with Godot 4.6 and run.
129: 
130: ### Normal C# Project
131: 
132: Located in `plugin/` directory. Traditional Godot C# project approach.
133: 
134: **Features:**
135: - Standard Godot .NET SDK project
136: - Uses `Godot.NET.Sdk`
137: - CEF files are automatically copied when using NuGet packages
138: - May need manual resource copying after export (except NuGet packages)
139: - Source code in `addons/GCefGlue/`
140: 
141: **When to use:**
142: - If you prefer traditional Godot C# development
143: - If GDExtension doesn't meet your needs
144: 
145: Update your `.csproj` to reference the projects:
146: ```xml
147: <ItemGroup>
148:   <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue\CefGlue.csproj" />
149:   <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue.Common\CefGlue.Common.csproj" />
150:   <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue.Common.Shared\CefGlue.Common.Shared.csproj" />
151: </ItemGroup>
152: ```
153: 
154: ### CefGlue Sources
155: 
156: | Source | CEF Version | Status | NuGet | GitHub |
157: |--------|-------------|--------|-------|--------|
158: | youfch/CefGlue | 134 | Unofficial (Recommended) | `CefGlue.Common 134.6998.178` | [GitHub](https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue) |
159: | OutSystems/CefGlue | 120 | Official | `CefGlue.Common 120.6099.0` | [GitHub](https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue) |
160: 
161: ## Build Instructions
162: 
163: ### Using NuGet Package
164: 
165: ```powershell
166: # Restore packages
167: dotnet restore
168: 
169: # Build
170: dotnet build
171: ```
172: 
173: ### Using Source Code
174: 
175: ```powershell
176: # Clone CefGlue (choose one)
177: git clone https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue.git   # CEF 134
178: # or
179: git clone https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue.git  # CEF 120
180: 
181: # Build
182: dotnet restore
183: dotnet build
184: ```
185: 
186: ## Build Output
187: 
188: After a successful build, the following files will be generated:
189: 
190: **Core Files:**
191: - `GDCefGlue.dll` - Main plugin assembly
192: - `Xilium.CefGlue.dll` - CefGlue wrapper
193: - `Xilium.CefGlue.Common.dll` - Common functionality
194: 
195: **CEF Native Files:**
196: - `libcef.dll` - Chromium core library
197: - `chrome_*.pak` - UI resources
198: - `resources.pak` - Application resources
199: - `locales\*.pak` - Language packs
200: 
201: **BrowserProcess Files:**
202: - `CefGlueBrowserProcess\` - Browser subprocess files
203: 
204: ## Export for Distribution
205: 
206: ### Using NuGet Package
207: 
208: When using NuGet packages, all necessary files are automatically copied during build. Just export your Godot project normally.
209: 
210: ### Using Source Code
211: 
212: When using source code dependencies, you need to manually copy files after export. See [CEF_EXPORT_GUIDE.md](CEF_EXPORT_GUIDE.md) for details.
213: 
214: ## CefGlueControl Properties
215: 
216: | Property | Type | Default | Description |
217: |----------|------|---------|-------------|
218: | `InitialUrl` | string | "about:blank" | The URL to load when the browser is created |
219: | `OpenPopupInCurrentBrowser` | bool | true | If true, popup windows navigate in the current browser |
220: | `GpuAcceleration` | bool | true | If true, enables GPU hardware acceleration |
221: | `FrameRate` | int | 60 | Browser frame rate, range 1-360 |
222: | `Transparent` | bool | false | If true, enables transparent background support |
223: 
224: ### Static Properties
225: 
226: | Property | Type | Description |
227: |----------|------|-------------|
228: | `UseGpuAcceleration` | bool | Global GPU acceleration setting, must be set before CEF initialization |
229: | `UseTransparent` | bool | Global transparent background setting, must be set before CEF initialization |
230: 
231: ### Read-only Properties
232: 
233: | Property | Type | Description |
234: |----------|------|-------------|
235: | `Address` | string | Current page URL |
236: | `IsBrowserInitialized` | bool | Whether the browser is initialized |
237: | `IsLoading` | bool | Whether the page is loading |
238: | `Title` | string | Current page title |
239: 
240: ## CefGlueControl Methods
241: 
242: | Method | Description |
243: |--------|-------------|
244: | `GoBack()` | Navigate back in history |
245: | `GoForward()` | Navigate forward in history |
246: | `NavigateToUrl(string url)` | Navigate to the specified URL |
247: | `Reload(bool ignoreCache = false)` | Reload the current page, optionally ignoring cache |
248: | `ExecuteJavaScript(string code, ...)` | Execute JavaScript code |
249: | `EvaluateJavaScript<T>(string code, ...)` | Execute JavaScript and return result |
250: | `ShowDeveloperTools()` | Open developer tools |
251: | `CloseDeveloperTools()` | Close developer tools |
252: 
253: ## GPU Configuration
254: 
255: ```csharp
256: // Enable GPU acceleration (default) - set in inspector
257: // GpuAcceleration = true;
258: 
259: // Disable GPU acceleration (software rendering) - set in inspector
260: // GpuAcceleration = false;
261: ```
262: 
263: **Note:** This property is exposed to the Godot inspector. Set it before the control is initialized.
264: 
265: ## Troubleshooting
266: 
267: ### GPU Process Crashed
268: 
269: 1. Ensure all CEF files are copied correctly
270: 2. Try disabling GPU acceleration in the inspector
271: 
272: ### Missing DLLs
273: 
274: 1. Run `dotnet restore` to restore NuGet packages
275: 2. Clean and rebuild the solution
276: 
277: ### Blank Page
278: 
279: 1. Check if `locales` directory exists
280: 2. Ensure `resources.pak` is present
281: 
282: ## Known Issues
283: 
284: 1. **Right-click Context Menu**: Not supported
285: 2. **Network Notification**: `WSALookupServiceBegin failed with: 10108` is a normal warning
286: 
287: ## License
288: 
289: GDCefGlue is licensed under the MIT License. See [LICENSE](LICENSE) for details.
290: 
291: Third-party dependencies:
292: - [CefGlue](https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue) - BSD-3-Clause
293: - [CEF](https://bitbucket.org/chromiumembedded/cef) - BSD-3-Clause
294: - [Godot Engine](https://godotengine.org) - MIT
295: - [godot-dotnet](https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet) - MIT

(End of file - total 295 lines)

  4026: "oldString": "```\nYourProject/\n├── GDCefGlue/\n│   ├── GDExtension/\n│   │   ├── Extension/     # C# GDExtension source code\n│   │   └── Project/       # Godot test project\n│   ├── NormalProject/     # Normal C# project example\n│   ├── img/\n│   └── README*.md\n└── CefGlue/               # Cloned repository\n```"
  4044: "oldString": "### GDExtension Project (Experimental)\n\nLocated in `GDExtension/` directory. For Godot 4.6+.\n\n> **Warning:** This project is currently unstable and not recommended for production use.\n\n**Features:**\n- Uses Godot's GDExtension system\n- Compiled as native AOT library\n- Better performance and smaller file size\n- Requires manual CEF file copying during export\n- Entry point: `gdcefglue_library_init`\n\n**Structure:**\n- `GDExtension/Extension/` - C# source code for GDExtension\n- `GDExtension/Extension/Dll/` - Godot .NET bindings (from [godot-dotnet](https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet))\n- `GDExtension/Project/` - Godot project for testing"
  4080: "oldString": "2. **Build GDExtension:**\n   ```bash\n   cd GDExtension/Extension\n   dotnet publish -c Debug -r win-x64 --self-contained true\n   ```\n\n3. **Deploy:**\n   Copy all files from `Debug/net10.0/win-x64/publish/` to `GDExtension/Project/lib/`.\n\n4. **Run:**\n   Open the project with Godot 4.6 and run.\n\n### Normal C# Project\n\nLocated in `NormalProject/` directory. Traditional Godot C# project approach."
  4116: "oldString": "```\n你的项目/\n├── GDCefGlue/\n│   ├── GDExtension/\n│   │   ├── Extension/     # C# GDExtension 源代码\n│   │   └── Project/       # Godot 测试项目\n│   ├── NormalProject/     # 普通 C# 项目示例\n│   ├── img/\n│   └── README*.md\n└── CefGlue/               # 克隆的仓库\n```"
  4133: "oldString": "位于 `GDExtension/` 目录。适用于 Godot 4.6+。\n\n> **警告：** 此项目当前不稳定，不推荐用于生产环境。\n\n**特点：**\n\n- 使用 Godot 的 GDExtension 系统\n- 编译为原生 AOT 库\n- 性能更好，文件体积更小\n- 导出时需要手动复制 CEF 文件\n- 入口点：`gdcefglue_library_init`\n\n**结构：**\n\n- `GDExtension/Extension/` - GDExtension 的 C# 源代码\n- `GDExtension/Extension/Dll/` - Godot .NET 绑定（来自 [godot-dotnet](https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet)）\n- `GDExtension/Project/` - 用于测试的 Godot 项目"
  4151: "oldString": "3. **构建 GDExtension：**\n\n    进入 `GDExtension/Extension` 目录执行："
  4198: 87: 位于 `GDExtension/` 目录。适用于 Godot 4.6+。

## Plugin Demo Analysis (plugin/ - C# NormalProject)

### Architecture
The plugin demo is a **standard Godot C# project** (using `Godot.NET.Sdk`), NOT an editor addon plugin. There is **no `plugin.cfg`** file — the `CefGlueControl` exposes itself via the `[GlobalClass]` attribute, making it available in Godot's node creation menu.

### UI Layout (MainUI.tscn → MainUi.cs)

```
MainUI (Control, fullscreen)
├── Browser (Control, CefGlueControl script, FrameRate=120, Transparent=true)
│   └── anchored: top=40px, bottom=-24px (leaving room for toolbar & status bar)
├── Toolbar (Panel, 40px tall, full width)
│   ├── BackButton    "◀"
│   ├── ForwardButton "▶"
│   ├── ReloadButton  "↻"
│   ├── UrlInput      (LineEdit, placeholder="Enter URL...")
│   └── GoButton      "Go"
├── StatusBar (Panel, 24px tall, anchored to bottom)
│   └── StatusLabel   ("Initializing...")
└── Node3D (3D environment for visual polish)
    ├── WorldEnvironment (ProceduralSky + glow)
    ├── DirectionalLight3D (with shadows)
    └── Camera3D
```

### MainUi.cs Feature Set

| Feature | Implementation |
|---------|---------------|
| **Initial URL** | `_browser.InitialUrl = "https://www.bing.com"` (line 25) |
| **URL navigation** | NavigateToUrl() auto-prepends `https://` if missing scheme (lines 101-116) |
| **Browser events** | 5 event handlers: BrowserInitialized → "Ready", AddressChanged → updates URL bar, LoadStart → "Loading...", LoadEnd → "Done", LoadError → error text |
| **Navigation buttons** | GoBack(), GoForward(), Reload() — simple passthrough to CefGlueControl methods |
| **URL input** | TextSubmitted (Enter key) + Go button both trigger NavigateToUrl() |
| **Status updates** | Uses CallDeferred for thread-safe UI updates from CEF callbacks |

### URL Initialization Pattern (Full Codebase)

| Location | File | Value |
|----------|------|-------|
| C# default (plugin) | `plugin/addons/GCefGlue/CefGlueControl.cs:56` | `"about:blank"` |
| C# demo override | `plugin/MainUi.cs:25` | `"https://www.bing.com"` |
| C# default (extension) | `extension/CefGlueControl.cs:44` | `"about:blank"` |
| GDScript demo | `test/GDExtensionGame/Main.gd:5` | `"https://www.bing.com"` |
| GDExtension scene | `test/GDExtensionGame/Main.tscn:31` | `"https://test.playready.microsoft.com/..."` |
| README example | `README.md:29` | `"https://godotengine.org"` |
| CefBrowserHost.Create | both CefGlueControl.cs files | uses `InitialUrl` property as 4th arg |
| ExecuteJavaScript default | both CefGlueControl.cs files | `url ?? "about:blank"` as 2nd arg |

### Key Difference: Plugin vs GDExtension Demos

| Aspect | plugin/ (C# demo) | test/GDExtensionGame/ (GDScript demo) |
|--------|-------------------|---------------------------------------|
| Language | C# (MainUi.cs, 117 lines) | GDScript (Main.gd, 5 lines) |
| UI | Full toolbar + status bar | Just a fullscreen CefGlueControl |
| Features | URL bar, nav buttons, status tracking | Only sets InitialUrl |
| CefGlueControl location | `addons/GCefGlue/CefGlueControl.cs` | Compiled from `extension/` |
| Registration | `[GlobalClass]` attribute | `BindMembers()` via Godot.Bridge |
</answer>

<next_steps>
To replicate the plugin demo's functionality in the GDExtension demo:

3. **URL logic** — Implement the `https://` auto-prepend pattern from MainUi.cs NavigateToUrl() (lines 107-110)
4. **Events** — CefGlueControl exposes the same events (BrowserInitialized, AddressChanged, LoadStart, LoadEnd, LoadError) — connect them in GDScript using `browser.connect("AddressChanged", _on_address_changed)`
5. **FrameRate optimization** — Consider setting `FrameRate = 120` in the GDExtension demo's tscn (plugin demo uses 120fps vs extension default of 60)

29:         _browser.LoadEnd += OnLoadEnd;
30:         _browser.LoadError += OnLoadError;
31: 
32:         _goButton.Pressed += OnGoPressed;
33:         _backButton.Pressed += OnBackPressed;
34:         _forwardButton.Pressed += OnForwardPressed;
35:         _reloadButton.Pressed += OnReloadPressed;
36:         _urlInput.TextSubmitted += OnUrlSubmitted;
37:     }
38: 
39:     private void OnBrowserInitialized()
40:     {
41:         CallDeferred(nameof(UpdateStatusLabel), "Ready");
42:     }
43: 
44:     private void OnAddressChanged(object sender, string url)
45:     {
46:         CallDeferred(nameof(UpdateUrlInput), url);
47:     }
48: 
49:     private void OnLoadStart(object sender, Xilium.CefGlue.Common.Events.LoadStartEventArgs e)
50:     {
51:         CallDeferred(nameof(UpdateStatusLabel), "Loading...");
52:     }
53: 
54:     private void OnLoadEnd(object sender, Xilium.CefGlue.Common.Events.LoadEndEventArgs e)
55:     {
56:         CallDeferred(nameof(UpdateStatusLabel), "Done");
57:     }
58: 
59:     private void OnLoadError(object sender, Xilium.CefGlue.Common.Events.LoadErrorEventArgs e)
60:     {
61:         CallDeferred(nameof(UpdateStatusLabel), $"Error: {e.ErrorText}");
62:     }
63: 
64:     private void UpdateStatusLabel(string text)
65:     {
66:         if (_statusLabel != null)
67:             _statusLabel.Text = text;
68:     }
69: 
70:     private void UpdateUrlInput(string url)
71:     {
72:         if (_urlInput != null)
73:             _urlInput.Text = url;
74:     }
75: 
76:     private void OnBackPressed()
77:     {
78:         _browser?.GoBack();
79:     }
80: 
81:     private void OnForwardPressed()
82:     {
83:         _browser?.GoForward();
84:     }
85: 
86:     private void OnReloadPressed()
87:     {
88:         _browser?.Reload();
89:     }
90: 
91:     private void OnGoPressed()
92:     {
93:         NavigateToUrl();
94:     }
95: 
96:     private void OnUrlSubmitted(string text)
97:     {
98:         NavigateToUrl();
99:     }
100: 
101:     private void NavigateToUrl()
102:     {
103:         var url = _urlInput?.Text?.Trim();
104:         if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(url))
105:             return;
106: 
107:         if (!url.StartsWith("http://") && !url.StartsWith("https://") && !url.StartsWith("about:"))
108:         {
109:             url = "https://" + url;
110:         }
111: 
112:         if (_browser != null)
113:         {
114:             _browser.Address = url;
115:         }
116:     }
117: }

For Main.gd:
```gdscript
extends Control

var _browser: CefGlueControl
var _url_input: LineEdit
var _go_button: Button
var _back_button: Button
var _forward_button: Button
var _reload_button: Button
var _status_label: Label

func _ready() -> void:
    _browser = $CefGlueControl
    _url_input = $Toolbar/UrlInput
    _go_button = $Toolbar/GoButton
    _back_button = $Toolbar/BackButton
    _forward_button = $Toolbar/ForwardButton
    _reload_button = $Toolbar/ReloadButton
    _status_label = $StatusBar/StatusLabel
    
    _browser.InitialUrl = "https://www.bing.com"
    _browser.BrowserInitialized.connect(_on_browser_initialized)
    _browser.AddressChanged.connect(_on_address_changed)
    _browser.LoadStart.connect(_on_load_start)
    _browser.LoadEnd.connect(_on_load_end)
    _browser.LoadError.connect(_on_load_error)
    
    _go_button.pressed.connect(_on_go_pressed)
    _back_button.pressed.connect(_on_back_pressed)
    _forward_button.pressed.connect(_on_forward_pressed)
    _reload_button.pressed.connect(_on_reload_pressed)
    _url_input.text_submitted.connect(_on_url_submitted)

func _on_browser_initialized() -> void:
    _status_label.text = "Ready"

func _on_address_changed(sender, url: String) -> void:
    _url_input.text = url

func _on_load_start(sender, e) -> void:
    _status_label.text = "Loading..."

func _on_load_end(sender, e) -> void:
    _status_label.text = "Done"

func _on_load_error(sender, e) -> void:
    _status_label.text = "Error: " + str(e.ErrorText)

func _on_back_pressed() -> void:
    _browser.GoBack()

func _on_forward_pressed() -> void:
    _browser.GoForward()

func _on_reload_pressed() -> void:
    _browser.Reload()

func _on_go_pressed() -> void:
    _navigate_to_url()

func _on_url_submitted(text: String) -> void:
    _navigate_to_url()

func _navigate_to_url() -> void:
    var url = _url_input.text.strip_edges()
    if url.is_empty():
        return
    
    if not url.begins_with("http://") and not url.begins_with("https://") and not url.begins_with("about:"):
        url = "https://" + url
    
    _browser.Address = url
```

```gdscript
extends Control

var _browser
var _url_input: LineEdit
var _go_button: Button
var _back_button: Button
var _forward_button: Button
var _reload_button: Button
var _status_label: Label

func _ready() -> void:
    _browser = $CefGlueControl
    _url_input = $Toolbar/UrlInput
    _go_button = $Toolbar/GoButton
    _back_button = $Toolbar/BackButton
    _forward_button = $Toolbar/ForwardButton
    _reload_button = $Toolbar/ReloadButton
    _status_label = $StatusBar/StatusLabel
    
    _browser.InitialUrl = "https://www.bing.com"
    _browser.FrameRate = 120
    _browser.Transparent = true
    
    _browser.BrowserInitialized.connect(_on_browser_initialized)
    _browser.AddressChanged.connect(_on_address_changed)
    _browser.LoadStart.connect(_on_load_start)
    _browser.LoadEnd.connect(_on_load_end)
    _browser.LoadError.connect(_on_load_error)
    
    _go_button.pressed.connect(_on_go_pressed)
    _back_button.pressed.connect(_on_back_pressed)
    _forward_button.pressed.connect(_on_forward_pressed)
    _reload_button.pressed.connect(_on_reload_pressed)
    _url_input.text_submitted.connect(_on_url_submitted)

func _on_browser_initialized() -> void:
    _status_label.text = "Ready"

func _on_address_changed(url: String) -> void:
    _url_input.text = url

func _on_load_start() -> void:
    _status_label.text = "Loading..."

func _on_load_end() -> void:
    _status_label.text = "Done"

func _on_load_error(error_text: String, failed_url: String) -> void:
    _status_label.text = "Error: " + error_text

func _on_back_pressed() -> void:
    _browser.GoBack()

func _on_forward_pressed() -> void:
    _browser.GoForward()

func _on_reload_pressed() -> void:
    _browser.Reload()

func _on_go_pressed() -> void:
    _navigate_to_url()

func _on_url_submitted(text: String) -> void:
    _navigate_to_url()

func _navigate_to_url() -> void:
    var url = _url_input.text.strip_edges()
    if url.is_empty():
        return
    
    if not url.begins_with("http://") and not url.begins_with("https://") and not url.begins_with("about:"):
        url = "https://" + url
    
    _browser.Address = url
```

public string Address
{
    get => InitialUrl;
    set

        if (_browser != null && _browser.GetMainFrame() != null)
        {
            _browser.GetMainFrame().LoadUrl(value);
        }
        else
        {
            InitialUrl = value;
        }
    }
}
```

Yes, setting `Address` triggers navigation. Good.

But wait, `Address` was bound as a readonly property in BindMembers. That means GDScript can only GET it, not SET it. This is a problem - we need Address to be writable for navigation.

Actually, there's a `NavigateToUrl(string url)` method that's already bound. We should use that instead of setting the Address property.

Let me update the GDScript to use `_browser.NavigateToUrl(url)` instead of `_browser.Address = url`.

</details>

<content>
1: # GDCefGlue
2: 
3: A CEF (Chromium Embedded Framework) browser control for Godot 4.x using CefGlue.
4: 
5: [中文文档](README_CN.md)
6: 
7: ## Features
8: 
9: - GPU hardware acceleration support
10: - IME support for Chinese/Japanese/Korean input
11: - Popup handling
12: - Complete keyboard and mouse support
13: - Easy integration with Godot 4.x
14: 
15: ## Performance Demo
16: 
17: ### WebGL Aquarium
18: 
19: Supports WebGL rendering with 20,000 fish at stable 120fps:
20: 
21: ![WebGL Aquarium](img/WebGL水族馆.png)
22: 
23: ## Quick Start
24: 
25: Add a `CefGlueControl` node to your scene as a browser control:
26: 
27: ```csharp
28: var browser = new CefGlueControl();
29: browser.InitialUrl = "https://godotengine.org";
30: AddChild(browser);
31: ```
32: 
33: ## Requirements
34: 
35: - **Godot Engine**: 4.6.0 or later (with .NET/Mono support)
36: - **.NET SDK**: 8.0 or later
37: - **Windows**: x64 architecture
38: 
39: ## Dependency Options
40: 
41: ### Option 1: NuGet Package (Recommended)
42: 
43: The easiest way to use GDCefGlue. All necessary files are automatically copied during build.
44: 
45: **CEF 134 (Recommended):**
46: ```xml
47: <ItemGroup>
48:   <PackageReference Include="CefGlue.Common" Version="134.6998.178" />
49:   <PackageReference Include="chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64" Version="134.3.9" />
50: </ItemGroup>
51: ```
52: 
53: **CEF 120 (Official):**
54: ```xml
55: <ItemGroup>
56:   <PackageReference Include="CefGlue.Common" Version="120.6099.0" />
57:   <PackageReference Include="chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64" Version="120.2.3" />
58: </ItemGroup>
59: ```
60: 
61: ### Option 2: Build from Source
62: 
63: If you need the latest version or want to customize CefGlue:
64: 
65: **CEF 134 (Recommended):**
66: ```bash
67: git clone https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue.git
68: ```
69: 
70: **CEF 120 (Official):**
71: ```bash
72: git clone https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue.git
73: ```
74: 
75: Place the cloned repository in your project directory:
76: ```
77: YourProject/
78: ├── GDCefGlue/
79: │   ├── extension/              # C# GDExtension source code
80: │   ├── plugin/                 # Normal C# plugin example
81: │   ├── test/
82: │   │   └── GDExtensionGame/    # Godot test project for GDExtension
83: │   ├── img/
84: │   └── README*.md
85: └── CefGlue/                    # Cloned repository
86: ```
87: 
88: ## Project Types
89: 
90: ### GDExtension Project (Experimental)
91: 
92: Located in `extension/` directory. For Godot 4.6+.
93: 
94: > **Warning:** This project is currently unstable and not recommended for production use.
95: 
96: **Features:**
97: - Uses Godot's GDExtension system
98: - Compiled as native AOT library
99: - Better performance and smaller file size
100: - Requires manual CEF file copying during export
101: - Entry point: `gdcefglue_library_init`
102: 
103: **Structure:**
104: - `extension/` - C# source code for GDExtension
105: - `extension/Dll/` - Godot .NET bindings (from [godot-dotnet](https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet))
106: - `test/GDExtensionGame/` - Godot project for testing
107: 
108: **Build Instructions:**
109: 
110: 1. **Get Godot .NET Bindings:**
111:    ```bash
112:    git clone https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet.git
113:    cd godot-dotnet
114:    dotnet build -p:GenerateGodotBindings=true
115:    ```
116:    Copy the generated `Godot.Bindings.dll` and related files to `extension/Dll/`.
117: 
118: 2. **Build GDExtension:**
119:    ```bash
120:    cd extension
121:    dotnet publish -c Debug -r win-x64 --self-contained true
122:    ```
123: 
124: 3. **Deploy:**
125:    Copy all files from `Debug/net10.0/win-x64/publish/` to `test/GDExtensionGame/lib/`.
126: 
127: 4. **Run:**
128:    Open the project with Godot 4.6 and run.
129: 
130: ### Normal C# Project
131: 
132: Located in `plugin/` directory. Traditional Godot C# project approach.
133: 
134: **Features:**
135: - Standard Godot .NET SDK project
136: - Uses `Godot.NET.Sdk`
137: - CEF files are automatically copied when using NuGet packages
138: - May need manual resource copying after export (except NuGet packages)
139: - Source code in `addons/GCefGlue/`
140: 
141: **When to use:**
142: - If you prefer traditional Godot C# development
143: - If GDExtension doesn't meet your needs
144: 
145: Update your `.csproj` to reference the projects:
146: ```xml
147: <ItemGroup>
148:   <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue\CefGlue.csproj" />
149:   <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue.Common\CefGlue.Common.csproj" />
150:   <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue.Common.Shared\CefGlue.Common.Shared.csproj" />
151: </ItemGroup>
152: ```
153: 
154: ### CefGlue Sources
155: 
156: | Source | CEF Version | Status | NuGet | GitHub |
157: |--------|-------------|--------|-------|--------|
158: | youfch/CefGlue | 134 | Unofficial (Recommended) | `CefGlue.Common 134.6998.178` | [GitHub](https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue) |
159: | OutSystems/CefGlue | 120 | Official | `CefGlue.Common 120.6099.0` | [GitHub](https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue) |
160: 
161: ## Build Instructions
162: 
163: ### Using NuGet Package
164: 
165: ```powershell
166: # Restore packages
167: dotnet restore
168: 
169: # Build
170: dotnet build
171: ```
172: 
173: ### Using Source Code
174: 
175: ```powershell
176: # Clone CefGlue (choose one)
177: git clone https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue.git   # CEF 134
178: # or
179: git clone https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue.git  # CEF 120
180: 
181: # Build
182: dotnet restore
183: dotnet build
184: ```
185: 
186: ## Build Output
187: 
188: After a successful build, the following files will be generated:
189: 
190: **Core Files:**
191: - `GDCefGlue.dll` - Main plugin assembly
192: - `Xilium.CefGlue.dll` - CefGlue wrapper
193: - `Xilium.CefGlue.Common.dll` - Common functionality
194: 
195: **CEF Native Files:**
196: - `libcef.dll` - Chromium core library
197: - `chrome_*.pak` - UI resources
198: - `resources.pak` - Application resources
199: - `locales\*.pak` - Language packs
200: 
201: **BrowserProcess Files:**
202: - `CefGlueBrowserProcess\` - Browser subprocess files
203: 
204: ## Export for Distribution
205: 
206: ### Using NuGet Package
207: 
208: When using NuGet packages, all necessary files are automatically copied during build. Just export your Godot project normally.
209: 
210: ### Using Source Code
211: 
212: When using source code dependencies, you need to manually copy files after export. See [CEF_EXPORT_GUIDE.md](CEF_EXPORT_GUIDE.md) for details.
213: 
214: ## CefGlueControl Properties
215: 
216: | Property | Type | Default | Description |
217: |----------|------|---------|-------------|
218: | `InitialUrl` | string | "about:blank" | The URL to load when the browser is created |
219: | `OpenPopupInCurrentBrowser` | bool | true | If true, popup windows navigate in the current browser |
220: | `GpuAcceleration` | bool | true | If true, enables GPU hardware acceleration |
221: | `FrameRate` | int | 60 | Browser frame rate, range 1-360 |
222: | `Transparent` | bool | false | If true, enables transparent background support |
223: 
224: ### Static Properties
225: 
226: | Property | Type | Description |
227: |----------|------|-------------|
228: | `UseGpuAcceleration` | bool | Global GPU acceleration setting, must be set before CEF initialization |
229: | `UseTransparent` | bool | Global transparent background setting, must be set before CEF initialization |
230: 
231: ### Read-only Properties
232: 
233: | Property | Type | Description |
234: |----------|------|-------------|
235: | `Address` | string | Current page URL |
236: | `IsBrowserInitialized` | bool | Whether the browser is initialized |
237: | `IsLoading` | bool | Whether the page is loading |
238: | `Title` | string | Current page title |
239: 
240: ## CefGlueControl Methods
241: 
242: | Method | Description |
243: |--------|-------------|
244: | `GoBack()` | Navigate back in history |
245: | `GoForward()` | Navigate forward in history |
246: | `NavigateToUrl(string url)` | Navigate to the specified URL |
247: | `Reload(bool ignoreCache = false)` | Reload the current page, optionally ignoring cache |
248: | `ExecuteJavaScript(string code, ...)` | Execute JavaScript code |
249: | `EvaluateJavaScript<T>(string code, ...)` | Execute JavaScript and return result |
250: | `ShowDeveloperTools()` | Open developer tools |
251: | `CloseDeveloperTools()` | Close developer tools |
252: 
253: ## GPU Configuration
254: 
255: ```csharp
256: // Enable GPU acceleration (default) - set in inspector
257: // GpuAcceleration = true;
258: 
259: // Disable GPU acceleration (software rendering) - set in inspector
260: // GpuAcceleration = false;
261: ```
262: 
263: **Note:** This property is exposed to the Godot inspector. Set it before the control is initialized.
264: 
265: ## Troubleshooting
266: 
267: ### GPU Process Crashed
268: 
269: 1. Ensure all CEF files are copied correctly
270: 2. Try disabling GPU acceleration in the inspector
271: 
272: ### Missing DLLs
273: 
274: 1. Run `dotnet restore` to restore NuGet packages
275: 2. Clean and rebuild the solution
276: 
277: ### Blank Page
278: 
279: 1. Check if `locales` directory exists
280: 2. Ensure `resources.pak` is present
281: 
282: ## Known Issues
283: 
284: 1. **Right-click Context Menu**: Not supported
285: 2. **Network Notification**: `WSALookupServiceBegin failed with: 10108` is a normal warning
286: 
287: ## License
288: 
289: GDCefGlue is licensed under the MIT License. See [LICENSE](LICENSE) for details.
290: 
291: Third-party dependencies:
292: - [CefGlue](https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue) - BSD-3-Clause
293: - [CEF](https://bitbucket.org/chromiumembedded/cef) - BSD-3-Clause
294: - [Godot Engine](https://godotengine.org) - MIT
295: - [godot-dotnet](https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet) - MIT

(End of file - total 295 lines)
</content>
