[Project README: E:\Work\Hub\GDCefGlue\README.md]
# GDCefGlue

A CEF (Chromium Embedded Framework) browser control for Godot 4.x using CefGlue.

[中文文档](README_CN.md)

## Features

- GPU hardware acceleration support
- IME support for Chinese/Japanese/Korean input
- Popup handling
- Complete keyboard and mouse support
- Easy integration with Godot 4.x

## Performance Demo

### WebGL Aquarium

Supports WebGL rendering with 20,000 fish at stable 120fps:

![WebGL Aquarium](img/WebGL水族馆.png)

## Quick Start

Add a `CefGlueControl` node to your scene as a browser control:

```csharp
var browser = new CefGlueControl();
browser.InitialUrl = "https://godotengine.org";
AddChild(browser);
```

## Requirements

- **Godot Engine**: 4.6.0 or later (with .NET/Mono support)
- **.NET SDK**: 8.0 or later
- **Windows**: x64 architecture

## Dependency Options

### Option 1: NuGet Package (Recommended)

The easiest way to use GDCefGlue. All necessary files are automatically copied during build.

**CEF 134 (Recommended):**
```xml
<ItemGroup>
  <PackageReference Include="CefGlue.Common" Version="134.6998.178" />
  <PackageReference Include="chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64" Version="134.3.9" />
</ItemGroup>
```

**CEF 120 (Official):**
```xml
<ItemGroup>
  <PackageReference Include="CefGlue.Common" Version="120.6099.0" />
  <PackageReference Include="chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64" Version="120.2.3" />
</ItemGroup>
```

### Option 2: Build from Source

If you need the latest version or want to customize CefGlue:

**CEF 134 (Recommended):**
```bash
git clone https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue.git
```

**CEF 120 (Official):**
```bash
git clone https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue.git
```

Place the cloned repository in your project directory:
```
YourProject/
├── GDCefGlue/
│   ├── GDExtension/
│   │   ├── Extension/     # C# GDExtension source code
│   │   └── Project/       # Godot test project
│   ├── NormalProject/     # Normal C# project example
│   ├── img/
│   └── README*.md
└── CefGlue/               # Cloned repository
```

## Project Types

### GDExtension Project (Experimental)

Located in `GDExtension/` directory. For Godot 4.6+.

> **Warning:** This project is currently unstable and not recommended for production use.

**Features:**
- Uses Godot's GDExtension system
- Compiled as native AOT library
- Better performance and smaller file size
- Requires manual CEF file copying during export
- Entry point: `gdcefglue_library_init`

**Structure:**
- `GDExtension/Extension/` - C# source code for GDExtension
- `GDExtension/Extension/Dll/` - Godot .NET bindings (from [godot-dotnet](https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet))
- `GDExtension/Project/` - Godot project for testing

**Build Instructions:**

1. **Get Godot .NET Bindings:**
   ```bash
   git clone https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet.git
   cd godot-dotnet
   dotnet build -p:GenerateGodotBindings=true
   ```
   Copy the generated `Godot.Bindings.dll` and related files to `GDExtension/Extension/Dll/`.

2. **Build GDExtension:**
   ```bash
   cd GDExtension/Extension
   dotnet publish -c Debug -r win-x64 --self-contained true
   ```

3. **Deploy:**
   Copy all files from `Debug/net10.0/win-x64/publish/` to `GDExtension/Project/lib/`.

4. **Run:**
   Open the project with Godot 4.6 and run.

### Normal C# Project

Located in `NormalProject/` directory. Traditional Godot C# project approach.

**Features:**
- Standard Godot .NET SDK project
- Uses `Godot.NET.Sdk`
- CEF files are automatically copied when using NuGet packages
- May need manual resource copying after export (except NuGet packages)
- Source code in `addons/GCefGlue/`

**When to use:**
- If you prefer traditional Godot C# development
- If GDExtension doesn't meet your needs

Update your `.csproj` to reference the projects:
```xml
<ItemGroup>
  <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue\CefGlue.csproj" />
  <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue.Common\CefGlue.Common.csproj" />
  <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue.Common.Shared\CefGlue.Common.Shared.csproj" />
</ItemGroup>
```

### CefGlue Sources

| Source | CEF Version | Status | NuGet | GitHub |
|--------|-------------|--------|-------|--------|
| youfch/CefGlue | 134 | Unofficial (Recommended) | `CefGlue.Common 134.6998.178` | [GitHub](https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue) |
| OutSystems/CefGlue | 120 | Official | `CefGlue.Common 120.6099.0` | [GitHub](https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue) |

## Build Instructions

### Using NuGet Package

```powershell
# Restore packages
dotnet restore

# Build
dotnet build
```

### Using Source Code

```powershell
# Clone CefGlue (choose one)
git clone https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue.git   # CEF 134
# or
git clone https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue.git  # CEF 120

# Build
dotnet restore
dotnet build
```

## Build Output

After a successful build, the following files will be generated:

**Core Files:**
- `GDCefGlue.dll` - Main plugin assembly
- `Xilium.CefGlue.dll` - CefGlue wrapper
- `Xilium.CefGlue.Common.dll` - Common functionality

**CEF Native Files:**
- `libcef.dll` - Chromium core library
- `chrome_*.pak` - UI resources
- `resources.pak` - Application resources
- `locales\*.pak` - Language packs

**BrowserProcess Files:**
- `CefGlueBrowserProcess\` - Browser subprocess files

## Export for Distribution

### Using NuGet Package

When using NuGet packages, all necessary files are automatically copied during build. Just export your Godot project normally.

### Using Source Code

When using source code dependencies, you need to manually copy files after export. See [CEF_EXPORT_GUIDE.md](CEF_EXPORT_GUIDE.md) for details.

## CefGlueControl Properties

| Property | Type | Default | Description |
|----------|------|---------|-------------|
| `InitialUrl` | string | "about:blank" | The URL to load when the browser is created |
| `OpenPopupInCurrentBrowser` | bool | true | If true, popup windows navigate in the current browser |
| `GpuAcceleration` | bool | true | If true, enables GPU hardware acceleration |
| `FrameRate` | int | 60 | Browser frame rate, range 1-360 |
| `Transparent` | bool | false | If true, enables transparent background support |

### Static Properties

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `UseGpuAcceleration` | bool | Global GPU acceleration setting, must be set before CEF initialization |
| `UseTransparent` | bool | Global transparent background setting, must be set before CEF initialization |

### Read-only Properties

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `Address` | string | Current page URL |
| `IsBrowserInitialized` | bool | Whether the browser is initialized |
| `IsLoading` | bool | Whether the page is loading |
| `Title` | string | Current page title |

## CefGlueControl Methods

| Method | Description |
|--------|-------------|
| `GoBack()` | Navigate back in history |
| `GoForward()` | Navigate forward in history |
| `NavigateToUrl(string url)` | Navigate to the specified URL |
| `Reload(bool ignoreCache = false)` | Reload the current page, optionally ignoring cache |
| `ExecuteJavaScript(string code, ...)` | Execute JavaScript code |
| `EvaluateJavaScript<T>(string code, ...)` | Execute JavaScript and return result |
| `ShowDeveloperTools()` | Open developer tools |
| `CloseDeveloperTools()` | Close developer tools |

## GPU Configuration

```csharp
// Enable GPU acceleration (default) - set in inspector
// GpuAcceleration = true;

// Disable GPU acceleration (software rendering) - set in inspector
// GpuAcceleration = false;
```

**Note:** This property is exposed to the Godot inspector. Set it before the control is initialized.

## Troubleshooting

### GPU Process Crashed

1. Ensure all CEF files are copied correctly
2. Try disabling GPU acceleration in the inspector

### Missing DLLs

1. Run `dotnet restore` to restore NuGet packages
2. Clean and rebuild the solution

### Blank Page

1. Check if `locales` directory exists
2. Ensure `resources.pak` is present

## Known Issues

1. **Right-click Context Menu**: Not supported
2. **Network Notification**: `WSALookupServiceBegin failed with: 10108` is a normal warning

## License

GDCefGlue is licensed under the MIT License. See [LICENSE](LICENSE) for details.

Third-party dependencies:
- [CefGlue](https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue) - BSD-3-Clause
- [CEF](https://bitbucket.org/chromiumembedded/cef) - BSD-3-Clause
- [Godot Engine](https://godotengine.org) - MIT
- [godot-dotnet](https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet) - MIT

```

243: 1: # GDCefGlue
244: 2: 
245: 3: A CEF (Chromium Embedded Framework) browser control for Godot 4.x using CefGlue.
246: 4: 
247: 5: [中文文档](README_CN.md)
248: 6: 
249: 7: ## Features
250: 8: 
251: 9: - GPU hardware acceleration support
252: 10: - IME support for Chinese/Japanese/Korean input
253: 11: - Popup handling
254: 12: - Complete keyboard and mouse support
255: 13: - Easy integration with Godot 4.x
256: 14: 
257: 15: ## Performance Demo
258: 16: 
259: 17: ### WebGL Aquarium
260: 18: 
261: 19: Supports WebGL rendering with 20,000 fish at stable 120fps:
262: 20: 
263: 21: ![WebGL Aquarium](img/WebGL水族馆.png)
264: 22: 
265: 23: ## Quick Start
266: 24: 
267: 25: Add a `CefGlueControl` node to your scene as a browser control:
268: 26: 
269: 27: ```csharp
270: 28: var browser = new CefGlueControl();
271: 29: browser.InitialUrl = "https://godotengine.org";
272: 30: AddChild(browser);
273: 31: ```
274: 32: 
275: 33: ## Requirements
276: 34: 
277: 35: - **Godot Engine**: 4.6.0 or later (with .NET/Mono support)
278: 36: - **.NET SDK**: 8.0 or later
279: 37: - **Windows**: x64 architecture
280: 38: 
281: 39: ## Dependency Options
282: 40: 
283: 41: ### Option 1: NuGet Package (Recommended)
284: 42: 
285: 43: The easiest way to use GDCefGlue. All necessary files are automatically copied during build.
286: 44: 
287: 45: **CEF 134 (Recommended):**
288: 46: ```xml
289: 47: <ItemGroup>
290: 48:   <PackageReference Include="CefGlue.Common" Version="134.6998.178" />
291: 49:   <PackageReference Include="chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64" Version="134.3.9" />
292: 50: </ItemGroup>
293: 51: ```
294: 52: 
295: 53: **CEF 120 (Official):**
296: 54: ```xml
297: 55: <ItemGroup>
298: 56:   <PackageReference Include="CefGlue.Common" Version="120.6099.0" />
299: 57:   <PackageReference Include="chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64" Version="120.2.3" />
300: 58: </ItemGroup>
301: 59: ```
302: 60: 
303: 61: ### Option 2: Build from Source
304: 62: 
305: 63: If you need the latest version or want to customize CefGlue:
306: 64: 
307: 65: **CEF 134 (Recommended):**
308: 66: ```bash
309: 67: git clone https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue.git
310: 68: ```
311: 69: 
312: 70: **CEF 120 (Official):**
313: 71: ```bash
314: 72: git clone https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue.git
315: 73: ```
316: 74: 
317: 75: Place the cloned repository in your project directory:
318: 76: ```
319: 77: YourProject/
320: 78: ├── GDCefGlue/
321: 79: │   ├── GDExtension/
322: 80: │   │   ├── Extension/     # C# GDExtension source code
323: 81: │   │   └── Project/       # Godot test project
324: 82: │   ├── NormalProject/     # Normal C# project example
325: 83: │   ├── img/
326: 84: │   └── README*.md
327: 85: └── CefGlue/               # Cloned repository
328: 86: ```
329: 87: 
330: 88: ## Project Types
331: 89: 
332: 90: ### GDExtension Project (Experimental)
333: 91: 
334: 92: Located in `GDExtension/` directory. For Godot 4.6+.
335: 93: 
336: 94: > **Warning:** This project is currently unstable and not recommended for production use.
337: 95: 
338: 96: **Features:**
339: 97: - Uses Godot's GDExtension system
340: 98: - Compiled as native AOT library
341: 99: - Better performance and smaller file size
342: 100: - Requires manual CEF file copying during export
343: 101: - Entry point: `gdcefglue_library_init`
344: 102: 
345: 103: **Structure:**
346: 104: - `GDExtension/Extension/` - C# source code for GDExtension
347: 105: - `GDExtension/Extension/Dll/` - Godot .NET bindings (from [godot-dotnet](https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet))
348: 106: - `GDExtension/Project/` - Godot project for testing
349: 107: 
350: 108: **Build Instructions:**
351: 109: 
352: 110: 1. **Get Godot .NET Bindings:**
353: 111:    ```bash
354: 112:    git clone https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet.git
355: 113:    cd godot-dotnet
356: 114:    dotnet build -p:GenerateGodotBindings=true
357: 115:    ```
358: 116:    Copy the generated `Godot.Bindings.dll` and related files to `GDExtension/Extension/Dll/`.
359: 117: 
360: 118: 2. **Build GDExtension:**
361: 119:    ```bash
362: 120:    cd GDExtension/Extension
363: 121:    dotnet publish -c Debug -r win-x64 --self-contained true
364: 122:    ```
365: 123: 
366: 124: 3. **Deploy:**
367: 125:    Copy all files from `Debug/net10.0/win-x64/publish/` to `GDExtension/Project/lib/`.
368: 126: 
369: 127: 4. **Run:**
370: 128:    Open the project with Godot 4.6 and run.
371: 129: 
372: 130: ### Normal C# Project
373: 131: 
374: 132: Located in `NormalProject/` directory. Traditional Godot C# project approach.
375: 133: 
376: 134: **Features:**
377: 135: - Standard Godot .NET SDK project
378: 136: - Uses `Godot.NET.Sdk`
379: 137: - CEF files are automatically copied when using NuGet packages
380: 138: - May need manual resource copying after export (except NuGet packages)
381: 139: - Source code in `addons/GCefGlue/`
382: 140: 
383: 141: **When to use:**
384: 142: - If you prefer traditional Godot C# development
385: 143: - If GDExtension doesn't meet your needs
386: 144: 
387: 145: Update your `.csproj` to reference the projects:
388: 146: ```xml
389: 147: <ItemGroup>
390: 148:   <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue\CefGlue.csproj" />
391: 149:   <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue.Common\CefGlue.Common.csproj" />
392: 150:   <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue.Common.Shared\CefGlue.Common.Shared.csproj" />
393: 151: </ItemGroup>
394: 152: ```
395: 153: 
396: 154: ### CefGlue Sources
397: 155: 
398: 156: | Source | CEF Version | Status | NuGet | GitHub |
399: 157: |--------|-------------|--------|-------|--------|
400: 158: | youfch/CefGlue | 134 | Unofficial (Recommended) | `CefGlue.Common 134.6998.178` | [GitHub](https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue) |
401: 159: | OutSystems/CefGlue | 120 | Official | `CefGlue.Common 120.6099.0` | [GitHub](https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue) |
402: 160: 
403: 161: ## Build Instructions
404: 162: 
405: 163: ### Using NuGet Package
406: 164: 
407: 165: ```powershell
408: 166: # Restore packages
409: 167: dotnet restore
410: 168: 
411: 169: # Build
412: 170: dotnet build
413: 171: ```
414: 172: 
415: 173: ### Using Source Code
416: 174: 
417: 175: ```powershell
418: 176: # Clone CefGlue (choose one)
419: 177: git clone https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue.git   # CEF 134
420: 178: # or
421: 179: git clone https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue.git  # CEF 120
422: 180: 
423: 181: # Build
424: 182: dotnet restore
425: 183: dotnet build
426: 184: ```
427: 185: 
428: 186: ## Build Output
429: 187: 
430: 188: After a successful build, the following files will be generated:
431: 189: 
432: 190: **Core Files:**
433: 191: - `GDCefGlue.dll` - Main plugin assembly
434: 192: - `Xilium.CefGlue.dll` - CefGlue wrapper
435: 193: - `Xilium.CefGlue.Common.dll` - Common functionality
436: 194: 
437: 195: **CEF Native Files:**
438: 196: - `libcef.dll` - Chromium core library
439: 197: - `chrome_*.pak` - UI resources
440: 198: - `resources.pak` - Application resources
441: 199: - `locales\*.pak` - Language packs
442: 200: 
443: 201: **BrowserProcess Files:**
444: 202: - `CefGlueBrowserProcess\` - Browser subprocess files
445: 203: 
446: 204: ## Export for Distribution
447: 205: 
448: 206: ### Using NuGet Package
449: 207: 
450: 208: When using NuGet packages, all necessary files are automatically copied during build. Just export your Godot project normally.
451: 209: 
452: 210: ### Using Source Code
453: 211: 
454: 212: When using source code dependencies, you need to manually copy files after export. See [CEF_EXPORT_GUIDE.md](CEF_EXPORT_GUIDE.md) for details.
455: 213: 
456: 214: ## CefGlueControl Properties
457: 215: 
458: 216: | Property | Type | Default | Description |
459: 217: |----------|------|---------|-------------|
460: 218: | `InitialUrl` | string | "about:blank" | The URL to load when the browser is created |
461: 219: | `OpenPopupInCurrentBrowser` | bool | true | If true, popup windows navigate in the current browser |
462: 220: | `GpuAcceleration` | bool | true | If true, enables GPU hardware acceleration |
463: 221: | `FrameRate` | int | 60 | Browser frame rate, range 1-360 |
464: 222: | `Transparent` | bool | false | If true, enables transparent background support |
465: 223: 
466: 224: ### Static Properties
467: 225: 
468: 226: | Property | Type | Description |
469: 227: |----------|------|-------------|
470: 228: | `UseGpuAcceleration` | bool | Global GPU acceleration setting, must be set before CEF initialization |
471: 229: | `UseTransparent` | bool | Global transparent background setting, must be set before CEF initialization |
472: 230: 
473: 231: ### Read-only Properties
474: 232: 
475: 233: | Property | Type | Description |
476: 234: |----------|------|-------------|
477: 235: | `Address` | string | Current page URL |
478: 236: | `IsBrowserInitialized` | bool | Whether the browser is initialized |
479: 237: | `IsLoading` | bool | Whether the page is loading |
480: 238: | `Title` | string | Current page title |
481: 239: 
482: 240: ## CefGlueControl Methods
483: 241: 
484: 242: | Method | Description |
485: 243: |--------|-------------|
486: 244: | `GoBack()` | Navigate back in history |
487: 245: | `GoForward()` | Navigate forward in history |
488: 246: | `NavigateToUrl(string url)` | Navigate to the specified URL |
489: 247: | `Reload(bool ignoreCache = false)` | Reload the current page, optionally ignoring cache |
490: 248: | `ExecuteJavaScript(string code, ...)` | Execute JavaScript code |
491: 249: | `EvaluateJavaScript<T>(string code, ...)` | Execute JavaScript and return result |
492: 250: | `ShowDeveloperTools()` | Open developer tools |
493: 251: | `CloseDeveloperTools()` | Close developer tools |
494: 252: 
495: 253: ## GPU Configuration
496: 254: 
497: 255: ```csharp
498: 256: // Enable GPU acceleration (default) - set in inspector
499: 257: // GpuAcceleration = true;
500: 258: 
501: 259: // Disable GPU acceleration (software rendering) - set in inspector
502: 260: // GpuAcceleration = false;
503: 261: ```
504: 262: 
505: 263: **Note:** This property is exposed to the Godot inspector. Set it before the control is initialized.
506: 264: 
507: 265: ## Troubleshooting
508: 266: 
509: 267: ### GPU Process Crashed
510: 268: 
511: 269: 1. Ensure all CEF files are copied correctly
512: 270: 2. Try disabling GPU acceleration in the inspector
513: 271: 
514: 272: ### Missing DLLs
515: 273: 
516: 274: 1. Run `dotnet restore` to restore NuGet packages
517: 275: 2. Clean and rebuild the solution
518: 276: 
519: 277: ### Blank Page
520: 278: 
521: 279: 1. Check if `locales` directory exists
522: 280: 2. Ensure `resources.pak` is present
523: 281: 
524: 282: ## Known Issues
525: 283: 
526: 284: 1. **Right-click Context Menu**: Not supported
527: 285: 2. **Network Notification**: `WSALookupServiceBegin failed with: 10108` is a normal warning
528: 286: 
529: 287: ## License
530: 288: 
531: 289: GDCefGlue is licensed under the MIT License. See [LICENSE](LICENSE) for details.
532: 290: 
533: 291: Third-party dependencies:
534: 292: - [CefGlue](https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue) - BSD-3-Clause
535: 293: - [CEF](https://bitbucket.org/chromiumembedded/cef) - BSD-3-Clause
536: 294: - [Godot Engine](https://godotengine.org) - MIT
537: 295: - [godot-dotnet](https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet) - MIT
538: 
539: (End of file - total 295 lines)

543: # GDCefGlue
544: 
545: A CEF (Chromium Embedded Framework) browser control for Godot 4.x using CefGlue.
546: 
547: [中文文档](README_CN.md)
548: 
549: ## Features
550: 
551: - GPU hardware acceleration support
552: - IME support for Chinese/Japanese/Korean input
553: - Popup handling
554: - Complete keyboard and mouse support
555: - Easy integration with Godot 4.x
556: 
557: ## Performance Demo
558: 
559: ### WebGL Aquarium
560: 
561: Supports WebGL rendering with 20,000 fish at stable 120fps:
562: 
563: ![WebGL Aquarium](img/WebGL水族馆.png)
564: 
565: ## Quick Start
566: 
567: Add a `CefGlueControl` node to your scene as a browser control:
568: 
569: ```csharp
570: var browser = new CefGlueControl();
571: browser.InitialUrl = "https://godotengine.org";
572: AddChild(browser);
573: ```
574: 
575: ## Requirements
576: 
577: - **Godot Engine**: 4.6.0 or later (with .NET/Mono support)
578: - **.NET SDK**: 8.0 or later
579: - **Windows**: x64 architecture
580: 
581: ## Dependency Options
582: 
583: ### Option 1: NuGet Package (Recommended)
584: 
585: The easiest way to use GDCefGlue. All necessary files are automatically copied during build.
586: 
587: **CEF 134 (Recommended):**
588: ```xml
589: <ItemGroup>
590:   <PackageReference Include="CefGlue.Common" Version="134.6998.178" />
591:   <PackageReference Include="chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64" Version="134.3.9" />
592: </ItemGroup>
593: ```
594: 
595: **CEF 120 (Official):**
596: ```xml
597: <ItemGroup>
598:   <PackageReference Include="CefGlue.Common" Version="120.6099.0" />
599:   <PackageReference Include="chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64" Version="120.2.3" />
600: </ItemGroup>
601: ```
602: 
603: ### Option 2: Build from Source
604: 
605: If you need the latest version or want to customize CefGlue:
606: 
607: **CEF 134 (Recommended):**
608: ```bash
609: git clone https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue.git
610: ```
611: 
612: **CEF 120 (Official):**
613: ```bash
614: git clone https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue.git
615: ```
616: 
617: Place the cloned repository in your project directory:
618: ```
619: YourProject/
620: ├── GDCefGlue/
621: │   ├── GDExtension/
622: │   │   ├── Extension/     # C# GDExtension source code
623: │   │   └── Project/       # Godot test project
624: │   ├── NormalProject/     # Normal C# project example
625: │   ├── img/
626: │   └── README*.md
627: └── CefGlue/               # Cloned repository
628: ```
629: 
630: ## Project Types
631: 
632: ### GDExtension Project (Experimental)
633: 
634: Located in `GDExtension/` directory. For Godot 4.6+.
635: 
636: > **Warning:** This project is currently unstable and not recommended for production use.
637: 
638: **Features:**
639: - Uses Godot's GDExtension system
640: - Compiled as native AOT library
641: - Better performance and smaller file size
642: - Requires manual CEF file copying during export
643: - Entry point: `gdcefglue_library_init`
644: 
645: **Structure:**
646: - `GDExtension/Extension/` - C# source code for GDExtension
647: - `GDExtension/Extension/Dll/` - Godot .NET bindings (from [godot-dotnet](https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet))
648: - `GDExtension/Project/` - Godot project for testing
649: 
650: **Build Instructions:**
651: 
652: 1. **Get Godot .NET Bindings:**
653:    ```bash
654:    git clone https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet.git
655:    cd godot-dotnet
656:    dotnet build -p:GenerateGodotBindings=true
657:    ```
658:    Copy the generated `Godot.Bindings.dll` and related files to `GDExtension/Extension/Dll/`.
659: 
660: 2. **Build GDExtension:**
661:    ```bash
662:    cd GDExtension/Extension
663:    dotnet publish -c Debug -r win-x64 --self-contained true
664:    ```
665: 
666: 3. **Deploy:**
667:    Copy all files from `Debug/net10.0/win-x64/publish/` to `GDExtension/Project/lib/`.
668: 
669: 4. **Run:**
670:    Open the project with Godot 4.6 and run.
671: 
672: ### Normal C# Project
673: 
674: Located in `NormalProject/` directory. Traditional Godot C# project approach.
675: 
676: **Features:**
677: - Standard Godot .NET SDK project
678: - Uses `Godot.NET.Sdk`
679: - CEF files are automatically copied when using NuGet packages
680: - May need manual resource copying after export (except NuGet packages)
681: - Source code in `addons/GCefGlue/`
682: 
683: **When to use:**
684: - If you prefer traditional Godot C# development
685: - If GDExtension doesn't meet your needs
686: 
687: Update your `.csproj` to reference the projects:
688: ```xml
689: <ItemGroup>
690:   <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue\CefGlue.csproj" />
691:   <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue.Common\CefGlue.Common.csproj" />
692:   <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue.Common.Shared\CefGlue.Common.Shared.csproj" />
693: </ItemGroup>
694: ```
695: 
696: ### CefGlue Sources
697: 
698: | Source | CEF Version | Status | NuGet | GitHub |
699: |--------|-------------|--------|-------|--------|
700: | youfch/CefGlue | 134 | Unofficial (Recommended) | `CefGlue.Common 134.6998.178` | [GitHub](https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue) |
701: | OutSystems/CefGlue | 120 | Official | `CefGlue.Common 120.6099.0` | [GitHub](https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue) |
702: 
703: ## Build Instructions
704: 
705: ### Using NuGet Package
706: 
707: ```powershell
708: # Restore packages
709: dotnet restore
710: 
711: # Build
712: dotnet build
713: ```
714: 
715: ### Using Source Code
716: 
717: ```powershell
718: # Clone CefGlue (choose one)
719: git clone https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue.git   # CEF 134
720: # or
721: git clone https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue.git  # CEF 120
722: 
723: # Build
724: dotnet restore
725: dotnet build
726: ```
727: 
728: ## Build Output
729: 
730: After a successful build, the following files will be generated:
731: 
732: **Core Files:**
733: - `GDCefGlue.dll` - Main plugin assembly
734: - `Xilium.CefGlue.dll` - CefGlue wrapper
735: - `Xilium.CefGlue.Common.dll` - Common functionality
736: 
737: **CEF Native Files:**
738: - `libcef.dll` - Chromium core library
739: - `chrome_*.pak` - UI resources
740: - `resources.pak` - Application resources
741: - `locales\*.pak` - Language packs
742: 
743: **BrowserProcess Files:**
744: - `CefGlueBrowserProcess\` - Browser subprocess files
745: 
746: ## Export for Distribution
747: 
748: ### Using NuGet Package
749: 
750: When using NuGet packages, all necessary files are automatically copied during build. Just export your Godot project normally.
751: 
752: ### Using Source Code
753: 
754: When using source code dependencies, you need to manually copy files after export. See [CEF_EXPORT_GUIDE.md](CEF_EXPORT_GUIDE.md) for details.
755: 
756: ## CefGlueControl Properties
757: 
758: | Property | Type | Default | Description |
759: |----------|------|---------|-------------|
760: | `InitialUrl` | string | "about:blank" | The URL to load when the browser is created |
761: | `OpenPopupInCurrentBrowser` | bool | true | If true, popup windows navigate in the current browser |
762: | `GpuAcceleration` | bool | true | If true, enables GPU hardware acceleration |
763: | `FrameRate` | int | 60 | Browser frame rate, range 1-360 |
764: | `Transparent` | bool | false | If true, enables transparent background support |
765: 
766: ### Static Properties
767: 
768: | Property | Type | Description |
769: |----------|------|-------------|
770: | `UseGpuAcceleration` | bool | Global GPU acceleration setting, must be set before CEF initialization |
771: | `UseTransparent` | bool | Global transparent background setting, must be set before CEF initialization |
772: 
773: ### Read-only Properties
774: 
775: | Property | Type | Description |
776: |----------|------|-------------|
777: | `Address` | string | Current page URL |
778: | `IsBrowserInitialized` | bool | Whether the browser is initialized |
779: | `IsLoading` | bool | Whether the page is loading |
780: | `Title` | string | Current page title |
781: 
782: ## CefGlueControl Methods
783: 
784: | Method | Description |
785: |--------|-------------|
786: | `GoBack()` | Navigate back in history |
787: | `GoForward()` | Navigate forward in history |
788: | `NavigateToUrl(string url)` | Navigate to the specified URL |
789: | `Reload(bool ignoreCache = false)` | Reload the current page, optionally ignoring cache |
790: | `ExecuteJavaScript(string code, ...)` | Execute JavaScript code |
791: | `EvaluateJavaScript<T>(string code, ...)` | Execute JavaScript and return result |
792: | `ShowDeveloperTools()` | Open developer tools |
793: | `CloseDeveloperTools()` | Close developer tools |
794: 
795: ## GPU Configuration
796: 
797: ```csharp
798: // Enable GPU acceleration (default) - set in inspector
799: // GpuAcceleration = true;
800: 
801: // Disable GPU acceleration (software rendering) - set in inspector
802: // GpuAcceleration = false;
803: ```
804: 
805: **Note:** This property is exposed to the Godot inspector. Set it before the control is initialized.
806: 
807: ## Troubleshooting
808: 
809: ### GPU Process Crashed
810: 
811: 1. Ensure all CEF files are copied correctly
812: 2. Try disabling GPU acceleration in the inspector
813: 
814: ### Missing DLLs
815: 
816: 1. Run `dotnet restore` to restore NuGet packages
817: 2. Clean and rebuild the solution
818: 
819: ### Blank Page
820: 
821: 1. Check if `locales` directory exists
822: 2. Ensure `resources.pak` is present
823: 
824: ## Known Issues
825: 
826: 1. **Right-click Context Menu**: Not supported
827: 2. **Network Notification**: `WSALookupServiceBegin failed with: 10108` is a normal warning
828:

863: 7: ## 功能特性
864: 8: 
865: 9: - GPU 硬件加速支持
866: 10: - 中文/日文/韩文输入法支持
867: 11: - 弹窗处理
868: 12: - 完整的键盘和鼠标支持
869: 13: - 易于集成到 Godot 4.x
870: 14: 
871: 15: ## 性能演示
872: 16:

876: 20: 
877: 21: !\[WebGL水族馆]\(img/WebGL水族馆.png null)
878: 22: 
879: 23: ## 环境要求
880: 24: 
881: 25: - **Godot Engine**: 4.6.0 或更高版本（需要 .NET/Mono 支持）
882: 26: - **.NET SDK**: 8.0 或更高版本
883: 27: - **Windows**: x64 架构
884: 28: 
885: 29: ## 依赖方式
886: 30: 
887: 31: ### 方式一：NuGet 包（推荐）
888: 32: 
889: 33: 最简单的使用方式。构建时自动复制所有必要文件。
890: 34: 
891: 35: **CEF 134（推荐）：**
892: 36: 
893: 37: ```xml
894: 38: <ItemGroup>
895: 39:   <PackageReference Include="CefGlue.Common" Version="134.6998.178" />
896: 40:   <PackageReference Include="chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64" Version="134.3.9" />
897: 41: </ItemGroup>
898: 42: ```
899: 43: 
900: 44: **CEF 120（官方）：**
901: 45: 
902: 46: ```xml
903: 47: <ItemGroup>
904: 48:   <PackageReference Include="CefGlue.Common" Version="120.6099.0" />
905: 49:   <PackageReference Include="chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64" Version="120.2.3" />
906: 50: </ItemGroup>
907: 51: ```
908: 52: 
909: 53: ### 方式二：从源码构建
910: 54: 
911: 55: 如果需要最新版本或自定义 CefGlue：
912: 56: 
913: 57: **CEF 134（推荐）：**
914: 58: 
915: 59: ```bash
916: 60: git clone https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue.git
917: 61: ```
918: 62: 
919: 63: **CEF 120（官方）：**
920: 64: 
921: 65: ```bash
922: 66: git clone https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue.git
923: 67: ```
924: 68: 
925: 69: 将克隆的仓库放置到项目目录：
926: 70: 
927: 71: ```
928: 72: 你的项目/
929: 73: ├── GDCefGlue/
930: 74: │   ├── GDExtension/
931: 75: │   │   ├── Extension/     # C# GDExtension 源代码
932: 76: │   │   └── Project/       # Godot 测试项目
933: 77: │   ├── NormalProject/     # 普通 C# 项目示例
934: 78: │   ├── img/
935: 79: │   └── README*.md
936: 80: └── CefGlue/               # 克隆的仓库
937: 81: ```
938: 82: 
939: 83: ## 项目类型
940: 84: 
941: 85: ### GDExtension 项目
942: 86: 
943: 87: 位于 `GDExtension/` 目录。适用于 Godot 4.6+。
944: 88: 
945: 89: **特点：**
946: 90: 
947: 91: - 使用 Godot 的 GDExtension 系统
948: 92: - 编译为原生 AOT 库
949: 93: - 性能更好，文件体积更小
950: 94: - 导出时需要手动复制 CEF 文件
951: 95: - 入口点：`gdcefglue_library_init`
952: 96: 
953: 97: **结构：**
954: 98: 
955: 99: - `GDExtension/Extension/` - GDExtension 的 C# 源代码
956: 100: - `GDExtension/Extension/Dll/` - Godot .NET 绑定（来自 [godot-dotnet](https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet)）
957: 101: - `GDExtension/Project/` - 用于测试的 Godot 项目
958: 102: 
959: 103: **构建说明：**
960: 104: 
961: 105: 1. **获取 Godot .NET 绑定：**
962: 106: 
963: 107:    不同 Godot 版本需要对应版本的 godot-dotnet：
964: 108:    | Godot 版本 | godot-dotnet 分支 |
965: 109:    | -------- | --------------- |
966: 110:    | 4.6.x    | master 或对应标签    |
967: 111:    | 4.5.x    | 检查对应 release 标签 |
968: 112:    | 4.4.x    | 检查对应 release 标签 |
969: 113:    > **注意：** godot-dotnet 没有发布 release 包，需要查看历史提交，下载对应 Godot 版本的源码后手动编译。
970: 114:    ```bash
971: 115:    git clone https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet.git
972: 116:    cd godot-dotnet
973: 117:    # 切换到对应 Godot 版本的分支/标签（如需要）
974: 118:    # git checkout <godot-version-tag>
975: 119:    dotnet build -p:GenerateGodotBindings=true
976: 120:    ```
977: 121:    将生成的 `Godot.Bindings.dll` 及相关文件复制到 `GDExtension/Extension/Dll/`。
978: 122: 2. **CEF 依赖（跨平台）：**
979: 123:    - **Windows：** 通过 NuGet 包自动获取 `chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64`
980: 124:    - **Linux：** 需要添加 [cef.redist.linux](https://github.com/OutSystems/cef.redist.linux) 依赖
981: 125:    - **macOS：** 需要添加 [cef.redist.osx](https://github.com/OutSystems/cef.redist.osx) 依赖
982: 126:    查看 [CefGlue 仓库](https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue) 了解如何添加跨平台依赖。
983: 127: 3. **构建 GDExtension：**
984: 128: 
985: 129:    进入 `GDExtension/Extension` 目录执行：
986: 130: 
987: 131:    **Windows x64：**
988: 132:    ```bash
989: 133:    # Debug 版本
990: 134:    dotnet publish -c Debug -r win-x64 --self-contained true
991: 135: 
992: 136:    # Release 版本
993: 137:    dotnet publish -c Release -r win-x64 --self-contained true
994: 138:    ```
995: 139:    **Linux x64：**
996: 140:    ```bash
997: 141:    dotnet publish -c Release -r linux-x64 --self-contained true
998: 142:    ```
999: 143:    **macOS x64/ARM64：**
1000: 144:    ```bash
1001: 145:    # Intel Mac
1002: 146:    dotnet publish -c Release -r osx-x64 --self-contained true
1003: 147: 
1004: 148:    # Apple Silicon Mac
1005: 149:    dotnet publish -c Release -r osx-arm64 --self-contained true
1006: 150:    ```
1007: 151: 4. **部署：**
1008: 152: 
1009: 153:    编译输出位于 `bin\Release(Debug)\net9.0\win-x64\publish\`（Windows）或对应平台目录。
1010: 154: 
1011: 155:    将 publish 目录中的所有文件复制到 `GDExtension/Game/lib/` 目录。
1012: 156: 5. **运行：**
1013: 157:    使用 Godot 4.6 打开 `GDExtension/Game/` 项目并运行。
1014: 158: 
1015: 159: **不同 CEF 版本支持：**
1016: 160: 
1017: 161: 如需使用不同版本的 CEF，有两种方式：
1018: 162: 
1019: 163: 1. **NuGet 包方式（推荐）：** 修改 `.csproj` 中的 NuGet 包版本
1020: 164:    ```xml
1021: 165:    <PackageReference Include="CefGlue.Common" Version="xxx.xxxx.x" />
1022: 166:    <PackageReference Include="chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64" Version="xxx.x.x" />
1023: 167:    ```
1024: 168: 2. **手动编译：** 从 [CefGlue 仓库](https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue) 下载对应 CEF 版本的源码，手动编译 NuGet 包后引用。
1025: 169: 
1026: 170: ### 普通 C# 项目
1027: 171: 
1028: 172: 位于 `NormalProject/` 目录。传统的 Godot C# 项目方式。
1029: 173: 
1030: 174: **特点：**
1031: 175: 
1032: 176: - 标准 Godot .NET SDK 项目
1033: 177: - 使用 `Godot.NET.Sdk`
1034: 178: - 使用 NuGet 包时 CEF 文件自动复制
1035: 179: - 导出后可能需要手动复制资源（NuGet 包除外）
1036: 180: - 源代码位于 `addons/GCefGlue/`
1037: 181: 
1038: 182: **适用场景：**
1039: 183: 
1040: 184: - 如果你偏好传统的 Godot C# 开发方式
1041: 185: - 如果 GDExtension 不能满足你的需求
1042: 186: 
1043: 187: 更新 `.csproj` 引用项目：
1044: 188: 
1045: 189: ```xml
1046: 190: <ItemGroup>
1047: 191:   <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue\CefGlue.csproj" />
1048: 192:   <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue.Common\CefGlue.Common.csproj" />
1049: 193:   <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue.Common.Shared\CefGlue.Common.Shared.csproj" />
1050: 194: </ItemGroup>
1051: 195: ```
1052: 196: 
1053: 197: ### CefGlue 来源
1054: 198: 
1055: 199: | 来源                 | CEF 版本 | 状态      | NuGet                         | GitHub                                          |
1056: 200: | ------------------ | ------ | ------- | ----------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------- |
1057: 201: | youfch/CefGlue     | 134    | 非官方（推荐） | `CefGlue.Common 134.6998.178` | [GitHub](https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue)     |
1058: 202: | OutSystems/CefGlue | 120    | 官方      | `CefGlue.Common 120.6099.0`   | [GitHub](https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue) |
1059: 203: 
1060: 204: ## 构建说明
1061: 205: 
1062: 206: ### 使用 NuGet 包
1063: 207: 
1064: 208: ```powershell
1065: 209: # 还原包
1066: 210: dotnet restore
1067: 211: 
1068: 212: # 构建
1069: 213: dotnet build
1070: 214: ```
1071: 215:

1073: 217: 
1074: 218: ```powershell
1075: 219: # 克隆 CefGlue（选择一个）
1076: 220: git clone https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue.git   # CEF 134
1077: 221: # 或
1078: 222: git clone https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue.git  # CEF 120
1079: 223:

1082: 226: dotnet build

1085: 229: ## 构建输出
1086: 230: 
1087: 231: 成功构建后，将生成以下文件：
1088: 232: 
1089: 233: **核心文件：**
1090: 234: 
1091: 235: - `GDCefGlue.dll` - 主插件程序集
1092: 236: - `Xilium.CefGlue.dll` - CefGlue 包装器
1093: 237: - `Xilium.CefGlue.Common.dll` - 通用功能
1094: 238: 
1095: 239: **CEF 原生文件：**
1096: 240: 
1097: 241: - `libcef.dll` - Chromium 核心库
1098: 242: - `chrome_*.pak` - UI 资源
1099: 243: - `resources.pak` - 应用程序资源
1100: 244: - `locales\*.pak` - 语言包
1101: 245: 
1102: 246: **BrowserProcess 文件：**
1103: 247: 
1104: 248: - `CefGlueBrowserProcess\` - 浏览器子进程文件
1105: 249: 
1106: 250: ## 导出分发
1107: 251: 
1108: 252: ### 使用 NuGet 包
1109: 253: 
1110: 254: 使用 NuGet 包时，所有必要文件在构建时自动复制。正常导出 Godot 项目即可。
1111: 255: 
1112: 256: ### 使用源码
1113: 257: 
1114: 258: 使用源码依赖时，导出后需要手动复制文件。详见 [CEF\_EXPORT\_GUIDE.md](CEF_EXPORT_GUIDE.md)。
1115: 259: 
1116: 260: ## CefGlueControl 属性
1117: 261: 
1118: 262: | 属性                          | 类型     | 默认值           | 描述                   |
1119: 263: | --------------------------- | ------ | ------------- | -------------------- |
1120: 264: | `InitialUrl`                | string | "about:blank" | 浏览器创建时加载的 URL        |
1121: 265: | `OpenPopupInCurrentBrowser` | bool   | true          | 如果为 true，弹窗在当前浏览器中导航 |
1122: 266: | `GpuAcceleration`           | bool   | true          | 如果为 true，启用 GPU 硬件加速 |
1123: 267: | `FrameRate`                 | int    | 60            | 浏览器帧率，范围 1-360       |
1124: 268: | `Transparent`               | bool   | false         | 如果为 true，启用透明背景支持    |
1125: 269: 
1126: 270: ### 静态属性
1127: 271: 
1128: 272: | 属性                | 类型   | 描述                          |
1129: 273: | ----------------- | ---- | --------------------------- |
1130: 274: | `UseGpuAcceleration` | bool | 全局 GPU 加速设置，需在 CEF 初始化前设置    |
1131: 275: | `UseTransparent`     | bool | 全局透明背景设置，需在 CEF 初始化前设置       |
1132: 276: 
1133: 277: ### 只读属性
1134: 278: 
1135: 279: | 属性                    | 类型     | 描述             |
1136: 280: | --------------------- | ------ | -------------- |
1137: 281: | `Address`             | string | 当前页面 URL       |
1138: 282: | `IsBrowserInitialized` | bool   | 浏览器是否已初始化      |
1139: 283: | `IsLoading`           | bool   | 页面是否正在加载       |
1140: 284: | `Title`               | string | 当前页面标题         |
1141: 285: 
1142: 286: ## CefGlueControl 方法
1143: 287: 
1144: 288: | 方法                                      | 描述                  |
1145: 289: | --------------------------------------- | ------------------- |
1146: 290: | `GoBack()`                              | 后退                  |
1147: 291: | `GoForward()`                           | 前进                  |
1148: 292: | `NavigateToUrl(string url)`             | 导航到指定 URL           |
1149: 293: | `Reload(bool ignoreCache = false)`      | 刷新当前页面，可选忽略缓存       |
1150: 294: | `ExecuteJavaScript(string code, ...)`   | 执行 JavaScript 代码    |
1151: 295: | `EvaluateJavaScript<T>(string code, ...)` | 执行 JavaScript 并返回结果 |
1152: 296: | `ShowDeveloperTools()`                  | 打开开发者工具             |
1153: 297: | `CloseDeveloperTools()`                 | 关闭开发者工具             |
1154: 298: 
1155: 299: ## GPU 配置
1156: 300: 
1157: 301: ```csharp
1158: 302: // 启用 GPU 加速（默认）- 在检查器中设置
1159: 303: // GpuAcceleration = true;
1160: 304: 
1161: 305: // 禁用 GPU 加速（软件渲染）- 在检查器中设置
1162: 306: // GpuAcceleration = false;
1163: 307: ```
1164: 308: 
1165: 309: **注意：** 此属性已暴露给 Godot 检查器，在控件初始化前设置。
1166: 310: 
1167: 311: ## 故障排除
1168: 312: 
1169: 313: ### GPU 进程崩溃
1170: 314: 
1171: 315: 1. 确保所有 CEF 文件正确复制
1172: 316: 2. 尝试在检查器中禁用 GPU 加速
1173: 317: 
1174: 318: ### 缺少 DLL
1175: 319: 
1176: 320: 1. 运行 `dotnet restore` 还原 NuGet 包
1177: 321: 2. 清理并重新构建解决方案
1178: 322: 
1179: 323: ### 空白页面
1180: 324: 
1181: 325: 1. 检查 `locales` 目录是否存在
1182: 326: 2. 确保 `resources.pak` 存在
1183: 327: 
1184: 328: ## 已知问题
1185: 329: 
1186: 330: 1. **右键菜单**：暂不支持
1187: 331: 2. **网络通知**：`WSALookupServiceBegin failed with: 10108` 是正常警告
1188: 332:

1420: 
1421: ## GDCefGlue 项目概览
1422: 
1423: ### 一句话定位
1424: **GDCefGlue** 是一个将 Chromium 浏览器嵌入 **Godot 4.x 游戏引擎**的 C# 插件，基于 CefGlue（CEF 的 .NET 绑定）。
1425: 
1426: ### 核心能力
1427: - 在 Godot 场景中嵌入完整的 Chromium 浏览器 (`CefGlueControl` 节点)
1428: - GPU 硬件加速，WebGL 渲染（2万条鱼 120fps）
1429: - 中文/日文/韩文输入法（IME）支持
1430: - 弹窗处理、完整键鼠交互
1431: - 支持透明背景、JS 执行、开发者工具
1432: 
1433: ### 技术栈
1434: | 层级 | 技术 |
1435: |------|------|
1436: | 引擎 | Godot 4.6+ (.NET/Mono) |
1437: | 运行时 | .NET 8.0/9.0 |
1438: | 浏览器内核 | Chromium (CEF 120 或 134) |
1439: | C# 绑定 | CefGlue (Xilium) |
1440: | 平台 | Windows x64（Linux/macOS 实验性支持） |
1441: 
1442: ### 项目结构
1443: 
1444: ```
1445: GDCefGlue/
1446: ├── NormalProject/          ← 传统 Godot C# 项目（推荐/稳定）
1447: │   ├── addons/GCefGlue/    # 10 个 C# 插件源文件
1448: │   │   ├── CefGlueControl.cs        # 浏览器控件主体
1449: │   │   ├── GodotCefClient.cs        # CEF 客户端
1450: │   │   ├── GodotRenderHandler.cs    # 渲染处理器
1451: │   │   ├── GodotLoadHandler.cs      # 加载处理器
1452: │   │   ├── GodotDisplayHandler.cs   # 显示处理器
1453: │   │   ├── GodotLifeSpanHandler.cs  # 生命周期处理器
1454: │   │   ├── GodotBrowserProcessHandler.cs
1455: │   │   ├── CefInitializer.cs        # CEF 初始化
1456: │   │   └── ...
1457: │   └── MainUi.cs
1458: ├── GDExtension/            ← 实验性 GDExtension 项目
1459: │   ├── Extension/          # AOT 编译的 GDExtension 源码
1460: │   └── Game/               # Godot 测试项目（GDScript）
1461: ├── src/
1462: │   └── GDCefGlue.Extension/  # 共享扩展代码（bin/obj 有文件，暂无 .cs）
1463: ├── demos/                  # 示例项目
1464: ├── conversations/          # 对话记录
1465: ├── img/                    # 截图
1466: └── Nuget/                  # NuGet 相关
1467: ```
1468: 
1469: ### 两种集成方式
1470: 
1471: | 特性 | NormalProject (推荐) | GDExtension (实验性) |
1472: |------|---------------------|---------------------|
1473: | 项目类型 | 标准 Godot .NET SDK | 原生 AOT 库 |
1474: | 稳定性 | 生产可用 | 不稳定 |
1475: | 性能 | 正常 | 更好，体积更小 |
1476: | 部署 | NuGet 自动复制 | 需手动复制 CEF 文件 |
1477: | 入口 | 标准 C# 类 | `gdcefglue_library_init` |
1478: 
1479: ### CefGlueControl 主要 API
1480: 
1481: ```csharp
1482: // 属性
1483: InitialUrl, GpuAcceleration, FrameRate, Transparent
1484: // 导航
1485: GoBack(), GoForward(), NavigateToUrl(url), Reload()
1486: // JS 交互
1487: ExecuteJavaScript(code), EvaluateJavaScript<T>(code)
1488: // 调试
1489: ShowDeveloperTools(), CloseDeveloperTools()
1490: ```
1491: 
1492: ### 已知局限
1493: - 不支持右键菜单
1494: - GDExtension 模式不稳定
1495: - 目前主要支持 Windows x64
1496:

<type>file</type>
<content>
1: # CEF Export File Copy Guide (Source Code Dependencies)
2: 
3: This guide is only for **source code dependencies**. If you are using NuGet packages, all necessary files are automatically copied during build and you don't need this guide.
4: 
5: ## Overview
6: 
7: When using source code dependencies (cloned CefGlue repository), Godot's export process does NOT automatically copy CEF-related files. You must manually copy files after export.
8: 
9: ## Export Directory
10: 
11: Default export directory: `<ExportDir>\data_GDCefGlue_windows_x86_64\`
12: 
13: ---
14: 
15: ## Files to Copy
16: 
17: ### 1. BrowserProcess Files (Required!)
18: 
19: **Source Directory:**
20: ```
21: CefGlue\CefGlue.BrowserProcess\bin\Debug\net8.0\win-x64\publish\
22: ```
23: 
24: **Target:** `<ExportDir>\data_GDCefGlue_windows_x86_64\`
25: 
26: **Files:**
27: - `Xilium.CefGlue.BrowserProcess.exe`
28: - `Xilium.CefGlue.BrowserProcess.dll`
29: - `Xilium.CefGlue.BrowserProcess.runtimeconfig.json`
30: - `Xilium.CefGlue.BrowserProcess.deps.json`
31: - `Xilium.CefGlue.dll`
32: - `Xilium.CefGlue.Common.Shared.dll`
33: 
34: ---
35: 
36: ### 2. CEF Native Files
37: 
38: **Source Directory:**
39: ```
40: CefGlue\packages\chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64\<version>\runtimes\win-x64\native\
41: ```
42: 
43: **Target:** Export directory root
44: 
45: **Files:**
46: - `chrome_100_percent.pak`
47: - `chrome_200_percent.pak`
48: - `chrome_elf.dll`
49: - `d3dcompiler_47.dll`
50: - `dxcompiler.dll`
51: - `dxil.dll`
52: - `icudtl.dat`
53: - `libEGL.dll`
54: - `libGLESv2.dll`
55: - `libcef.dll`
56: - `resources.pak`
57: - `v8_context_snapshot.bin`
58: - `vk_swiftshader.dll`
59: - `vk_swiftshader_icd.json`
60: - `vulkan-1.dll`
61: 
62: ---
63: 
64: ### 3. Locales Files
65: 
66: **Source Directory:**
67: ```
68: CefGlue\packages\chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64\<version>\CEF\win-x64\locales\
69: ```
70: 
71: **Target:** `Export directory\locales\`
72: 
73: **Files:** All `.pak` files
74: 
75: ---
76: 
77: ## Copy Commands
78: 
79: ### PowerShell Commands
80: 
81: ```powershell
82: # Set paths (using relative paths)
83: $projectRoot = "."  # Current directory, or specify project root
84: $exportDir = "<ExportDir>\data_GDCefGlue_windows_x86_64"  # Export data directory path
85: $browserProcessSrc = "$projectRoot\CefGlue\CefGlue.BrowserProcess\bin\Debug\net8.0\win-x64\publish"
86: $cefSrc = "$projectRoot\CefGlue\packages\chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64\<version>\runtimes\win-x64\native"
87: $localesSrc = "$projectRoot\CefGlue\packages\chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64\<version>\CEF\win-x64\locales"
88: 
89: # 1. Copy BrowserProcess files
90: Copy-Item "$browserProcessSrc\Xilium.CefGlue.BrowserProcess.exe" $exportDir -Force
91: Copy-Item "$browserProcessSrc\Xilium.CefGlue.BrowserProcess.dll" $exportDir -Force
92: Copy-Item "$browserProcessSrc\Xilium.CefGlue.BrowserProcess.runtimeconfig.json" $exportDir -Force
93: Copy-Item "$browserProcessSrc\Xilium.CefGlue.BrowserProcess.deps.json" $exportDir -Force
94: Copy-Item "$browserProcessSrc\Xilium.CefGlue.dll" $exportDir -Force
95: Copy-Item "$browserProcessSrc\Xilium.CefGlue.Common.Shared.dll" $exportDir -Force
96: 
97: # 2. Copy CEF native files
98: Copy-Item "$cefSrc\*.*" $exportDir -Force
99: 
100: # 3. Copy locales
101: $localesDest = "$exportDir\locales"
102: if (!(Test-Path $localesDest)) { New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $localesDest -Force }
103: Copy-Item "$localesSrc\*.pak" $localesDest -Force
104: 
105: Write-Host "Copy complete!"
106: ```
107: 
108: ### CMD Commands
109: 
110: ```batch
111: @echo off
112: set PROJECT_ROOT=.
113: set EXPORT_DIR=<ExportDir>\data_GDCefGlue_windows_x86_64
114: set BROWSER_PROCESS=%PROJECT_ROOT%\CefGlue\CefGlue.BrowserProcess\bin\Debug\net8.0\win-x64\publish
115: set CEF_SRC=%PROJECT_ROOT%\CefGlue\packages\chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64\<version>\runtimes\win-x64\native
116: set LOCALES_SRC=%PROJECT_ROOT%\CefGlue\packages\chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64\<version>\CEF\win-x64\locales
117: 
118: echo Copying BrowserProcess files...
119: copy /Y "%BROWSER_PROCESS%\Xilium.CefGlue.BrowserProcess.exe" "%EXPORT_DIR%\"
120: copy /Y "%BROWSER_PROCESS%\Xilium.CefGlue.BrowserProcess.dll" "%EXPORT_DIR%\"
121: copy /Y "%BROWSER_PROCESS%\Xilium.CefGlue.BrowserProcess.runtimeconfig.json" "%EXPORT_DIR%\"
122: copy /Y "%BROWSER_PROCESS%\Xilium.CefGlue.BrowserProcess.deps.json" "%EXPORT_DIR%\"
123: copy /Y "%BROWSER_PROCESS%\Xilium.CefGlue.dll" "%EXPORT_DIR%\"
124: copy /Y "%BROWSER_PROCESS%\Xilium.CefGlue.Common.Shared.dll" "%EXPORT_DIR%\"
125: 
126: echo Copying CEF native files...
127: copy /Y "%CEF_SRC%\*.*" "%EXPORT_DIR%\"
128: 
129: echo Copying locales...
130: if not exist "%EXPORT_DIR%\locales" mkdir "%EXPORT_DIR%\locales"
131: copy /Y "%LOCALES_SRC%\*.pak" "%EXPORT_DIR%\locales\"
132: 
133: echo Copy complete!
134: ```
135: 
136: **Note:** Replace `<version>` with the actual CEF version number (e.g., `134.3.9`).
137: 
138: ---
139: 
140: ## Export Directory Structure
141: 
142: ```
143: <ExportDir>\
144: ├── Game.exe                           # Godot main executable
145: └── data_GDCefGlue_windows_x86_64\     # Data directory
146:     ├── locales\                       # CEF language packs
147:     │   ├── zh-CN.pak
148:     │   └── ...
149:     ├── CefGlueBrowserProcess\         # Browser subprocess (self-contained)
150:     │   ├── Xilium.CefGlue.BrowserProcess.exe
151:     │   ├── Xilium.CefGlue.BrowserProcess.dll
152:     │   ├── coreclr.dll               # .NET runtime
153:     │   └── ... (all .NET runtime files)
154:     ├── GDCefGlue.dll                 # Plugin assembly
155:     ├── Xilium.CefGlue.BrowserProcess.exe  # Also in root
156:     ├── Xilium.CefGlue.BrowserProcess.dll  # Also in root
157:     ├── Xilium.CefGlue.dll
158:     ├── Xilium.CefGlue.Common.dll
159:     ├── Xilium.CefGlue.Common.Shared.dll
160:     ├── libcef.dll                    # CEF core
161:     ├── chrome_100_percent.pak
162:     ├── chrome_200_percent.pak
163:     ├── resources.pak
164:     └── ... (other CEF files)
165: ```
166: 
167: ---
168: 
169: ## Common Issues
170: 
171: ### Q: Export crashes with "GPU process isn't usable"
172: 
173: **A:** Ensure `Xilium.CefGlue.BrowserProcess.dll` is copied to the export directory. This is the most common issue.
174: 
175: ### Q: Export crashes with "Network service crashed"
176: 
177: **A:** Same as above, ensure all BrowserProcess files are copied.
178: 
179: ### Q: Page shows blank or cannot load
180: 
181: **A:** Check if `locales` directory exists and `resources.pak` file is copied.

(End of file - total 181 lines)
</content>
```

## Critical Structural Problems

1. **9 C# files are 100% duplicated** between `NormalProject/addons/GCefGlue/` and `GDExtension/Extension/`. Any fix must be applied twice.

2. **`src/` is dead weight** — it appears to be where shared code was *intended* to go but it only contains build artifacts (bin/obj/), no source. The name `GDCefGlue.Extension` suggests it was meant for the GDExtension variant.

3. **No solution-level organization** — the single `.sln` only covers NormalProject. GDExtension builds independently via `dotnet publish`.

4. **`demos/` is empty** — a placeholder that should either be populated or removed.

5. **Build outputs leak into source tree** — top-level `bin/` and `obj/` are from building the Extension project.

6. **GDExtension/Game/ is a Godot project** but GDExtension/Extension/ is a C# project — the directory mixes engine project and C# source concerns.

7. **CI manually copies .uid files** from NormalProject for the release artifact, indicating these UID bindings are release-critical.

8. **Two different .NET TFMs** — NormalProject: net8.0, GDExtension: net10.0. They also have different CEF version configurations (Win 145 vs Unix 134).

</answer>

The `Main.cs` (GDExtension entry) and `MainUi.cs` (NormalProject entry) remain unique to each mode. [Project README: E:\Work\Hub\GDCefGlue\README.md]
# GDCefGlue

A CEF (Chromium Embedded Framework) browser control for Godot 4.x using CefGlue.

[中文文档](README_CN.md)

## Features

- GPU hardware acceleration support
- IME support for Chinese/Japanese/Korean input
- Popup handling
- Complete keyboard and mouse support
- Easy integration with Godot 4.x

## Performance Demo

### WebGL Aquarium

Supports WebGL rendering with 20,000 fish at stable 120fps:

![WebGL Aquarium](img/WebGL水族馆.png)

## Quick Start

Add a `CefGlueControl` node to your scene as a browser control:

```csharp
var browser = new CefGlueControl();
browser.InitialUrl = "https://godotengine.org";
AddChild(browser);
```

## Requirements

- **Godot Engine**: 4.6.0 or later (with .NET/Mono support)
- **.NET SDK**: 8.0 or later
- **Windows**: x64 architecture

## Dependency Options

### Option 1: NuGet Package (Recommended)

The easiest way to use GDCefGlue. All necessary files are automatically copied during build.

**CEF 134 (Recommended):**
```xml
<ItemGroup>
  <PackageReference Include="CefGlue.Common" Version="134.6998.178" />
  <PackageReference Include="chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64" Version="134.3.9" />
</ItemGroup>
```

**CEF 120 (Official):**
```xml
<ItemGroup>
  <PackageReference Include="CefGlue.Common" Version="120.6099.0" />
  <PackageReference Include="chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64" Version="120.2.3" />
</ItemGroup>
```

### Option 2: Build from Source

If you need the latest version or want to customize CefGlue:

**CEF 134 (Recommended):**
```bash
git clone https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue.git
```

**CEF 120 (Official):**
```bash
git clone https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue.git
```

Place the cloned repository in your project directory:
```
YourProject/
├── GDCefGlue/
│   ├── GDExtension/
│   │   ├── Extension/     # C# GDExtension source code
│   │   └── Project/       # Godot test project
│   ├── NormalProject/     # Normal C# project example
│   ├── img/
│   └── README*.md
└── CefGlue/               # Cloned repository
```

## Project Types

### GDExtension Project (Experimental)

Located in `GDExtension/` directory. For Godot 4.6+.

> **Warning:** This project is currently unstable and not recommended for production use.

**Features:**
- Uses Godot's GDExtension system
- Compiled as native AOT library
- Better performance and smaller file size
- Requires manual CEF file copying during export
- Entry point: `gdcefglue_library_init`

**Structure:**
- `GDExtension/Extension/` - C# source code for GDExtension
- `GDExtension/Extension/Dll/` - Godot .NET bindings (from [godot-dotnet](https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet))
- `GDExtension/Project/` - Godot project for testing

**Build Instructions:**

1. **Get Godot .NET Bindings:**
   ```bash
   git clone https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet.git
   cd godot-dotnet
   dotnet build -p:GenerateGodotBindings=true
   ```
   Copy the generated `Godot.Bindings.dll` and related files to `GDExtension/Extension/Dll/`.

2. **Build GDExtension:**
   ```bash
   cd GDExtension/Extension
   dotnet publish -c Debug -r win-x64 --self-contained true
   ```

3. **Deploy:**
   Copy all files from `Debug/net10.0/win-x64/publish/` to `GDExtension/Project/lib/`.

4. **Run:**
   Open the project with Godot 4.6 and run.

### Normal C# Project

Located in `NormalProject/` directory. Traditional Godot C# project approach.

**Features:**
- Standard Godot .NET SDK project
- Uses `Godot.NET.Sdk`
- CEF files are automatically copied when using NuGet packages
- May need manual resource copying after export (except NuGet packages)
- Source code in `addons/GCefGlue/`

**When to use:**
- If you prefer traditional Godot C# development
- If GDExtension doesn't meet your needs

Update your `.csproj` to reference the projects:
```xml
<ItemGroup>
  <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue\CefGlue.csproj" />
  <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue.Common\CefGlue.Common.csproj" />
  <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue.Common.Shared\CefGlue.Common.Shared.csproj" />
</ItemGroup>
```

### CefGlue Sources

| Source | CEF Version | Status | NuGet | GitHub |
|--------|-------------|--------|-------|--------|
| youfch/CefGlue | 134 | Unofficial (Recommended) | `CefGlue.Common 134.6998.178` | [GitHub](https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue) |
| OutSystems/CefGlue | 120 | Official | `CefGlue.Common 120.6099.0` | [GitHub](https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue) |

## Build Instructions

### Using NuGet Package

```powershell
# Restore packages
dotnet restore

# Build
dotnet build
```

### Using Source Code

```powershell
# Clone CefGlue (choose one)
git clone https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue.git   # CEF 134
# or
git clone https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue.git  # CEF 120

# Build
dotnet restore
dotnet build
```

## Build Output

After a successful build, the following files will be generated:

**Core Files:**
- `GDCefGlue.dll` - Main plugin assembly
- `Xilium.CefGlue.dll` - CefGlue wrapper
- `Xilium.CefGlue.Common.dll` - Common functionality

**CEF Native Files:**
- `libcef.dll` - Chromium core library
- `chrome_*.pak` - UI resources
- `resources.pak` - Application resources
- `locales\*.pak` - Language packs

**BrowserProcess Files:**
- `CefGlueBrowserProcess\` - Browser subprocess files

## Export for Distribution

### Using NuGet Package

When using NuGet packages, all necessary files are automatically copied during build. Just export your Godot project normally.

### Using Source Code

When using source code dependencies, you need to manually copy files after export. See [CEF_EXPORT_GUIDE.md](CEF_EXPORT_GUIDE.md) for details.

## CefGlueControl Properties

| Property | Type | Default | Description |
|----------|------|---------|-------------|
| `InitialUrl` | string | "about:blank" | The URL to load when the browser is created |
| `OpenPopupInCurrentBrowser` | bool | true | If true, popup windows navigate in the current browser |
| `GpuAcceleration` | bool | true | If true, enables GPU hardware acceleration |
| `FrameRate` | int | 60 | Browser frame rate, range 1-360 |
| `Transparent` | bool | false | If true, enables transparent background support |

### Static Properties

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `UseGpuAcceleration` | bool | Global GPU acceleration setting, must be set before CEF initialization |
| `UseTransparent` | bool | Global transparent background setting, must be set before CEF initialization |

### Read-only Properties

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `Address` | string | Current page URL |
| `IsBrowserInitialized` | bool | Whether the browser is initialized |
| `IsLoading` | bool | Whether the page is loading |
| `Title` | string | Current page title |

## CefGlueControl Methods

| Method | Description |
|--------|-------------|
| `GoBack()` | Navigate back in history |
| `GoForward()` | Navigate forward in history |
| `NavigateToUrl(string url)` | Navigate to the specified URL |
| `Reload(bool ignoreCache = false)` | Reload the current page, optionally ignoring cache |
| `ExecuteJavaScript(string code, ...)` | Execute JavaScript code |
| `EvaluateJavaScript<T>(string code, ...)` | Execute JavaScript and return result |
| `ShowDeveloperTools()` | Open developer tools |
| `CloseDeveloperTools()` | Close developer tools |

## GPU Configuration

```csharp
// Enable GPU acceleration (default) - set in inspector
// GpuAcceleration = true;

// Disable GPU acceleration (software rendering) - set in inspector
// GpuAcceleration = false;
```

**Note:** This property is exposed to the Godot inspector. Set it before the control is initialized.

## Troubleshooting

### GPU Process Crashed

1. Ensure all CEF files are copied correctly
2. Try disabling GPU acceleration in the inspector

### Missing DLLs

1. Run `dotnet restore` to restore NuGet packages
2. Clean and rebuild the solution

### Blank Page

1. Check if `locales` directory exists
2. Ensure `resources.pak` is present

## Known Issues

1. **Right-click Context Menu**: Not supported
2. **Network Notification**: `WSALookupServiceBegin failed with: 10108` is a normal warning

## License

GDCefGlue is licensed under the MIT License. See [LICENSE](LICENSE) for details.

Third-party dependencies:
- [CefGlue](https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue) - BSD-3-Clause
- [CEF](https://bitbucket.org/chromiumembedded/cef) - BSD-3-Clause
- [Godot Engine](https://godotengine.org) - MIT
- [godot-dotnet](https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet) - MIT

```

1. **Merge into shared `src/`** (recommended): Create `src/GDCefGlue.Shared/` for the truly common code (GodotCefClient.cs is already 95% identical). Use `#if NORMALPROJECT` / `#if GDEXTENSION` compilation symbols to handle the divergent parts (namespace, Godot API access, internal fields). Both csproj files would add `<Compile Include="../src/GDCefGlue.Shared/**/*.cs" Link="..." />`.

2. **Keep separated but extract common NuGet**: Move shared handler logic (CefClient, handlers, initializer core) into a `GDCefGlue.Common` NuGet package that both projects reference. This avoids the `#if` complexity but adds package publishing overhead.

3. **Status quo with sync discipline**: Keep the current structure but establish a convention — when changing one codebase, check the other. This is fragile but requires zero restructuring.

The `src/GDCefGlue.Extension/` directory is yours to repurpose — it's dead and untracked. Clear it and use it for the shared code.

[Project README: E:\Work\Hub\GDCefGlue\README.md]
# GDCefGlue

A CEF (Chromium Embedded Framework) browser control for Godot 4.x using CefGlue.

[中文文档](README_CN.md)

## Features

- GPU hardware acceleration support
- IME support for Chinese/Japanese/Korean input
- Popup handling
- Complete keyboard and mouse support
- Easy integration with Godot 4.x

## Performance Demo

### WebGL Aquarium

Supports WebGL rendering with 20,000 fish at stable 120fps:

![WebGL Aquarium](img/WebGL水族馆.png)

## Quick Start

Add a `CefGlueControl` node to your scene as a browser control:

```csharp
var browser = new CefGlueControl();
browser.InitialUrl = "https://godotengine.org";
AddChild(browser);
```

## Requirements

- **Godot Engine**: 4.6.0 or later (with .NET/Mono support)
- **.NET SDK**: 8.0 or later
- **Windows**: x64 architecture

## Dependency Options

### Option 1: NuGet Package (Recommended)

The easiest way to use GDCefGlue. All necessary files are automatically copied during build.

**CEF 134 (Recommended):**
```xml
<ItemGroup>
  <PackageReference Include="CefGlue.Common" Version="134.6998.178" />
  <PackageReference Include="chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64" Version="134.3.9" />
</ItemGroup>
```

**CEF 120 (Official):**
```xml
<ItemGroup>
  <PackageReference Include="CefGlue.Common" Version="120.6099.0" />
  <PackageReference Include="chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64" Version="120.2.3" />
</ItemGroup>
```

### Option 2: Build from Source

If you need the latest version or want to customize CefGlue:

**CEF 134 (Recommended):**
```bash
git clone https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue.git
```

**CEF 120 (Official):**
```bash
git clone https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue.git
```

Place the cloned repository in your project directory:
```
YourProject/
├── GDCefGlue/
│   ├── extension/              # C# GDExtension source code
│   ├── plugin/                 # Normal C# plugin example
│   ├── test/
│   │   └── GDExtensionGame/    # Godot test project for GDExtension
│   ├── img/
│   └── README*.md
└── CefGlue/                    # Cloned repository
```

## Project Types

### GDExtension Project (Experimental)

Located in `extension/` directory. For Godot 4.6+.

> **Warning:** This project is currently unstable and not recommended for production use.

**Features:**
- Uses Godot's GDExtension system
- Compiled as native AOT library
- Better performance and smaller file size
- Requires manual CEF file copying during export
- Entry point: `gdcefglue_library_init`

**Structure:**
- `extension/` - C# source code for GDExtension
- `extension/Dll/` - Godot .NET bindings (from [godot-dotnet](https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet))
- `test/GDExtensionGame/` - Godot project for testing

**Build Instructions:**

1. **Get Godot .NET Bindings:**
   ```bash
   git clone https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet.git
   cd godot-dotnet
   dotnet build -p:GenerateGodotBindings=true
   ```
   Copy the generated `Godot.Bindings.dll` and related files to `extension/Dll/`.

2. **Build GDExtension:**
   ```bash
   cd extension
   dotnet publish -c Debug -r win-x64 --self-contained true
   ```

3. **Deploy:**
   Copy all files from `Debug/net10.0/win-x64/publish/` to `test/GDExtensionGame/lib/`.

4. **Run:**
   Open the project with Godot 4.6 and run.

### Normal C# Project

Located in `plugin/` directory. Traditional Godot C# project approach.

**Features:**
- Standard Godot .NET SDK project
- Uses `Godot.NET.Sdk`
- CEF files are automatically copied when using NuGet packages
- May need manual resource copying after export (except NuGet packages)
- Source code in `addons/GCefGlue/`

**When to use:**
- If you prefer traditional Godot C# development
- If GDExtension doesn't meet your needs

Update your `.csproj` to reference the projects:
```xml
<ItemGroup>
  <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue\CefGlue.csproj" />
  <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue.Common\CefGlue.Common.csproj" />
  <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue.Common.Shared\CefGlue.Common.Shared.csproj" />
</ItemGroup>
```

### CefGlue Sources

| Source | CEF Version | Status | NuGet | GitHub |
|--------|-------------|--------|-------|--------|
| youfch/CefGlue | 134 | Unofficial (Recommended) | `CefGlue.Common 134.6998.178` | [GitHub](https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue) |
| OutSystems/CefGlue | 120 | Official | `CefGlue.Common 120.6099.0` | [GitHub](https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue) |

## Build Instructions

### Using NuGet Package

```powershell
# Restore packages
dotnet restore

# Build
dotnet build
```

### Using Source Code

```powershell
# Clone CefGlue (choose one)
git clone https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue.git   # CEF 134
# or
git clone https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue.git  # CEF 120

# Build
dotnet restore
dotnet build
```

## Build Output

After a successful build, the following files will be generated:

**Core Files:**
- `GDCefGlue.dll` - Main plugin assembly
- `Xilium.CefGlue.dll` - CefGlue wrapper
- `Xilium.CefGlue.Common.dll` - Common functionality

**CEF Native Files:**
- `libcef.dll` - Chromium core library
- `chrome_*.pak` - UI resources
- `resources.pak` - Application resources
- `locales\*.pak` - Language packs

**BrowserProcess Files:**
- `CefGlueBrowserProcess\` - Browser subprocess files

## Export for Distribution

### Using NuGet Package

When using NuGet packages, all necessary files are automatically copied during build. Just export your Godot project normally.

### Using Source Code

When using source code dependencies, you need to manually copy files after export. See [CEF_EXPORT_GUIDE.md](CEF_EXPORT_GUIDE.md) for details.

## CefGlueControl Properties

| Property | Type | Default | Description |
|----------|------|---------|-------------|
| `InitialUrl` | string | "about:blank" | The URL to load when the browser is created |
| `OpenPopupInCurrentBrowser` | bool | true | If true, popup windows navigate in the current browser |
| `GpuAcceleration` | bool | true | If true, enables GPU hardware acceleration |
| `FrameRate` | int | 60 | Browser frame rate, range 1-360 |
| `Transparent` | bool | false | If true, enables transparent background support |

### Static Properties

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `UseGpuAcceleration` | bool | Global GPU acceleration setting, must be set before CEF initialization |
| `UseTransparent` | bool | Global transparent background setting, must be set before CEF initialization |

### Read-only Properties

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `Address` | string | Current page URL |
| `IsBrowserInitialized` | bool | Whether the browser is initialized |
| `IsLoading` | bool | Whether the page is loading |
| `Title` | string | Current page title |

## CefGlueControl Methods

| Method | Description |
|--------|-------------|
| `GoBack()` | Navigate back in history |
| `GoForward()` | Navigate forward in history |
| `NavigateToUrl(string url)` | Navigate to the specified URL |
| `Reload(bool ignoreCache = false)` | Reload the current page, optionally ignoring cache |
| `ExecuteJavaScript(string code, ...)` | Execute JavaScript code |
| `EvaluateJavaScript<T>(string code, ...)` | Execute JavaScript and return result |
| `ShowDeveloperTools()` | Open developer tools |
| `CloseDeveloperTools()` | Close developer tools |

## GPU Configuration

```csharp
// Enable GPU acceleration (default) - set in inspector
// GpuAcceleration = true;

// Disable GPU acceleration (software rendering) - set in inspector
// GpuAcceleration = false;
```

**Note:** This property is exposed to the Godot inspector. Set it before the control is initialized.

## Troubleshooting

### GPU Process Crashed

1. Ensure all CEF files are copied correctly
2. Try disabling GPU acceleration in the inspector

### Missing DLLs

1. Run `dotnet restore` to restore NuGet packages
2. Clean and rebuild the solution

### Blank Page

1. Check if `locales` directory exists
2. Ensure `resources.pak` is present

## Known Issues

1. **Right-click Context Menu**: Not supported
2. **Network Notification**: `WSALookupServiceBegin failed with: 10108` is a normal warning

## License

GDCefGlue is licensed under the MIT License. See [LICENSE](LICENSE) for details.

Third-party dependencies:
- [CefGlue](https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue) - BSD-3-Clause
- [CEF](https://bitbucket.org/chromiumembedded/cef) - BSD-3-Clause
- [Godot Engine](https://godotengine.org) - MIT
- [godot-dotnet](https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet) - MIT

115:         _texture = ImageTexture.CreateFromImage(_image);
116: 
117:         CallDeferred("_create_browser_deferred");
118:     }
119:     
120:     private void ActivateIme()
121:     {
122:         var window = GetWindow();
123:         if (window != null && HasFocus())
124:         {
125:             window.SetImeActive(true);
126:         }
127:     }
128:     
129:     private void DeactivateIme()
130:     {
131:         var window = GetWindow();
132:         if (window != null)
133:         {
134:             window.SetImeActive(false);
135:         }
136:     }

138:     private void CreateBrowserDeferred()
139:     {
140:         if (_browserCreated)
141:             return;
142: 
143:         var size = Size;
144:         if (size.X > 0 && size.Y > 0)
145:         {
146:             _browserCreated = true;
147:             CreateBrowser((int)size.X, (int)size.Y);
148:         }
149:     }
150: 
151:     private void CreateBrowser(int width, int height)
152:     {
153:         _width = width;
154:         _height = height;
155:         _controlWidth = width;
156:         _controlHeight = height;
157: 
158:         var frameRate = Math.Clamp(FrameRate, 1, 360);
159:         GD.Print($"CefGlueControl: Creating browser {width}x{height} @ {frameRate}fps (Transparent: {Transparent})");
160: 
161:         var windowInfo = CefWindowInfo.Create();
162:         windowInfo.SetAsWindowless(IntPtr.Zero, Transparent);
163: 
164:         var settings = new CefBrowserSettings
165:         {
166:             WindowlessFrameRate = frameRate
167:         };
168: 
169:         _client = new GodotCefClient(this);
170: 
171:         try
172:         {
173:             CefBrowserHost.CreateBrowser(windowInfo, _client, settings, InitialUrl);
174:             GD.Print($"CefGlueControl: Browser creation initiated for {InitialUrl}");
175:         }
176:         catch (Exception ex)
177:         {
178:             GD.PrintErr($"CefGlueControl: Failed to create browser - {ex.Message}");
179:         }
180:     }
181:

187:             return;
188:         }
189:         
190:         _browser = browser;
191:         _browserHost = browser.GetHost();
192:         CallDeferred("_notify_browser_initialized");
193:     }
