254: 
255:     internal void OnPaint(IntPtr buffer, int width, int height, CefRectangle[] dirtyRects)
256:     {
257:         if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) return;
258:         
259:         int bufferSize = width * height * 4;
260:         
261:         lock (_bufferLock)
262:         {
263:             _width = width;
264:             _height = height;
265:             
266:             if (_pixelBuffer == null || _pixelBuffer.Length != bufferSize)
267:             {
268:                 _pixelBuffer = new byte[bufferSize];
269:             }
270: 
271:             Marshal.Copy(buffer, _pixelBuffer, 0, bufferSize);
272:             ConvertBgraToRgba(_pixelBuffer, width * height);
273:             _isDirty = true;
274:         }
275:     }
276: 
277:     [MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.AggressiveInlining)]
278:     private static unsafe void ConvertBgraToRgba(byte[] buffer, int pixelCount)
279:     {
280:         if (Ssse3.IsSupported)
281:         {
282:             int vectorSize = 16;
283:             int vectorCount = pixelCount / 4;
284:             
285:             var shuffleMask = Vector128.Create((byte)2, 1, 0, 3, 6, 5, 4, 7, 10, 9, 8, 11, 14, 13, 12, 15);
286:             
287:             fixed (byte* ptr = buffer)
288:             {
289:                 for (int i = 0; i < vectorCount; i++)
290:                 {
291:                     int offset = i * vectorSize;
292:                     var data = Sse2.LoadVector128(ptr + offset);
293:                     var shuffled = Ssse3.Shuffle(data, shuffleMask);
294:                     Sse2.Store(ptr + offset, shuffled);
295:                 }
296:                 
297:                 for (int i = vectorCount * 4; i < pixelCount; i++)
298:                 {
299:                     int offset = i * 4;
300:                     byte b = ptr[offset];
301:                     ptr[offset] = ptr[offset + 2];
302:                     ptr[offset + 2] = b;
303:                 }
304:             }
305:         }
306:         else
307:         {
308:             for (int i = 0; i < pixelCount; i++)
309:             {
310:                 int offset = i * 4;
311:                 byte b = buffer[offset];
312:                 buffer[offset] = buffer[offset + 2];
313:                 buffer[offset + 2] = b;
314:             }
315:         }
316:     }
317: 
318:     /// <summary>
319:     /// Called every frame. Updates the texture with new pixel data and handles browser creation.
320:     /// Implements a delayed resize mechanism to prevent flickering during rapid window resizing.
321:     /// </summary>
322:     protected override void _Process(double delta)
323:     {
324:         _cachedGlobalPosition = GlobalPosition;
325: 
326:         if (_browserHost != null && Size.X > 0 && Size.Y > 0)
327:         {
328:             int newWidth = (int)Size.X;
329:             int newHeight = (int)Size.Y;
330:             
331:             if (newWidth != _controlWidth || newHeight != _controlHeight)
332:             {
333:                 _controlWidth = newWidth;
334:                 _controlHeight = newHeight;
335:                 _pendingWidth = newWidth;
336:                 _pendingHeight = newHeight;
337:                 _resizeStableCount = 0;
338:                 QueueRedraw();
339:             }
340:             else if (_pendingWidth > 0 && _pendingHeight > 0)
341:             {
342:                 _resizeStableCount++;
343:                 if (_resizeStableCount >= ResizeStableThreshold)
344:                 {
345:                     _browserHost.WasResized();
346:                     _browserHost.Invalidate(CefPaintElementType.View);
347:                     _pendingWidth = 0;
348:                     _pendingHeight = 0;
349:                 }
350:             }
351:             else if (_width != _controlWidth || _height != _controlHeight)
352:             {
353:                 _browserHost.Invalidate(CefPaintElementType.View);
354:             }
355:         }
356: 
357:         if (_isDirty && _pixelBuffer != null && _width > 0 && _height > 0)
358:         {
359:             int expectedBufferSize = _width * _height * 4;
360:             if (_pixelBuffer.Length == expectedBufferSize)
361:             {
362:                 if (_packedBuffer == null || _packedBuffer.Count != expectedBufferSize)
363:                 {
364:                     _packedBuffer = new PackedByteArray();
365:                     _packedBuffer.Resize(expectedBufferSize);
366:                 }
367:                 
368:                 lock (_bufferLock)
369:                 {
370:                     _packedBuffer.Clear();
371:                     _packedBuffer.AddRange(_pixelBuffer);
372:                 }
373:                 
374:                 if (_texture.GetSize().X != _width || _texture.GetSize().Y != _height)
375:                 {
376:                     _image.SetData(_width, _height, false, Image.Format.Rgba8, _packedBuffer);
377:                     _texture = ImageTexture.CreateFromImage(_image);
378:                 }
379:                 else
380:                 {
381:                     _image.SetData(_width, _height, false, Image.Format.Rgba8, _packedBuffer);
382:                     _texture.Update(_image);
383:                 }
384:                 QueueRedraw();
385:             }
386:             _isDirty = false;
387:         }
388: 
389:         if (!_browserCreated && Size.X > 0 && Size.Y > 0)
390:         {
391:             CreateBrowserDeferred();
392:         }
393:     }
394: 
395:     /// <summary>
396:     /// Called when the control needs to be redrawn. Draws the browser texture.
397:     /// Uses control size for drawing to ensure proper scaling during resize operations.
398:     /// </summary>
399:     protected override void _Draw()
400:     {
401:         if (_texture != null && _controlWidth > 0 && _controlHeight > 0)
402:         {
403:             if (Transparent)
404:             {
405:                 DrawTextureRect(_texture, new Rect2(Vector2.Zero, _controlWidth, _controlHeight), false);
406:             }
407:             else
408:             {
409:                 if (_width == _controlWidth && _height == _controlHeight)
410:                 {
411:                     DrawTexture(_texture, Vector2.Zero);
412:                 }
413:                 else
414:                 {
415:                     DrawTextureRect(_texture, new Rect2(Vector2.Zero, _controlWidth, _controlHeight), false);
416:                 }
417:             }
418:         }
419:     }
420: 
421:     protected override void _GuiInput(InputEvent @event)
422:     {
423:         if (_browserHost == null)
424:             return;
425: 
426:         switch (@event)
427:         {
428:             case InputEventMouseMotion mouseMotion:
429:                 SendMouseMoveEvent(mouseMotion);
430:                 break;
431:             case InputEventMouseButton mouseButton:
432:                 SendMouseButtonEvent(mouseButton);
433:                 break;
434:             case InputEventKey key:
435:                 SendKeyEvent(key);
436:                 break;
437:         }
438:     }
439: 
440:     private void SendMouseMoveEvent(InputEventMouseMotion e)
441:     {
442:         if (_browserHost == null) return;
443:         
444:         var localPos = GetLocalMousePosition();
445:         var modifiers = GetModifiers(e);
446:         
447:         if (_isMousePressed && _pressedButton != (CefMouseButtonType)(-1))
448:         {
449:             modifiers |= GetMouseButtonModifier(_pressedButton);
450:         }
451:         
452:         var mouseEvent = new CefMouseEvent
453:         {
454:             X = (int)localPos.X,
455:             Y = (int)localPos.Y,
456:             Modifiers = modifiers
457:         };
458:         
459:         _browserHost.SendMouseMoveEvent(mouseEvent, false);
460:     }
461:     
462:     private CefEventFlags GetMouseButtonModifier(CefMouseButtonType button)
463:     {
464:         return button switch
465:         {
466:             CefMouseButtonType.Left => CefEventFlags.LeftMouseButton,
467:             CefMouseButtonType.Right => CefEventFlags.RightMouseButton,
468:             CefMouseButtonType.Middle => CefEventFlags.MiddleMouseButton,
469:             _ => CefEventFlags.None
470:         };
471:     }
472: 
473:     private void SendMouseButtonEvent(InputEventMouseButton e)
474:     {
475:         if (_browserHost == null) return;
476:         
477:         var localPos = GetLocalMousePosition();
478:         var mouseEvent = new CefMouseEvent
479:         {
480:             X = (int)localPos.X,
481:             Y = (int)localPos.Y,
482:             Modifiers = GetModifiers(e)
483:         };
484: 
485:         var button = ConvertMouseButton(e.ButtonIndex);
486: 
487:         if (e.ButtonIndex == MouseButton.WheelUp || e.ButtonIndex == MouseButton.WheelDown)
488:         {
489:             int delta = e.ButtonIndex == MouseButton.WheelUp ? 120 : -120;
490:             _browserHost.SendMouseWheelEvent(mouseEvent, 0, delta);
491:             return;
492:         }
493: 
494:         if (button == (CefMouseButtonType)(-1))
495:             return;
496: 
497:         if (e.Pressed)
498:         {
499:             var currentTime = Godot.Time.Singleton.GetTicksMsec() / 1000.0;
500:             if (currentTime - _lastClickTime < DoubleClickInterval)
501:             {
502:                 _clickCount++;
503:             }
504:             else
505:             {
506:                 _clickCount = 1;
507:             }
508:             _lastClickTime = currentTime;
509:             
510:             _pressedButton = button;
511:             _isMousePressed = true;
512:             _browserHost.SendMouseClickEvent(mouseEvent, button, false, _clickCount);
513:             GrabFocus();
514:             ActivateIme();
515:         }
516:         else
517:         {
518:             _isMousePressed = false;
519:             _pressedButton = (CefMouseButtonType)(-1);
520:             _browserHost.SendMouseClickEvent(mouseEvent, button, true, 1);
521:         }
522:     }
523: 
524:     private void SendKeyEvent(InputEventKey e)
525:     {
526:         var windowsKeyCode = GetWindowsKeyCode(e.Keycode);
527:         
528:         var keyEvent = new CefKeyEvent
529:         {
530:             EventType = e.Pressed ? CefKeyEventType.KeyDown : CefKeyEventType.KeyUp,
531:             Modifiers = GetModifiers(e),
532:             WindowsKeyCode = windowsKeyCode,
533:             NativeKeyCode = (int)e.PhysicalKeycode,
534:             IsSystemKey = false
535:         };
536: 
537:         _browserHost.SendKeyEvent(keyEvent);
538: 
539:         if (e.Pressed && e.Unicode.Value != 0 && !IsSpecialKey(e.Keycode))
540:         {
541:             var charEvent = new CefKeyEvent
542:             {
543:                 EventType = CefKeyEventType.Char,
544:                 WindowsKeyCode = e.Unicode.Value,
545:                 NativeKeyCode = e.Unicode.Value,
546:                 Modifiers = GetModifiers(e),
547:                 Character = (char)e.Unicode.Value
548:             };
549:             _browserHost.SendKeyEvent(charEvent);
550:         }
551:     }
552:     
553:     private int GetWindowsKeyCode(Key keycode)
554:     {
555:         return keycode switch
556:         {
557:             Key.Backspace => 0x08,
558:             Key.Tab => 0x09,
559:             Key.Enter => 0x0D,
560:             Key.Shift => 0x10,
561:             Key.Ctrl => 0x11,
562:             Key.Alt => 0x12,
563:             Key.Pause => 0x13,
564:             Key.Capslock => 0x14,
565:             Key.Escape => 0x1B,
566:             Key.Space => 0x20,
567:             Key.Pageup => 0x21,
568:             Key.Pagedown => 0x22,
569:             Key.End => 0x23,
570:             Key.Home => 0x24,
571:             Key.Left => 0x25,
572:             Key.Up => 0x26,
573:             Key.Right => 0x27,
574:             Key.Down => 0x28,
575:             Key.Insert => 0x2D,
576:             Key.Delete => 0x2E,
577:             Key.F1 => 0x70,
578:             Key.F2 => 0x71,
579:             Key.F3 => 0x72,
580:             Key.F4 => 0x73,
581:             Key.F5 => 0x74,
582:             Key.F6 => 0x75,
583:             Key.F7 => 0x76,
584:             Key.F8 => 0x77,
585:             Key.F9 => 0x78,
586:             Key.F10 => 0x79,
587:             Key.F11 => 0x7A,
588:             Key.F12 => 0x7B,
589:             Key.Numlock => 0x90,
590:             Key.Scrolllock => 0x91,
591:             _ => (int)keycode
592:         };
593:     }
594:     
595:     private bool IsSpecialKey(Key keycode)
596:     {
597:         return keycode switch
598:         {
599:             Key.Backspace => true,
600:             Key.Tab => true,
601:             Key.Enter => true,
602:             Key.Escape => true,
603:             Key.Delete => true,
604:             Key.Insert => true,
605:             Key.Home => true,
606:             Key.End => true,
607:             Key.Pageup => true,
608:             Key.Pagedown => true,
609:             Key.Left => true,
610:             Key.Right => true,
611:             Key.Up => true,
612:             Key.Down => true,
613:             Key.F1 => true,
614:             Key.F2 => true,
615:             Key.F3 => true,
616:             Key.F4 => true,
617:             Key.F5 => true,
618:             Key.F6 => true,
619:             Key.F7 => true,
620:             Key.F8 => true,
621:             Key.F9 => true,
622:             Key.F10 => true,
623:             Key.F11 => true,
624:             Key.F12 => true,
625:             _ => false
626:         };
627:     }
628: 
629:     private CefEventFlags GetModifiers(InputEventWithModifiers e)
630:     {
631:         var modifiers = CefEventFlags.None;
632:         if (e.ShiftPressed) modifiers |= CefEventFlags.ShiftDown;
633:         if (e.CtrlPressed) modifiers |= CefEventFlags.ControlDown;
634:         if (e.AltPressed) modifiers |= CefEventFlags.AltDown;
635:         if (e.MetaPressed) modifiers |= CefEventFlags.AltGrDown;
636:         return modifiers;
637:     }
638: 
639:     private CefMouseButtonType ConvertMouseButton(MouseButton button)
640:     {
641:         return button switch
642:         {
643:             MouseButton.Left => CefMouseButtonType.Left,
644:             MouseButton.Right => CefMouseButtonType.Right,
645:             MouseButton.Middle => CefMouseButtonType.Middle,
646:             _ => (CefMouseButtonType)(-1)
647:         };
648:     }
649: 
650:     /// <summary>
651:     /// Handles notifications from Godot such as resize, mouse exit, and focus changes.
652:     /// Note: Resize handling is done in _Process for smoother resizing experience.
653:     /// </summary>
654:     protected override void _Notification(int what)
655:     {
656:         switch ((long)what)
657:         {
658:             case NotificationResized:
659:                 break;
660: 
661:             case NotificationMouseExit:
662:                 if (_browserHost != null)
663:                 {
664:                     var mouseEvent = new CefMouseEvent { X = 0, Y = 0, Modifiers = CefEventFlags.None };
665:                     _browserHost.SendMouseMoveEvent(mouseEvent, true);
666:                     _isMousePressed = false;
667:                     _pressedButton = (CefMouseButtonType)(-1);
668:                 }
669:                 break;
670: 
671:             case NotificationFocusEnter:
672:                 _isFocused = true;
673:                 _browserHost?.SetFocus(true);
674:                 ActivateIme();
675:                 break;
676: 
677:             case NotificationFocusExit:
678:                 _isFocused = false;
679:                 _browserHost?.SetFocus(false);
680:                 DeactivateIme();
681:                 break;
682:         }
683:     }

750:     internal static void BindMembers(ClassRegistrationContext context)
751:     {
752:         context.BindConstructor(() => new CefGlueControl());
753: 
754:         context.BindProperty(
755:             new PropertyInfo(new StringName(nameof(InitialUrl)), VariantType.String)
756:             {
757:                 Usage = PropertyUsageFlags.Default
758:             },
759:             static (CefGlueControl instance) => instance.InitialUrl,
760:             static (CefGlueControl instance, string value) => instance.InitialUrl = value);
761: 
762:         context.BindProperty(
763:             new PropertyInfo(new StringName(nameof(OpenPopupInCurrentBrowser)), VariantType.Bool)
764:             {
765:                 Usage = PropertyUsageFlags.Default
766:             },
767:             static (CefGlueControl instance) => instance.OpenPopupInCurrentBrowser,
768:             static (CefGlueControl instance, bool value) => instance.OpenPopupInCurrentBrowser = value);
769: 
770:         context.BindProperty(
771:             new PropertyInfo(new StringName(nameof(GpuAcceleration)), VariantType.Bool)
772:             {
773:                 Usage = PropertyUsageFlags.Default
774:             },
775:             static (CefGlueControl instance) => instance.GpuAcceleration,
776:             static (CefGlueControl instance, bool value) => instance.GpuAcceleration = value);
777: 
778:         context.BindProperty(
779:             new PropertyInfo(new StringName(nameof(FrameRate)), VariantType.Int)
780:             {
781:                 Usage = PropertyUsageFlags.Default
782:             },
783:             static (CefGlueControl instance) => instance.FrameRate,
784:             static (CefGlueControl instance, int value) => instance.FrameRate = value);
785: 
786:         context.BindProperty(
787:             new PropertyInfo(new StringName(nameof(Transparent)), VariantType.Bool)
788:             {
789:                 Usage = PropertyUsageFlags.Default
790:             },
791:             static (CefGlueControl instance) => instance.Transparent,
792:             static (CefGlueControl instance, bool value) => instance.Transparent = value);
793: 
794:         context.BindMethod(new StringName(nameof(GoBack)),
795:             static (CefGlueControl instance) =>
796:             {
797:                 instance.GoBack();
798:             });
799: 
800:         context.BindMethod(new StringName(nameof(GoForward)),
801:             static (CefGlueControl instance) =>
802:             {
803:                 instance.GoForward();
804:             });
805: 
806:         context.BindMethod(new StringName(nameof(NavigateToUrl)),
807:             new ParameterInfo(new StringName("url"), VariantType.String),
808:             static (CefGlueControl instance, string url) =>
809:             {
810:                 instance.NavigateToUrl(url);
811:             });
812: 
813:         context.BindMethod(new StringName(nameof(Reload)),
814:             new ParameterInfo(new StringName("ignoreCache"), VariantType.Bool, VariantTypeMetadata.None, Variant.CreateFrom(false)),
815:             static (CefGlueControl instance, bool ignoreCache) =>
816:             {
817:                 instance.Reload(ignoreCache);
818:             });
819: 
820:         context.BindMethod(new StringName(nameof(ExecuteJavaScript)),

357:         /// <summary>
358:         /// Called when CEF renders a new frame. Copies pixel data and converts BGRA to RGBA.
359:         /// Uses double buffering to prevent color flickering during rendering.
360:         /// Optimized with ArrayPool for reduced GC pressure.
361:         /// </summary>
362:         /// <param name="buffer">Pointer to the pixel buffer in BGRA format.</param>
363:         /// <param name="width">Width of the rendered frame.</param>
364:         /// <param name="height">Height of the rendered frame.</param>
365:         /// <param name="dirtyRects">Array of dirty rectangles that need repainting.</param>
366:         internal void OnPaint(IntPtr buffer, int width, int height, CefRectangle[] dirtyRects)
367:         {
368:             if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) return;
369:             
370:             int bufferSize = width * height * 4;
371:             
372:             bool lockTaken = false;
373:             try
374:             {
375:                 _spinLock.Enter(ref lockTaken);
376:                 
377:                 _width = width;
378:                 _height = height;
379:                 
380:                 if (_pixelBuffer == null || _pixelBufferSize != bufferSize)
381:                 {
382:                     if (_pixelBuffer != null && _pixelBufferSize > 0)
383:                     {
384:                         ArrayPool<byte>.Shared.Return(_pixelBuffer);
385:                     }
386:                     _pixelBuffer = ArrayPool<byte>.Shared.Rent(bufferSize);
387:                     _pixelBufferSize = bufferSize;
388:                 }
389: 
390:                 Marshal.Copy(buffer, _pixelBuffer, 0, bufferSize);
391:                 ConvertBgraToRgba(_pixelBuffer, width * height);
392:                 _isDirty = true;
393:             }
394:             finally
395:             {
396:                 if (lockTaken) _spinLock.Exit();
397:             }
398:         }
399: 
400:         /// <summary>
401:         /// Converts BGRA pixel data to RGBA format using SIMD when available.
402:         /// Supports AVX2 (8 pixels at once) and SSSE3 (4 pixels at once).
403:         /// </summary>
404:         [MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.AggressiveInlining)]
405:         private static unsafe void ConvertBgraToRgba(byte[] buffer, int pixelCount)
406:         {
407:             if (Avx2.IsSupported)
408:             {
409:                 int vectorSize = 32;
410:                 int vectorCount = pixelCount / 8;
411:                 
412:                 var shuffleMask = Vector256.Create(
413:                     (byte)2, 1, 0, 3, 6, 5, 4, 7, 10, 9, 8, 11, 14, 13, 12, 15,
414:                     (byte)2, 1, 0, 3, 6, 5, 4, 7, 10, 9, 8, 11, 14, 13, 12, 15
415:                 );
416:                 
417:                 fixed (byte* ptr = buffer)
418:                 {
419:                     for (int i = 0; i < vectorCount; i++)
420:                     {
421:                         int offset = i * vectorSize;
422:                         var data = Avx.LoadVector256(ptr + offset);
423:                         var shuffled = Avx2.Shuffle(data, shuffleMask);
424:                         Avx.Store(ptr + offset, shuffled);
425:                     }
426:                     
427:                     for (int i = vectorCount * 8; i < pixelCount; i++)
428:                     {
429:                         int offset = i * 4;
430:                         byte b = ptr[offset];
431:                         ptr[offset] = ptr[offset + 2];
432:                         ptr[offset + 2] = b;
433:                     }
434:                 }
435:             }
436:             else if (Ssse3.IsSupported)
437:             {
438:                 int vectorSize = 16;
439:                 int vectorCount = pixelCount / 4;
440:                 
441:                 var shuffleMask = Vector128.Create((byte)2, 1, 0, 3, 6, 5, 4, 7, 10, 9, 8, 11, 14, 13, 12, 15);
442:                 
443:                 fixed (byte* ptr = buffer)
444:                 {
445:                     for (int i = 0; i < vectorCount; i++)
446:                     {
447:                         int offset = i * vectorSize;
448:                         var data = Sse2.LoadVector128(ptr + offset);
449:                         var shuffled = Ssse3.Shuffle(data, shuffleMask);
450:                         Sse2.Store(ptr + offset, shuffled);
451:                     }
452:                     
453:                     for (int i = vectorCount * 4; i < pixelCount; i++)
454:                     {
455:                         int offset = i * 4;
456:                         byte b = ptr[offset];
457:                         ptr[offset] = ptr[offset + 2];
458:                         ptr[offset + 2] = b;
459:                     }
460:                 }
461:             }
462:             else
463:             {
464:                 for (int i = 0; i < pixelCount; i++)
465:                 {
466:                     int offset = i * 4;
467:                     byte b = buffer[offset];
468:                     buffer[offset] = buffer[offset + 2];
469:                     buffer[offset + 2] = b;
470:                 }
471:             }
472:         }
473: 
474:         /// <summary>
475:         /// Called every frame. Updates the texture with new pixel data and handles browser creation.
476:         /// Uses double buffering with ArrayPool for reduced GC pressure.
477:         /// Implements a delayed resize mechanism to prevent flickering during rapid window resizing.
478:         /// </summary>
479:         public override void _Process(double delta)
480:         {
481:             _cachedGlobalPosition = GlobalPosition;
482: 
483:             if (_browserHost != null && Size.X > 0 && Size.Y > 0)
484:             {
485:                 int newWidth = (int)Size.X;
486:                 int newHeight = (int)Size.Y;
487:                 
488:                 if (newWidth != _controlWidth || newHeight != _controlHeight)
489:                 {
490:                     _controlWidth = newWidth;
491:                     _controlHeight = newHeight;
492:                     _pendingWidth = newWidth;
493:                     _pendingHeight = newHeight;
494:                     _resizeStableCount = 0;
495:                     QueueRedraw();
496:                 }
497:                 else if (_pendingWidth > 0 && _pendingHeight > 0)
498:                 {
499:                     _resizeStableCount++;
500:                     if (_resizeStableCount >= ResizeStableThreshold)
501:                     {
502:                         _browserHost.WasResized();
503:                         _browserHost.Invalidate(CefPaintElementType.View);
504:                         _pendingWidth = 0;
505:                         _pendingHeight = 0;
506:                     }
507:                 }
508:                 else if (_width != _controlWidth || _height != _controlHeight)
509:                 {
510:                     _browserHost.Invalidate(CefPaintElementType.View);
511:                 }
512:             }
513: 
514:             if (_isDirty && _pixelBuffer != null && _width > 0 && _height > 0)
515:             {
516:                 int expectedBufferSize = _width * _height * 4;
517:                 if (_pixelBufferSize >= expectedBufferSize)
518:                 {
519:                     if (_renderBuffer == null || _renderBufferSize != expectedBufferSize)
520:                     {
521:                         _renderBuffer = new byte[expectedBufferSize];
522:                         _renderBufferSize = expectedBufferSize;
523:                     }
524:                     
525:                     bool lockTaken = false;
526:                     try
527:                     {
528:                         _spinLock.Enter(ref lockTaken);
529:                         Buffer.BlockCopy(_pixelBuffer, 0, _renderBuffer, 0, expectedBufferSize);
530:                     }
531:                     finally
532:                     {
533:                         if (lockTaken) _spinLock.Exit();
534:                     }
535:                     
536:                     if (_texture.GetSize().X != _width || _texture.GetSize().Y != _height)
537:                     {
538:                         _image.SetData(_width, _height, false, Image.Format.Rgba8, _renderBuffer);
539:                         _texture = ImageTexture.CreateFromImage(_image);
540:                     }
541:                     else
542:                     {
543:                         _image.SetData(_width, _height, false, Image.Format.Rgba8, _renderBuffer);
544:                         _texture.Update(_image);
545:                     }
546:                     QueueRedraw();
547:                 }
548:                 _isDirty = false;
549:             }
550: 
551:             if (!_browserCreated && Size.X > 0 && Size.Y > 0)
552:             {
553:                 CreateBrowserDeferred();
554:             }
555:         }
556: 
557:         /// <summary>
558:         /// Called when the control needs to be redrawn. Draws the browser texture.
559:         /// Uses control size for drawing to ensure proper scaling during resize operations.
560:         /// </summary>
561:         public override void _Draw()
562:         {
563:             if (_texture != null && _controlWidth > 0 && _controlHeight > 0)
564:             {
565:                 if (Transparent)
566:                 {
567:                     DrawTextureRect(_texture, new Rect2(Vector2.Zero, _controlWidth, _controlHeight), false, Colors.White, false);
568:                 }
569:                 else
570:                 {
571:                     if (_width == _controlWidth && _height == _controlHeight)
572:                     {
573:                         DrawTexture(_texture, Vector2.Zero);
574:                     }
575:                     else
576:                     {
577:                         DrawTextureRect(_texture, new Rect2(Vector2.Zero, _controlWidth, _controlHeight), false);
578:                     }
579:                 }
580:             }
581:         }
582: 
583:         /// <summary>
584:         /// Handles input events from Godot and forwards them to the browser.
585:         /// </summary>
586:         public override void _GuiInput(InputEvent @event)
587:         {
588:             if (_browserHost == null)
589:                 return;
590: 
591:             switch (@event)
592:             {
593:                 case InputEventMouseMotion mouseMotion:
594:                     SendMouseMoveEvent(mouseMotion);
595:                     break;
596:                 case InputEventMouseButton mouseButton:
597:                     SendMouseButtonEvent(mouseButton);
598:                     break;
599:                 case InputEventKey key:
600:                     SendKeyEvent(key);
601:                     break;
602:             }
603:         }
604: 
605:         /// <summary>
606:         /// Sends a mouse move event to the browser.
607:         /// </summary>
608:         private void SendMouseMoveEvent(InputEventMouseMotion e)
609:         {
610:             if (_browserHost == null) return;
611:             
612:             var localPos = GetLocalMousePosition();
613:             var modifiers = GetModifiers(e);
614:             
615:             if (_isMousePressed && _pressedButton != (CefMouseButtonType)(-1))
616:             {
617:                 modifiers |= GetMouseButtonModifier(_pressedButton);
618:             }
619:             
620:             var mouseEvent = new CefMouseEvent
621:             {
622:                 X = (int)localPos.X,
623:                 Y = (int)localPos.Y,
624:                 Modifiers = modifiers
625:             };
626:             
627:             _browserHost.SendMouseMoveEvent(mouseEvent, false);
628:         }
629:         
630:         /// <summary>
631:         /// Gets the CEF event flags for a mouse button.
632:         /// </summary>
633:         private CefEventFlags GetMouseButtonModifier(CefMouseButtonType button)
634:         {
635:             return button switch
636:             {
637:                 CefMouseButtonType.Left => CefEventFlags.LeftMouseButton,
638:                 CefMouseButtonType.Right => CefEventFlags.RightMouseButton,
639:                 CefMouseButtonType.Middle => CefEventFlags.MiddleMouseButton,
640:                 _ => CefEventFlags.None
641:             };
642:         }
643: 
644:         /// <summary>
645:         /// Sends a mouse button event to the browser, including click counting for double-click detection.
646:         /// </summary>
647:         private void SendMouseButtonEvent(InputEventMouseButton e)
648:         {
649:             if (_browserHost == null) return;
650:             
651:             var localPos = GetLocalMousePosition();
652:             var mouseEvent = new CefMouseEvent
653:             {
654:                 X = (int)localPos.X,
655:                 Y = (int)localPos.Y,
656:                 Modifiers = GetModifiers(e)
657:             };
658: 
659:             var button = ConvertMouseButton(e.ButtonIndex);
660: 
661:             if (e.ButtonIndex == MouseButton.WheelUp || e.ButtonIndex == MouseButton.WheelDown)
662:             {
663:                 int delta = e.ButtonIndex == MouseButton.WheelUp ? 120 : -120;
664:                 _browserHost.SendMouseWheelEvent(mouseEvent, 0, delta);
665:                 return;
666:             }
667: 
668:             if (button == (CefMouseButtonType)(-1))
669:                 return;
670: 
671:             if (e.Pressed)
672:             {
673:                 var currentTime = Time.GetTicksMsec() / 1000.0;
674:                 if (currentTime - _lastClickTime < DoubleClickInterval)
675:                 {
676:                     _clickCount++;
677:                 }
678:                 else
679:                 {
680:                     _clickCount = 1;
681:                 }
682:                 _lastClickTime = currentTime;
683:                 
684:                 _pressedButton = button;
685:                 _isMousePressed = true;
686:                 _browserHost.SendMouseClickEvent(mouseEvent, button, false, _clickCount);
687:                 GrabFocus();
688:                 ActivateIme();
689:             }
690:             else
691:             {
692:                 _isMousePressed = false;
693:                 _pressedButton = (CefMouseButtonType)(-1);
694:                 _browserHost.SendMouseClickEvent(mouseEvent, button, true, 1);
695:             }
696:         }
697: 
698:         /// <summary>
699:         /// Sends a keyboard event to the browser, including character input for text.
700:         /// </summary>
701:         private void SendKeyEvent(InputEventKey e)
702:         {
703:             var windowsKeyCode = GetWindowsKeyCode(e.Keycode);
704:             
705:             var keyEvent = new CefKeyEvent
706:             {
707:                 EventType = e.Pressed ? CefKeyEventType.KeyDown : CefKeyEventType.KeyUp,
708:                 Modifiers = GetModifiers(e),
709:                 WindowsKeyCode = windowsKeyCode,
710:                 NativeKeyCode = (int)e.PhysicalKeycode,
711:                 IsSystemKey = false
712:             };
713: 
714:             _browserHost.SendKeyEvent(keyEvent);
715: 
716:             if (e.Pressed && e.Unicode != 0 && !IsSpecialKey(e.Keycode))
717:             {
718:                 var charEvent = new CefKeyEvent
719:                 {
720:                     EventType = CefKeyEventType.Char,
721:                     WindowsKeyCode = (int)e.Unicode,
722:                     NativeKeyCode = (int)e.Unicode,
723:                     Modifiers = GetModifiers(e),
724:                     Character = (char)e.Unicode
725:                 };
726:                 _browserHost.SendKeyEvent(charEvent);
727:             }
728:         }
729:         
730:         /// <summary>
731:         /// Converts a Godot Key to a Windows virtual key code.
732:         /// </summary>
733:         private int GetWindowsKeyCode(Key keycode)
734:         {
735:             return keycode switch
736:             {
737:                 Key.Backspace => 0x08,
738:                 Key.Tab => 0x09,
739:                 Key.Enter => 0x0D,
740:                 Key.Shift => 0x10,
741:                 Key.Ctrl => 0x11,
742:                 Key.Alt => 0x12,
743:                 Key.Pause => 0x13,
744:                 Key.Capslock => 0x14,
745:                 Key.Escape => 0x1B,
746:                 Key.Space => 0x20,
747:                 Key.Pageup => 0x21,
748:                 Key.Pagedown => 0x22,
749:                 Key.End => 0x23,
750:                 Key.Home => 0x24,
751:                 Key.Left => 0x25,
752:                 Key.Up => 0x26,
753:                 Key.Right => 0x27,
754:                 Key.Down => 0x28,
755:                 Key.Insert => 0x2D,
756:                 Key.Delete => 0x2E,
757:                 Key.F1 => 0x70,
758:                 Key.F2 => 0x71,
759:                 Key.F3 => 0x72,
760:                 Key.F4 => 0x73,
761:                 Key.F5 => 0x74,
762:                 Key.F6 => 0x75,
763:                 Key.F7 => 0x76,
764:                 Key.F8 => 0x77,
765:                 Key.F9 => 0x78,
766:                 Key.F10 => 0x79,
767:                 Key.F11 => 0x7A,
768:                 Key.F12 => 0x7B,
769:                 Key.Numlock => 0x90,
770:                 Key.Scrolllock => 0x91,
771:                 _ => (int)keycode
772:             };
773:         }
774:         
775:         /// <summary>
776:         /// Determines if a key is a special key that should not generate character input.
777:         /// </summary>
778:         private bool IsSpecialKey(Key keycode)
779:         {
780:             return keycode switch
781:             {
782:                 Key.Backspace => true,
783:                 Key.Tab => true,
784:                 Key.Enter => true,
785:                 Key.Escape => true,
786:                 Key.Delete => true,
787:                 Key.Insert => true,
788:                 Key.Home => true,
789:                 Key.End => true,
790:                 Key.Pageup => true,
791:                 Key.Pagedown => true,
792:                 Key.Left => true,
793:                 Key.Right => true,
794:                 Key.Up => true,
795:                 Key.Down => true,
796:                 Key.F1 => true,
797:                 Key.F2 => true,
798:                 Key.F3 => true,
799:                 Key.F4 => true,
800:                 Key.F5 => true,
801:                 Key.F6 => true,
802:                 Key.F7 => true,
803:                 Key.F8 => true,
804:                 Key.F9 => true,
805:                 Key.F10 => true,
806:                 Key.F11 => true,
807:                 Key.F12 => true,
808:                 _ => false
809:             };
810:         }
811: 
812:         /// <summary>
813:         /// Converts Godot modifier keys to CEF event flags.
814:         /// </summary>
815:         private CefEventFlags GetModifiers(InputEventWithModifiers e)
816:         {
817:             var modifiers = CefEventFlags.None;
818:             if (e.ShiftPressed) modifiers |= CefEventFlags.ShiftDown;
819:             if (e.CtrlPressed) modifiers |= CefEventFlags.ControlDown;
820:             if (e.AltPressed) modifiers |= CefEventFlags.AltDown;
821:             if (e.MetaPressed) modifiers |= CefEventFlags.AltGrDown;
822:             return modifiers;
823:         }

827:         /// </summary>
828:         private CefMouseButtonType ConvertMouseButton(MouseButton button)
829:         {
830:             return button switch
831:             {
832:                 MouseButton.Left => CefMouseButtonType.Left,
833:                 MouseButton.Right => CefMouseButtonType.Right,
834:                 MouseButton.Middle => CefMouseButtonType.Middle,
835:                 _ => (CefMouseButtonType)(-1)
836:             };
837:         }

839:         /// <summary>
840:         /// Handles notifications from Godot such as resize, mouse exit, and focus changes.
841:         /// Note: Resize handling is done in _Process for smoother resizing experience.
842:         /// </summary>
843:         public override void _Notification(int what)
844:         {
845:             switch ((long)what)
846:             {
847:                 case NotificationResized:
848:                     break;
849: 
850:                 case NotificationMouseExit:
851:                     if (_browserHost != null)
852:                     {
853:                         var mouseEvent = new CefMouseEvent { X = 0, Y = 0, Modifiers = CefEventFlags.None };
854:                         _browserHost.SendMouseMoveEvent(mouseEvent, true);
855:                         _isMousePressed = false;
856:                         _pressedButton = (CefMouseButtonType)(-1);
857:                     }
858:                     break;
859: 
860:                 case NotificationFocusEnter:
861:                     _isFocused = true;
862:                     _browserHost?.SetFocus(true);
863:                     ActivateIme();
864:                     break;
865: 
866:                 case NotificationFocusExit:
867:                     _isFocused = false;
868:                     _browserHost?.SetFocus(false);
869:                     DeactivateIme();
870:                     break;
871:             }
872:         }

100:             var basePath = AppContext.BaseDirectory;

121:

124:             {
125:                 searchPaths.Add(Path.Combine(assemblyDir, "CefGlueBrowserProcess", browserProcessFileName));
126:                 searchPaths.Add(Path.Combine(assemblyDir, browserProcessFileName));
127:             }

160:                     searchPaths.Add(Path.Combine(basePath, "..", "runtimes", "linux-x64", "native"));
161:                     break;

165:                     searchPaths.Add(Path.Combine(basePath, "Resources"));

181:             return basePath;

## Plugin Demo Analysis (plugin/ - C# NormalProject)

### Architecture
The plugin demo is a **standard Godot C# project** (using `Godot.NET.Sdk`), NOT an editor addon plugin. There is **no `plugin.cfg`** file — the `CefGlueControl` exposes itself via the `[GlobalClass]` attribute, making it available in Godot's node creation menu.

### UI Layout (MainUI.tscn → MainUi.cs)

```
MainUI (Control, fullscreen)
├── Browser (Control, CefGlueControl script, FrameRate=120, Transparent=true)
│   └── anchored: top=40px, bottom=-24px (leaving room for toolbar & status bar)
├── Toolbar (Panel, 40px tall, full width)
│   ├── BackButton    "◀"
│   ├── ForwardButton "▶"
│   ├── ReloadButton  "↻"
│   ├── UrlInput      (LineEdit, placeholder="Enter URL...")
│   └── GoButton      "Go"
├── StatusBar (Panel, 24px tall, anchored to bottom)
│   └── StatusLabel   ("Initializing...")
└── Node3D (3D environment for visual polish)
    ├── WorldEnvironment (ProceduralSky + glow)
    ├── DirectionalLight3D (with shadows)
    └── Camera3D
```

### MainUi.cs Feature Set

| Feature | Implementation |
|---------|---------------|
| **Initial URL** | `_browser.InitialUrl = "https://www.bing.com"` (line 25) |
| **URL navigation** | NavigateToUrl() auto-prepends `https://` if missing scheme (lines 101-116) |
| **Browser events** | 5 event handlers: BrowserInitialized → "Ready", AddressChanged → updates URL bar, LoadStart → "Loading...", LoadEnd → "Done", LoadError → error text |
| **Navigation buttons** | GoBack(), GoForward(), Reload() — simple passthrough to CefGlueControl methods |
| **URL input** | TextSubmitted (Enter key) + Go button both trigger NavigateToUrl() |
| **Status updates** | Uses CallDeferred for thread-safe UI updates from CEF callbacks |

### URL Initialization Pattern (Full Codebase)

| Location | File | Value |
|----------|------|-------|
| C# default (plugin) | `plugin/addons/GCefGlue/CefGlueControl.cs:56` | `"about:blank"` |
| C# demo override | `plugin/MainUi.cs:25` | `"https://www.bing.com"` |
| C# default (extension) | `extension/CefGlueControl.cs:44` | `"about:blank"` |
| GDScript demo | `test/GDExtensionGame/Main.gd:5` | `"https://www.bing.com"` |
| GDExtension scene | `test/GDExtensionGame/Main.tscn:31` | `"https://test.playready.microsoft.com/..."` |
| README example | `README.md:29` | `"https://godotengine.org"` |
| CefBrowserHost.Create | both CefGlueControl.cs files | uses `InitialUrl` property as 4th arg |
| ExecuteJavaScript default | both CefGlueControl.cs files | `url ?? "about:blank"` as 2nd arg |

### Key Difference: Plugin vs GDExtension Demos

| Aspect | plugin/ (C# demo) | test/GDExtensionGame/ (GDScript demo) |
|--------|-------------------|---------------------------------------|
| Language | C# (MainUi.cs, 117 lines) | GDScript (Main.gd, 5 lines) |
| UI | Full toolbar + status bar | Just a fullscreen CefGlueControl |
| Features | URL bar, nav buttons, status tracking | Only sets InitialUrl |
| CefGlueControl location | `addons/GCefGlue/CefGlueControl.cs` | Compiled from `extension/` |
| Registration | `[GlobalClass]` attribute | `BindMembers()` via Godot.Bridge |
</answer>

[Project README: E:\Work\Hub\GDCefGlue\README.md]
# GDCefGlue

A CEF (Chromium Embedded Framework) browser control for Godot 4.x using CefGlue.

[中文文档](README_CN.md)

## Features

- GPU hardware acceleration support
- IME support for Chinese/Japanese/Korean input
- Popup handling
- Complete keyboard and mouse support
- Easy integration with Godot 4.x

## Performance Demo

### WebGL Aquarium

Supports WebGL rendering with 20,000 fish at stable 120fps:

![WebGL Aquarium](img/WebGL水族馆.png)

## Quick Start

Add a `CefGlueControl` node to your scene as a browser control:

```csharp
var browser = new CefGlueControl();
browser.InitialUrl = "https://godotengine.org";
AddChild(browser);
```

## Requirements

- **Godot Engine**: 4.6.0 or later (with .NET/Mono support)
- **.NET SDK**: 8.0 or later
- **Windows**: x64 architecture

## Dependency Options

### Option 1: NuGet Package (Recommended)

The easiest way to use GDCefGlue. All necessary files are automatically copied during build.

**CEF 134 (Recommended):**
```xml
<ItemGroup>
  <PackageReference Include="CefGlue.Common" Version="134.6998.178" />
  <PackageReference Include="chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64" Version="134.3.9" />
</ItemGroup>
```

**CEF 120 (Official):**
```xml
<ItemGroup>
  <PackageReference Include="CefGlue.Common" Version="120.6099.0" />
  <PackageReference Include="chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64" Version="120.2.3" />
</ItemGroup>
```

### Option 2: Build from Source

If you need the latest version or want to customize CefGlue:

**CEF 134 (Recommended):**
```bash
git clone https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue.git
```

**CEF 120 (Official):**
```bash
git clone https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue.git
```

Place the cloned repository in your project directory:
```
YourProject/
├── GDCefGlue/
│   ├── extension/              # C# GDExtension source code
│   ├── plugin/                 # Normal C# plugin example
│   ├── test/
│   │   └── GDExtensionGame/    # Godot test project for GDExtension
│   ├── img/
│   └── README*.md
└── CefGlue/                    # Cloned repository
```

## Project Types

### GDExtension Project (Experimental)

Located in `extension/` directory. For Godot 4.6+.

> **Warning:** This project is currently unstable and not recommended for production use.

**Features:**
- Uses Godot's GDExtension system
- Compiled as native AOT library
- Better performance and smaller file size
- Requires manual CEF file copying during export
- Entry point: `gdcefglue_library_init`

**Structure:**
- `extension/` - C# source code for GDExtension
- `extension/Dll/` - Godot .NET bindings (from [godot-dotnet](https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet))
- `test/GDExtensionGame/` - Godot project for testing

**Build Instructions:**

1. **Get Godot .NET Bindings:**
   ```bash
   git clone https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet.git
   cd godot-dotnet
   dotnet build -p:GenerateGodotBindings=true
   ```
   Copy the generated `Godot.Bindings.dll` and related files to `extension/Dll/`.

2. **Build GDExtension:**
   ```bash
   cd extension
   dotnet publish -c Debug -r win-x64 --self-contained true
   ```

3. **Deploy:**
   Copy all files from `Debug/net10.0/win-x64/publish/` to `test/GDExtensionGame/lib/`.

4. **Run:**
   Open the project with Godot 4.6 and run.

### Normal C# Project

Located in `plugin/` directory. Traditional Godot C# project approach.

**Features:**
- Standard Godot .NET SDK project
- Uses `Godot.NET.Sdk`
- CEF files are automatically copied when using NuGet packages
- May need manual resource copying after export (except NuGet packages)
- Source code in `addons/GCefGlue/`

**When to use:**
- If you prefer traditional Godot C# development
- If GDExtension doesn't meet your needs

Update your `.csproj` to reference the projects:
```xml
<ItemGroup>
  <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue\CefGlue.csproj" />
  <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue.Common\CefGlue.Common.csproj" />
  <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue.Common.Shared\CefGlue.Common.Shared.csproj" />
</ItemGroup>
```

### CefGlue Sources

| Source | CEF Version | Status | NuGet | GitHub |
|--------|-------------|--------|-------|--------|
| youfch/CefGlue | 134 | Unofficial (Recommended) | `CefGlue.Common 134.6998.178` | [GitHub](https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue) |
| OutSystems/CefGlue | 120 | Official | `CefGlue.Common 120.6099.0` | [GitHub](https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue) |

## Build Instructions

### Using NuGet Package

```powershell
# Restore packages
dotnet restore

# Build
dotnet build
```

### Using Source Code

```powershell
# Clone CefGlue (choose one)
git clone https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue.git   # CEF 134
# or
git clone https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue.git  # CEF 120

# Build
dotnet restore
dotnet build
```

## Build Output

After a successful build, the following files will be generated:

**Core Files:**
- `GDCefGlue.dll` - Main plugin assembly
- `Xilium.CefGlue.dll` - CefGlue wrapper
- `Xilium.CefGlue.Common.dll` - Common functionality

**CEF Native Files:**
- `libcef.dll` - Chromium core library
- `chrome_*.pak` - UI resources
- `resources.pak` - Application resources
- `locales\*.pak` - Language packs

**BrowserProcess Files:**
- `CefGlueBrowserProcess\` - Browser subprocess files

## Export for Distribution

### Using NuGet Package

When using NuGet packages, all necessary files are automatically copied during build. Just export your Godot project normally.

### Using Source Code

When using source code dependencies, you need to manually copy files after export. See [CEF_EXPORT_GUIDE.md](CEF_EXPORT_GUIDE.md) for details.

## CefGlueControl Properties

| Property | Type | Default | Description |
|----------|------|---------|-------------|
| `InitialUrl` | string | "about:blank" | The URL to load when the browser is created |
| `OpenPopupInCurrentBrowser` | bool | true | If true, popup windows navigate in the current browser |
| `GpuAcceleration` | bool | true | If true, enables GPU hardware acceleration |
| `FrameRate` | int | 60 | Browser frame rate, range 1-360 |
| `Transparent` | bool | false | If true, enables transparent background support |

### Static Properties

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `UseGpuAcceleration` | bool | Global GPU acceleration setting, must be set before CEF initialization |
| `UseTransparent` | bool | Global transparent background setting, must be set before CEF initialization |

### Read-only Properties

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `Address` | string | Current page URL |
| `IsBrowserInitialized` | bool | Whether the browser is initialized |
| `IsLoading` | bool | Whether the page is loading |
| `Title` | string | Current page title |

## CefGlueControl Methods

| Method | Description |
|--------|-------------|
| `GoBack()` | Navigate back in history |
| `GoForward()` | Navigate forward in history |
| `NavigateToUrl(string url)` | Navigate to the specified URL |
| `Reload(bool ignoreCache = false)` | Reload the current page, optionally ignoring cache |
| `ExecuteJavaScript(string code, ...)` | Execute JavaScript code |
| `EvaluateJavaScript<T>(string code, ...)` | Execute JavaScript and return result |
| `ShowDeveloperTools()` | Open developer tools |
| `CloseDeveloperTools()` | Close developer tools |

## GPU Configuration

```csharp
// Enable GPU acceleration (default) - set in inspector
// GpuAcceleration = true;

// Disable GPU acceleration (software rendering) - set in inspector
// GpuAcceleration = false;
```

**Note:** This property is exposed to the Godot inspector. Set it before the control is initialized.

## Troubleshooting

### GPU Process Crashed

1. Ensure all CEF files are copied correctly
2. Try disabling GPU acceleration in the inspector

### Missing DLLs

1. Run `dotnet restore` to restore NuGet packages
2. Clean and rebuild the solution

### Blank Page

1. Check if `locales` directory exists
2. Ensure `resources.pak` is present

## Known Issues

1. **Right-click Context Menu**: Not supported
2. **Network Notification**: `WSALookupServiceBegin failed with: 10108` is a normal warning

## License

GDCefGlue is licensed under the MIT License. See [LICENSE](LICENSE) for details.

Third-party dependencies:
- [CefGlue](https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue) - BSD-3-Clause
- [CEF](https://bitbucket.org/chromiumembedded/cef) - BSD-3-Clause
- [Godot Engine](https://godotengine.org) - MIT
- [godot-dotnet](https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet) - MIT

Now I have a complete picture. Let me summarize:

1. **Main.gd** (GDScript for GDExtension demo) - explicitly sets `browser.InitialUrl = "https://www.bing.com"` in `_ready()`
2. **Main.tscn** (scene file) - sets `InitialUrl = "https://test.playready.microsoft.com/core/rightsmanager.asmx?cfg="` in the node definition
3. **plugin/MainUi.cs** (C# plugin demo) - also sets `_browser.InitialUrl = "https://www.bing.com"` in `_Ready()`
4. Both CefGlueControl.cs files (extension and plugin) have `InitialUrl` defaulting to `"about:blank"` as the C# property default

The flow for the GDExtension demo:
- Main.tscn loads → CefGlueControl node created with InitialUrl = "https://test.playready.microsoft.com/..."
- Main.gd _ready() runs → overrides InitialUrl to "https://www.bing.com"
- The browser then navigates to Bing

So the user's suspicion is correct - the GDScript explicitly sets Bing as the initial URL. The scene file had a Microsoft test URL, but the GDScript overrides it.
